local function px(n)
local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
local vp=cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
local s=math.clamp(math.min(vp.X,vp.Y)/500,0.75,1.35)
return math.floor(n*s+0.5)
end
local function ensureGui()
if gui and gui.Parent and container and container.Parent then return true end
local lp=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local pg=lp and(lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui",5))
if not pg then return false end
gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name="YQXEarlyNotify"
gui.ResetOnSpawn=false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
gui.DisplayOrder=1000
gui.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

container.Name="NotifyContainer"
container.Parent=gui
container.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,1)
container.Position=UDim2.new(1,-14,1,-14)
container.Size=UDim2.new(0,px(235),1,-28)
container.BackgroundTransparency=1

lay.VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
lay.HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
lay.Padding=UDim.new(0,8)
return true
end

local dismissed=false
local function dismiss()
if dismissed or not card.Parent then return end
dismissed=true
local t1=TS:Create(card,TweenInfo.new(0.2),{BackgroundTransparency=1,Position=UDim2.new(0,px(24),0,0)})
TS:Create(label,TweenInfo.new(0.15),{TextTransparency=1}):Play()
TS:Create(stroke,TweenInfo.new(0.15),{Transparency=1}):Play()
TS:Create(badge,TweenInfo.new(0.15),{BackgroundTransparency=1}):Play()
TS:Create(badgeIc,TweenInfo.new(0.15),{TextTransparency=1}):Play()
t1:Play()
t1.Completed:Wait()
if card.Parent then card:Destroy()end
if container and container.Parent then
local left=0
for _,c in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do
if c:IsA("Frame")then left=left+1 end
end
if left==0 and gui then gui:Destroy()gui=nil container=nil end
end
end
local btn=Instance.new("TextButton")
btn.Parent=card
btn.BackgroundTransparency=1
btn.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
btn.Text=""
btn.ZIndex=205
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()task.spawn(dismiss)end)
task.delay(duration,function()task.spawn(dismiss)end)
end)
end

do
local CFG={

local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local HttpService=game:GetService("HttpService")
local StarterGui=game:GetService("StarterGui")
local LP=Players.LocalPlayer
local req=(syn and syn.request)or(http and http.request)or http_request or request
local function hwid()
local ok,v=pcall(function()
if gethwid then return gethwid()end
return game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService"):GetClientId()
end)
return ok and tostring(v)or""
end
local function execName()
local ok,n,v=pcall(function()
if identifyexecutor then return identifyexecutor()end
end)
if ok and n then return tostring(n)..(v and(" "..tostring(v))or"")end
return"unknown"
end
local function deviceType()
local ok,UIS=pcall(function()return game:GetService("UserInputService")end)
if not ok then return"pc"end
if UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled then return"mobile"end
if UIS.GamepadEnabled and not UIS.KeyboardEnabled then return"console"end
return"pc"
end
local function gameName()
local ok,info=pcall(function()
return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
end)
return(ok and info and info.Name)or""
end
local function notifyFallback(text)
notify(text,"warn",6)
end
local function post(path,body)
if not req then return nil,0,"当前执行器不支持 HTTP 请求"end
local headers={["Content-Type"]="application/json"}
if CFG.KEY~=""then headers["X-K"]=CFG.KEY end
local ok,res=pcall(req,{Url=CFG.HOST..path,Method="POST",Headers=headers,Body=HttpService:JSONEncode(body)})
if not ok or not res then return nil,0,"请求失败："..tostring(res)end
local data
pcall(function()data=HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)end)
if not data then return nil,res.StatusCode,"状态码 "..tostring(res.StatusCode)end
return data,res.StatusCode
end
local YT={
global={},mine={},flags={},handlers={},watch={},last={},
whitelisted=false,token=nil,poll=35,bc="",stopped=false,cfg=CFG,
}
genv.__YQX_CLIENT=YT
YQX=YT
local function applyFlags()
local f=YT.flags
for name,fn in pairs(YT.watch)do
if f[name]~=YT.last[name]then
YT.last[name]=f[name]
task.spawn(fn,f[name])
end
end
end
local function rebuild()
local m={}
for k,v in pairs(YT.global)do m[k]=v end
for k,v in pairs(YT.mine)do m[k]=v end
YT.flags=m
applyFlags()
end
function YT.flag(name,default)
local v=YT.flags[name]
if v==nil then return default end
return v
end
function YT.onFlag(name,fn)
YT.watch[name]=fn
task.spawn(fn,YT.flags[name])
YT.last[name]=YT.flags[name]
end
local function applyCfg(c)
if not c then return end
if c.flags then YT.global=c.flags end
if c.poll then YT.poll=c.poll end
if c.bc~=nil then YT.bc=c.bc end
rebuild()
if c.announce and c.announce~=""and c.announce~=YT.lastAnnounce then
YT.lastAnnounce=c.announce
YT.pendingAnnounce=c.announce
end
end
local hello
local function call(path,body)
body=body or{}
body.t=YT.token
body.version=CFG.VERSION
body.placeId=tostring(game.PlaceId)
local d,code=post(path,body)
if code==401 and hello()then
body.t=YT.token
d,code=post(path,body)
end
return d,code
end
function hello()
local d,code,errMsg=post("/s/hello",{
userId=tostring(LP.UserId),user=LP.Name,dn=LP.DisplayName,ag=LP.AccountAge,
hwid=hwid(),placeId=tostring(game.PlaceId),gameId=game.JobId,gn=gameName(),
version=CFG.VERSION,ex=execName(),dv=deviceType(),ts=os.time(),
})
if not d then
YT.err=errMsg or"unknown"
return false,"network"
end
if not d.ok then
YT.lastFail=d
if d.banned then return false,"banned"end
if d.wl==false then return false,"nowl"end
return false,"deny"
end
YT.token,YT.whitelisted=d.token,d.wl
YT.mine=(d.cfg and d.cfg.pf)or{}
applyCfg(d.cfg)
return true
end
function YT.report(action,data)
task.spawn(function()
call("/s/event",{action=action,data=data,gameId=game.JobId})
end)
end
function YT.on(cmd,fn)YT.handlers[cmd]=fn end
function YT.loadPayload()
if not YT.whitelisted then return false,"not whitelisted"end
local d=call("/s/payload",{})
if d and d.ok and d.src~=""then
local fn,e=loadstring(d.src)
if not fn then return false,e end
return pcall(fn,YT)
end
return false,"empty"
end
function YT.disconnect()YT.stopped=true genv.__YQX_CLIENT=nil end
YT._call,YT._post=call,post
YT._hwid,YT._ex,YT._dv=hwid(),execName(),deviceType()
warn("[YQX] authorization request starting; http="..tostring(req~=nil))
local ok,reason=hello()
if not ok then
warn("[YQX] authorization failed: reason="..tostring(reason)..", detail="..tostring(YT.err or(YT.lastFail and YT.lastFail.msg)or""))
if reason=="banned"then
LP:Kick("\n你已被封禁\n"..tostring((YT.lastFail and YT.lastFail.msg)or"banned"))
elseif reason=="nowl"then
LP:Kick("\n未获得白名单，无法使用该脚本\n"..tostring((YT.lastFail and YT.lastFail.msg)or""))
elseif reason=="deny"then
LP:Kick("\n"..tostring((YT.lastFail and YT.lastFail.msg)or"验证失败，请稍后重试"))
else
notifyFallback("无法连接到服务器："..tostring(YT.err or"未知原因"))
end
genv.__YQX_CLIENT=nil
return
end
warn("[YQX] authorization passed; continuing script")
notify("白名单检查通过，正在加载功能...","success",5)
end
end
local function reportError(fnName,err,extra)
print("[YQX][ERROR] "..tostring(fnName or"unknown")..": "..tostring(err))
pcall(function()
notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error",4)
end)
pcall(function()
if YQX and YQX.report then
YQX.report("script_error",{
fn=tostring(fnName or"unknown"),
err=tostring(err):sub(1,800),
extra=extra,
placeId=tostring(game.PlaceId),
ts=os.time(),
})
end
end)
end
local function safeCall(fnName,fn,...)
local args={...}
local ok,a,b,c,d=pcall(function()
return fn(table.unpack(args))
end)
if not ok then
reportError(fnName,a)
return false,a
end
return true,a,b,c,d
end

local currentClickSoundId=ASSETS.sound.default
local function tabImage(assetId)
return("rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=%s&w=150&h=150"):format(tostring(assetId))
end
local function playClick()
if not clickSoundEnabled then return end
pcall(function()
local targetId=tostring(currentClickSoundId or(config and config.clickSoundId)or ASSETS.sound.default)
if targetId==""then targetId=ASSETS.sound.default end
local want="rbxassetid://"..targetId
if clickSound and clickSound.Parent and clickSound.SoundId~=want then
clickSound:Destroy()
clickSound=nil
end
if not clickSound or not clickSound.Parent then
clickSound=Instance.new("Sound")
clickSound.Volume=1
clickSound.Name="ClickAudio"
clickSound.SoundId=want
clickSound.Parent=services.SoundService
end
clickSound:Stop()
clickSound.TimePosition=0
clickSound:Play()
end)
end

}

pcall(function()
writefile(CONFIG_FILE,HttpService:JSONEncode(config))
end)
end
local function loadConfig()
pcall(function()
if isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE)then
local data=readfile(CONFIG_FILE)
local parsed=HttpService:JSONDecode(data)
if type(parsed)=="table"then
for k,v in pairs(parsed)do
config[k]=v
end
end
end
end)
end

local PAT={
{"^已取消选中: (.*)$","Deselected: %1"},{"^已选中: (.*)$","Selected: %1"},
{"^已添加 (%d+) 个同名物品$","Added %1 same-name items"},{"^传送完成，成功 (%d+) / (%d+) 个物品$","Teleport complete: %1 / %2 items"},
{"^已选中 (%d+) 个同名物品$","Selected %1 same-name items"},{"^已取消本组: (%d+) 个$","Deselected group: %1"},
{"^保存失败: (.*)$","Save failed: %1"},{"^配置已删除，已恢复默认值 %(次数=(.-), 间隔=(.-)%)$","Config deleted, defaults restored (attempts=%1, interval=%2)"},
{"^删除失败: (.*)$","Delete failed: %1"},{"^没有可卖的(.*)$","Nothing to sell: %1"},{"^开始卖(.-) 共(%d+)个$","Selling %1, %2 total"},
{"^卖出(.-) (%d+)/(%d+)$","Sold %1 %2/%3"},{"^正在把(%d+)根木头拉过来$","Pulling %1 logs to you"},
}
function YQXtr(s)
if LANG~="en"then return s end
s=tostring(s)
if EN[s]then return EN[s]end
for _,p in ipairs(PAT)do
if s:match(p[1])then
return(s:gsub(p[1],p[2]):gsub("木头","wood"):gsub("木板","planks"))
end
end
return s
end
end
function YQXnotifyTr(desc,dur)
desc=tostring(desc or"")
if YQXSellQuiet and desc:find("^没有可卖的")then return end
pcall(function()notify(YQXtr(desc),"info",tonumber(dur)or 3)end)
end
function YQXsectionProxy(sec)
return setmetatable({},{__index=function(_,k)
return function(self,text,...)
if k=="Label"then
local lbl=sec:Label(YQXtr(text))
return setmetatable({SetText=function(_,s)lbl.Text=YQXtr(s)end},{__index=lbl,__newindex=function(_,kk,v)lbl[kk]=v end})
end
return sec[k](sec,YQXtr(text),...)
end
end})
end
local STORE_TRANSLATIONS={}
pcall(function()
local raw=game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/jjyy1234/XJ/main/%E7%BF%BB%E8%AF%91%E5%95%86%E5%BA%97.lua")
local chunk=loadstring(raw)
local data=chunk and chunk()
if type(data)=="table"then STORE_TRANSLATIONS=data end
end)
local TREE_TRANSLATIONS={}
pcall(function()
local raw=game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/jjyy1234/XJ/refs/heads/main/TreeClass_%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87%E7%BF%BB%E8%AF%91.lua")
local chunk=loadstring(raw)
local data=chunk and chunk()
if type(data)=="table"then TREE_TRANSLATIONS=data end
end)
local function treeDisplayName(name)
return TREE_TRANSLATIONS[name]or tostring(name)
end

YQX_THEME_ZH={["Default (Dark Blue)"]="深蓝默认",["Minimal White"]="极简白",["Light Blue"]="浅蓝",["Mint Green"]="浅薄荷绿",["Lavender Purple"]="薰衣草紫",["Peach Pink"]="桃子粉",["Custom Image"]="自定义图片"}
YQX_THEME_ORDER={"Default (Dark Blue)","Minimal White","Light Blue","Mint Green","Lavender Purple","Peach Pink","Custom Image"}
function YQXThemeDisplay(key)
local zh=YQX_THEME_ZH[key]
if zh then return key.."（"..zh.."）"end
return key
end
function YQXThemeKey(display)
for _,k in ipairs(YQX_THEME_ORDER)do
if display==YQXThemeDisplay(k)then return k end
end
return display
end
function Tween(obj,t,data)
services.TweenService:Create(obj,TweenInfo.new(t[1],Enum.EasingStyle[t[2]],Enum.EasingDirection[t[3]]),data):Play()
return true
end
function Ripple(obj)
spawn(function()
if obj.ClipsDescendants~=true then obj.ClipsDescendants=true end
local Ripple=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
Ripple.Name="Ripple"
Ripple.Parent=obj
Ripple.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
Ripple.BackgroundTransparency=1.000
Ripple.ZIndex=8
Ripple.Image="rbxassetid://"..ASSETS.ui.ripple
Ripple.ImageTransparency=0.800
Ripple.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit
Ripple.ImageColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
Ripple.Position=UDim2.new((mouse.X-Ripple.AbsolutePosition.X)/obj.AbsoluteSize.X,0,(mouse.Y-Ripple.AbsolutePosition.Y)/obj.AbsoluteSize.Y,0)
Tween(Ripple,{.3,"Linear","InOut"},{Position=UDim2.new(-5.5,0,-5.5,0),Size=UDim2.new(12,0,12,0)})
wait(0.15)
Tween(Ripple,{.3,"Linear","InOut"},{ImageTransparency=1})
wait(.3)
Ripple:Destroy()
end)
end
do
local toggled=false
local switchingTabs=false
local function tweenTabIcon(tabIco,transparency)
services.TweenService:Create(tabIco,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{ImageTransparency=transparency}):Play()
end
function switchTab(new)
if switchingTabs then return end
local old=library.currentTab
if old==nil then
new[2].Visible=true
library.currentTab=new
tweenTabIcon(new[1],0)
services.TweenService:Create(new[1].TabText,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{TextTransparency=0}):Play()
return
end
if old[1]==new[1]then return end
switchingTabs=true
library.currentTab=new
tweenTabIcon(old[1],0.2)
tweenTabIcon(new[1],0)
services.TweenService:Create(old[1].TabText,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{TextTransparency=0.2}):Play()
services.TweenService:Create(new[1].TabText,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{TextTransparency=0}):Play()
old[2].Visible=false
new[2].Visible=true
task.wait(0.1)
switchingTabs=false
end
end
function drag(frame,hold)
if not hold then hold=frame end
local dragging,dragInput,dragStart,startPos
local function update(input)
local delta=input.Position-dragStart
frame.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+delta.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+delta.Y)
end
hold.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
dragging=true
dragStart=input.Position
startPos=frame.Position
input.Changed:Connect(function()
if input.UserInputState==Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging=false end
end)
end
end)
frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then dragInput=input end
end)
services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if input==dragInput and dragging then update(input)end
end)
end

library.screenGui=dogent

library.currentTheme="Default (Dark Blue)"
function library.applyTheme(themeName)
if themeName=="Pink Gradient"then themeName="Peach Pink"end
local t=THEMES[themeName]
if not t then return end
local old=library.currentColors
local Main=library.mainFrame
if not Main then return end
local bgImage=Main:FindFirstChild("BgImage")
if t.useImage then
if not bgImage then
bgImage=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
bgImage.Name="BgImage"
bgImage.Parent=Main
bgImage.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
bgImage.BackgroundTransparency=1
bgImage.ZIndex=0
end
local selectedAsset=(config.bgAssetId and config.bgAssetId~=""and config.bgAssetId)or t.imageAssetId or ASSETS.background.default
if selectedAsset and selectedAsset~=""then
bgImage.Image="rbxassetid://"..tostring(selectedAsset)
end
local bgt=t.bgTransparency or 0
Main.BackgroundTransparency=bgt
else
if bgImage then bgImage:Destroy()end
Main.BackgroundTransparency=config.bgTransparency or 0
end
do
local sb=Main:FindFirstChild("SB")
local side=sb and sb:FindFirstChild("Side")
local isDefault=(themeName=="Default (Dark Blue)")
local sideT=isDefault and 0 or 1
if sb then
sb.BackgroundColor3=t.zy
sb.BackgroundTransparency=sideT
local sbg=sb:FindFirstChild("SBG")
if sbg and sbg:IsA("UIGradient")then sbg.Color=ColorSequence.new(t.zy,t.zy)end
end
if side then
side.BackgroundColor3=t.zy
side.BackgroundTransparency=sideT
local sideg=side:FindFirstChild("SideG")
if sideg and sideg:IsA("UIGradient")then sideg.Color=ColorSequence.new(t.zy,t.zy)end
end
end
Main.BackgroundColor3=t.bg
local themeGrad=Main:FindFirstChild("ThemeGradient")
if t.useGradient then
if not themeGrad then
themeGrad=Instance.new("UIGradient")
themeGrad.Name="ThemeGradient"
themeGrad.Parent=Main
end
themeGrad.Color=ColorSequence.new(t.gradientStart,t.gradientEnd)
themeGrad.Rotation=90
else
if themeGrad then themeGrad:Destroy()end
end
local gui=library.screenGui
if gui then
for _,v in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants())do
if v:IsA("GuiObject")then
if v.Name~="Open"then
if v.BackgroundColor3==old.zy then
v.BackgroundColor3=t.zy
elseif v.BackgroundColor3==old.main then
v.BackgroundColor3=t.bg
end
end
if v:IsA("TextButton")and v.Name~="Open"and v.Name~="TabBtn"and v.Name~="MinSlider"and v.Name~="AddSlider"and v.Name~="DropdownOpen"then
v.BackgroundTransparency=t.buttonTransparency or 0
end
if v.Name=="DropdownTop"or v.Name:match("^Option_")then
v.BackgroundColor3=t.dropdownColor or t.zy
v.BackgroundTransparency=t.dropdownTransparency or t.buttonTransparency or 0
elseif v.Name=="ToggleSwitch"then
v.BackgroundColor3=t.switchOff or t.controlFill or t.text
elseif v.Name=="ToggleDisable"or v.Name=="SliderBar"or v.Name=="SliderValBG"then
v.BackgroundColor3=t.controlTrack or t.bg
elseif v.Name=="SliderPart"then
v.BackgroundColor3=t.controlFill or t.text
elseif v.Name=="DropdownOpen"then
v.BackgroundTransparency=1
v.TextColor3=t.text
elseif v.Name=="DropdownText"then
v.TextColor3=t.text
v.PlaceholderColor3=t.text
end
if(v:IsA("TextLabel")or v:IsA("TextButton")or v:IsA("TextBox"))and v.Name~="ScriptTitle"and v.Name~="Open"then
v.TextColor3=t.text
end
end
end
end
MainColor=t.bg
Background=t.bg
zyColor=t.zy
beijingColor=t.switchOff or t.controlFill or t.text
switchOnColor=t.switchOn or t.controlFill or t.text
controlTrackColor=t.controlTrack or t.bg
controlFillColor=t.controlFill or t.text
library.currentColors={main=t.bg,bg=t.bg,zy=t.zy,text=t.text}
library.currentTheme=themeName
config.theme=themeName
end

coroutine.wrap(function()
local gradient=UIGradientTitle
local ts=game:GetService("TweenService")
local ti=TweenInfo.new(1,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear,Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local offset={Offset=Vector2.new(1,0)}
local create=ts:Create(gradient,ti,offset)
local startingPos=Vector2.new(-1,0)
local list={}
local s,kpt=ColorSequence.new,ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
local counter=0
local status="down"
gradient.Offset=startingPos
local function rainbowColors()
local sat,val=255,255
for i=1,10 do
local hue=i*17
table.insert(list,Color3.fromHSV(hue/255,sat/255,val/255))
end
end
rainbowColors()
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,list[#list]),kpt(0.5,list[#list-1]),kpt(1,list[#list-2])})
counter=#list
local function animate()
create:Play()
create.Completed:Wait()
gradient.Offset=startingPos
gradient.Rotation=180
if counter==#list-1 and status=="down"then
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,gradient.Color.Keypoints[1].Value),kpt(0.5,list[#list]),kpt(1,list[1])})
counter=1;status="up"
elseif counter==#list and status=="down"then
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,gradient.Color.Keypoints[1].Value),kpt(0.5,list[1]),kpt(1,list[2])})
counter=2;status="up"
elseif counter<=#list-2 and status=="down"then
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,gradient.Color.Keypoints[1].Value),kpt(0.5,list[counter+1]),kpt(1,list[counter+2])})
counter=counter+2;status="up"
end
create:Play()
create.Completed:Wait()
gradient.Offset=startingPos
gradient.Rotation=0
if counter==#list-1 and status=="up"then
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,list[1]),kpt(0.5,list[#list]),kpt(1,gradient.Color.Keypoints[3].Value)})
counter=1;status="down"
elseif counter==#list and status=="up"then
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,list[2]),kpt(0.5,list[1]),kpt(1,gradient.Color.Keypoints[3].Value)})
counter=2;status="down"
elseif counter<=#list-2 and status=="up"then
gradient.Color=s({kpt(0,list[counter+2]),kpt(0.5,list[counter+1]),kpt(1,gradient.Color.Keypoints[3].Value)})
counter=counter+2;status="down"
end
animate()
end
animate()
end)()

library._persistReg={}
library._persistIcons={[ASSETS.tab.home]=true,[ASSETS.tab.teleport]=true,[ASSETS.tab.other]=true}
function library._persist(section,flag,kind,funcs,callback)
if not section.persist then return end
library._persistReg[flag]={kind=kind,funcs=funcs,cb=callback}
end
function YQXLoadUiFlags()
local saved=config.uiFlags
if type(saved)~="table"then return end
for flag,val in pairs(saved)do
local r=library._persistReg[flag]
if r then
pcall(function()
if r.kind=="toggle"then
if type(val)=="boolean"then r.funcs:SetState(val)end
elseif r.kind=="slider"then
if tonumber(val)then r.funcs:SetValue(tonumber(val))end
elseif r.kind=="textbox"then
if type(val)=="string"and val~=""then r.funcs:SetValue(val)pcall(r.cb,val)end
elseif r.kind=="dropdown"then
if type(val)=="string"then r.funcs:SetValue(val)pcall(r.cb,val)end
end
end)
end
end
end
function YQXStartUiAutosave()
task.spawn(function()
local last=""
while task.wait(3)do
local snap={}
for flag,_ in pairs(library._persistReg)do
local v=library.flags[flag]
if v~=nil then snap[flag]=v end
end
local ok,enc=pcall(function()return HttpService:JSONEncode(snap)end)
if ok and enc~=last then
last=enc
config.uiFlags=snap
saveConfig()
end
end
end)
end

function section.Toggle(section,text,flag,enabled,callback)
local callback=callback or function()end
local enabled=enabled or false
assert(text,"No text provided")
assert(flag,"No flag provided")
library.flags[flag]=enabled
local ToggleModule=Instance.new("Frame")
local ToggleBtn=Instance.new("TextButton")
local ToggleBtnC=Instance.new("UICorner")
local ToggleDisable=Instance.new("Frame")
local ToggleSwitch=Instance.new("Frame")
local ToggleSwitchC=Instance.new("UICorner")
local ToggleDisableC=Instance.new("UICorner")
ToggleModule.Name="ToggleModule"
ToggleModule.Parent=Objs
ToggleModule.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
ToggleModule.BackgroundTransparency=1.000
ToggleModule.BorderSizePixel=0
ToggleModule.Size=UDim2.new(0,428,0,38)
ToggleBtn.Name="ToggleBtn"
ToggleBtn.Parent=ToggleModule
ToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3=zyColor
ToggleBtn.BorderSizePixel=0
ToggleBtn.Size=UDim2.new(0,428,0,38)
ToggleBtn.AutoButtonColor=false
ToggleBtn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
ToggleBtn.Text="   "..text
ToggleBtn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
ToggleBtn.TextSize=16.000
ToggleBtn.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
ToggleBtnC.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
ToggleBtnC.Name="ToggleBtnC"
ToggleBtnC.Parent=ToggleBtn
ToggleDisable.Name="ToggleDisable"
ToggleDisable.Parent=ToggleBtn
ToggleDisable.BackgroundColor3=controlTrackColor
ToggleDisable.BorderSizePixel=0
ToggleDisable.Position=UDim2.new(0.901869178,0,0.208881587,0)
ToggleDisable.Size=UDim2.new(0,36,0,22)
ToggleSwitch.Name="ToggleSwitch"
ToggleSwitch.Parent=ToggleDisable
ToggleSwitch.BackgroundColor3=beijingColor
ToggleSwitch.Size=UDim2.new(0,24,0,22)
ToggleSwitchC.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
ToggleSwitchC.Name="ToggleSwitchC"
ToggleSwitchC.Parent=ToggleSwitch
ToggleDisableC.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
ToggleDisableC.Name="ToggleDisableC"
ToggleDisableC.Parent=ToggleDisable
local funcs={
SetState=function(self,state)
if state==nil then state=not library.flags[flag]end
if library.flags[flag]==state then return end
services.TweenService:Create(ToggleSwitch,TweenInfo.new(0.2),{
Position=UDim2.new(0,(state and ToggleSwitch.Size.X.Offset/2 or 0),0,0),
BackgroundColor3=(state and switchOnColor or beijingColor)
}):Play()
library.flags[flag]=state
local ok,err=pcall(callback,state)
if not ok then
reportError("Toggle:"..tostring(flag),err)
pcall(function()notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error")end)
end
end,
Module=ToggleModule
}
if enabled~=false then funcs:SetState(flag,true)end
ToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()playClick()funcs:SetState()end)
library._persist(section,flag,"toggle",funcs,callback)
return funcs
end
function section.Slider(section,text,flag,default,min,max,precise,callback)

local min=min or 1
local max=max or 10
local default=default or min
local precise=precise or false
library.flags[flag]=default
assert(text,"No text provided")
assert(flag,"No flag provided")
local SliderModule=Instance.new("Frame")

local SliderPartC=Instance.new("UICorner")
local SliderValBG=Instance.new("TextButton")
local SliderValBGC=Instance.new("UICorner")
local SliderValue=Instance.new("TextBox")
local MinSlider=Instance.new("TextButton")
local AddSlider=Instance.new("TextButton")
SliderModule.Name="SliderModule"

SliderPartC.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
SliderPartC.Name="SliderPartC"
SliderPartC.Parent=SliderPart
SliderValBG.Name="SliderValBG"
SliderValBG.Parent=SliderBack
SliderValBG.BackgroundColor3=controlTrackColor
SliderValBG.BorderSizePixel=0
SliderValBG.Position=UDim2.new(0.883177578,0,0.131578952,0)
SliderValBG.Size=UDim2.new(0,44,0,28)
SliderValBG.AutoButtonColor=false
SliderValBG.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
SliderValBG.Text=""
SliderValBG.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
SliderValBG.TextSize=14.000

SliderValue.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
SliderValue.Text=tostring(default)
SliderValue.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
SliderValue.TextSize=14.000
MinSlider.Name="MinSlider"
MinSlider.Parent=SliderModule
MinSlider.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
MinSlider.BackgroundTransparency=1.000
MinSlider.BorderSizePixel=0
MinSlider.Position=UDim2.new(0.296728969,40,0.236842096,0)
MinSlider.Size=UDim2.new(0,20,0,20)
MinSlider.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
MinSlider.Text="-"
MinSlider.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
MinSlider.TextSize=24.000
MinSlider.TextWrapped=true
AddSlider.Name="AddSlider"
AddSlider.Parent=SliderModule
AddSlider.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5)
AddSlider.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
AddSlider.BackgroundTransparency=1.000
AddSlider.BorderSizePixel=0
AddSlider.Position=UDim2.new(0.810906529,0,0.5,0)
AddSlider.Size=UDim2.new(0,20,0,20)
AddSlider.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
AddSlider.Text="+"
AddSlider.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
AddSlider.TextSize=24.000
AddSlider.TextWrapped=true
local funcs={
SetValue=function(self,value)
local percent=(mouse.X-SliderBar.AbsolutePosition.X)/SliderBar.AbsoluteSize.X
if value then percent=(value-min)/(max-min)end
percent=math.clamp(percent,0,1)
if precise then
value=value or tonumber(string.format("%.1f",tostring(min+(max-min)*percent)))
else
value=value or math.floor(min+(max-min)*percent)
end
library.flags[flag]=tonumber(value)
SliderValue.Text=tostring(value)
SliderPart.Size=UDim2.new(percent,0,1,0)
callback(tonumber(value))
end
}
MinSlider.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
local currentValue=library.flags[flag]
currentValue=math.clamp(currentValue-1,min,max)
funcs:SetValue(currentValue)
end)
AddSlider.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
local currentValue=library.flags[flag]
currentValue=math.clamp(currentValue+1,min,max)
funcs:SetValue(currentValue)
end)
funcs:SetValue(default)
local dragging,boxFocused=false,false
SliderBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
funcs:SetValue();dragging=true
end
end)
services.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging=false end
end)
services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then funcs:SetValue()end
end)
SliderValue.Focused:Connect(function()boxFocused=true end)
SliderValue.FocusLost:Connect(function()
boxFocused=false
if SliderValue.Text==""then funcs:SetValue(default)end
end)
SliderValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
if not boxFocused then return end
SliderValue.Text=SliderValue.Text:gsub("%D+","")
local text=SliderValue.Text
if not tonumber(text)then
SliderValue.Text=SliderValue.Text:gsub("%D+","")
elseif tonumber(text)then
if tonumber(text)>max then text=max;SliderValue.Text=tostring(max)end
funcs:SetValue(tonumber(text))
end
end)
library._persist(section,flag,"slider",funcs,callback)
return funcs
end

DropdownText.Position=UDim2.new(0.0373831764,0,0,0)
DropdownText.Size=UDim2.new(0,184,0,38)
DropdownText.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
DropdownText.PlaceholderColor3=library.currentColors.text
DropdownText.PlaceholderText=text
DropdownText.Text=""
DropdownText.TextColor3=library.currentColors.text
DropdownText.TextSize=16.000
DropdownText.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
DropdownText.ZIndex=2
DropdownText.Active=false
DropdownModuleL.Name="DropdownModuleL"
DropdownModuleL.Parent=DropdownModule
DropdownModuleL.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
DropdownModuleL.Padding=UDim.new(0,4)
local setAllVisible=function()
for _,option in pairs(DropdownModule:GetChildren())do
if option:IsA("TextButton")and option.Name:match("Option_")then
option.Visible=true
end
end
end
local searchDropdown=function(text)
for _,option in pairs(DropdownModule:GetChildren())do
if option:IsA("TextButton")and option.Name:match("Option_")then
if text==""then option.Visible=true
elseif option.Text:lower():match(text:lower())then option.Visible=true
else option.Visible=false end
end
end
end
local open=false
local refreshOnOpen=nil
local funcs
local selectedOrder={}
local selectedSet={}
local function updateSummary()
if not multi then return end
DropdownText.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
if#selectedOrder==0 then
DropdownText.PlaceholderText=text
else
DropdownText.PlaceholderText=text..": "..table.concat(selectedOrder,", ")
end
end
local ToggleDropVis=function()
open=not open
DropdownText.Active=open
if open then
if refreshOnOpen then
local ok,opts=pcall(refreshOnOpen)
if ok and type(opts)=="table"and#opts>0 then
pcall(funcs.SetOptions,funcs,opts)
end
end
setAllVisible()
end
DropdownOpen.Text=(open and"-"or"+")
DropdownModule.Size=UDim2.new(0,428,0,(open and DropdownModuleL.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+4 or 38))
end
DropdownOpen.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ToggleDropVis)
DropdownTop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ToggleDropVis)
DropdownText.Focused:Connect(function()
if open then return end
ToggleDropVis()
end)
DropdownText:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
if not open then return end
searchDropdown(DropdownText.Text)
end)
DropdownModuleL:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
if not open then return end
DropdownModule.Size=UDim2.new(0,428,0,(DropdownModuleL.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+4))
end)
funcs={}
funcs.AddOption=function(self,option)
local Option=Instance.new("TextButton")
local OptionC=Instance.new("UICorner")
Option.Name="Option_"..option
Option.Parent=DropdownModule
Option.BackgroundColor3=zyColor
Option.BorderSizePixel=0
Option.Size=UDim2.new(0,428,0,26)
Option.AutoButtonColor=false
Option.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
Option.Text=option
Option.TextColor3=library.currentColors.text
Option.TextSize=14.000
OptionC.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
OptionC.Name="OptionC"
OptionC.Parent=Option
if multi then
local Check=Instance.new("TextLabel")
Check.Name="Check"
Check.Parent=Option
Check.BackgroundTransparency=1
Check.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0.5)
Check.Position=UDim2.new(1,-8,0.5,0)
Check.Size=UDim2.new(0,20,0,20)
Check.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
Check.Text="✓"
Check.TextSize=16
Check.TextColor3=library.currentColors.text
Check.Visible=selectedSet[option]==true
Option.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if selectedSet[option]then
selectedSet[option]=nil
for i,v in ipairs(selectedOrder)do
if v==option then table.remove(selectedOrder,i)break end
end
else
selectedSet[option]=true
table.insert(selectedOrder,option)
end
Check.Visible=selectedSet[option]==true
updateSummary()
local copy={}
for i,v in ipairs(selectedOrder)do copy[i]=v end
library.flags[flag]=copy
callback(copy)
end)
else
Option.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
ToggleDropVis()
callback(Option.Text)
DropdownText.Text=Option.Text
library.flags[flag]=Option.Text
end)
end
end
funcs.RemoveOption=function(self,option)
local opt=DropdownModule:FindFirstChild("Option_"..option)
if opt then opt:Destroy()end
end
funcs.SetOptions=function(self,options)
for _,v in next,DropdownModule:GetChildren()do
if v.Name:match("Option_")then v:Destroy()end
end
for _,v in next,options do funcs:AddOption(v)end
end
funcs.SetValue=function(self,val)
DropdownText.Text=val
library.flags[flag]=val
end
funcs.SetRefresh=function(self,cb)refreshOnOpen=cb end
funcs.GetSelected=function(self)
local copy={}
for i,v in ipairs(selectedOrder)do copy[i]=v end
return copy
end
funcs:SetOptions(options)
if not multi then library._persist(section,flag,"dropdown",funcs,callback)end
return funcs
end
return section
end
tab.page=Tab
tab.Page=Tab
return tab
end
return window
end

local function getSwordFromWorld(name)
local lp2=Players.LocalPlayer
local bp=lp2:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
if bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name)then return bp:FindFirstChild(name)end
local char=lp2.Character
if char and char:FindFirstChild(name)then return char:FindFirstChild(name)end
local pm=workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if pm then
for _,obj in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
if obj.Name==name then
local owner=obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner and owner.Value==lp2 then return obj end
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function getBestSword()
local lp2=Players.LocalPlayer
local containers={lp2:FindFirstChild("Backpack"),lp2.Character}
for _,container in ipairs(containers)do
if container then
for _,obj in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do
local itemName=obj:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
if itemName and itemName:IsA("StringValue")then
return obj,itemName.Value
end
end
end
end
return nil,nil
end
local speaker=Players.LocalPlayer
local currentMoney=nil
pcall(function()
local tx=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Transactions")
local stc=tx and tx:FindFirstChild("ServerToClient")
local fc=stc and stc:FindFirstChild("FundsChanged")
if fc and fc:IsA("RemoteEvent")then
fc.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(a,b)
if type(a)=="number"then
currentMoney=a
elseif type(b)=="number"then
currentMoney=b
elseif type(a)=="table"and type(a.Money)=="number"then
currentMoney=a.Money
end
end)
end
end)
local function getMoney()
if type(currentMoney)=="number"then
return currentMoney
end
local function from(inst)
if not inst then return nil end
for _,n in ipairs({"Money","Cash","Funds","Gold","money","cash"})do
local v=inst:FindFirstChild(n)
if v and v:IsA("ValueBase")and type(v.Value)=="number"then
return v.Value
end
end
for _,v in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants())do
if v:IsA("ValueBase")and(v.Name=="Money"or v.Name=="Cash"or v.Name=="Funds")then
if type(v.Value)=="number"then return v.Value end
end
end
return nil
end
local m=from(speaker:FindFirstChild("leaderstats"))
or from(speaker)
or from(speaker:FindFirstChild("PlayerData"))
or from(speaker:FindFirstChild("Stats"))
if type(m)=="number"then
currentMoney=m
return m
end
return currentMoney
end

local function getUIScale()
local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
local vp=cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
local short=math.min(vp.X,vp.Y)
return math.clamp(short/500,0.75,1.35)
end
local S=getUIScale()

local LP=Players.LocalPlayer
if YQX.pendingAnnounce then
task.defer(function()serverMsg(YQX.pendingAnnounce,"公告")end)
end
YQX.on("kick",function(m)LP:Kick("\n"..tostring(m or"已被移出"))end)
YQX.on("notify",function(m)notify(tostring(m or""),"server",6)end)
YQX.on("msg",function(m)serverMsg(tostring(m or""),"服务器消息")end)
YQX.on("teleport",function(m)
local pid=tonumber(m)
if pid then pcall(function()TeleportService:Teleport(pid,LP)end)end
end)
YQX.on("rejoin",function()
pcall(function()
if#Players:GetPlayers()<=1 then
LP:Kick("\n正在重新加入…")
task.wait()
TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId,LP)
else
TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId,game.JobId,LP)
end
end)
end)
YQX.on("flag",function(m)
local k,v=tostring(m):match("^(%w+)=(.*)$")
if not k then return end
if v=="true"then v=true
elseif v=="false"then v=false
elseif v==""then v=nil
elseif tonumber(v)then v=tonumber(v)end
YQX.mine[k]=v
local merged={}
for kk,vv in pairs(YQX.global)do merged[kk]=vv end
for kk,vv in pairs(YQX.mine)do merged[kk]=vv end
YQX.flags=merged
for name,fn in pairs(YQX.watch)do
if YQX.flags[name]~=YQX.last[name]then
YQX.last[name]=YQX.flags[name]
task.spawn(fn,YQX.flags[name])
end
end
notify("已同步开关 "..tostring(k),"info",2)
end)
YQX.on("ping",function()if YQX._doPoll then task.spawn(YQX._doPoll)end end)
YQX.on("reload",function()
local src
if YQX.whitelisted then
local d=YQX._call("/s/payload",{})
if d and d.ok and d.src and d.src~=""then src=d.src end
end
if not src and YQX.cfg and YQX.cfg.RELOAD~=""then src=YQX.cfg.RELOAD end
if not src then notify("没有可重载的代码","warn",3);return end
local fn,e=loadstring(src)
if not fn then notify("重载失败："..tostring(e),"error",4);return end
notify("正在重载…","info",2)
task.spawn(function()
local ok,e2=pcall(fn,YQX)
if not ok then notify("重载出错："..tostring(e2),"error",5)end
end)
end)
local function runRemoteLua(m)
local src=tostring(m or"")
if src==""then return end
local fn,e=loadstring(src)
if not fn then
notify("远程代码加载失败："..tostring(e),"error",4)
pcall(function()YQX.report("remote_lua_error",{e=tostring(e)})end)
return
end
task.spawn(function()
local ok,e2=pcall(fn,YQX)
if not ok then
notify("远程代码执行出错："..tostring(e2),"error",4)
pcall(function()YQX.report("remote_lua_error",{e=tostring(e2)})end)
else
pcall(function()YQX.report("remote_lua_ok",{})end)
end
end)
end
YQX.on("lua",runRemoteLua)
YQX.on("exec",runRemoteLua)
YQX.onFlag("antiAfk",function(v)
if not v then return end
pcall(function()
LP.Idled:Connect(function()
if YQX.flag("antiAfk",false)then
local vu=game:GetService("VirtualUser")
vu:CaptureController()
vu:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
end
end)
end)
end)
YQX.onFlag("fpsCap",function(v)
if v and setfpscap then pcall(setfpscap,tonumber(v)or 60)end
end)
local errReported=0
pcall(function()
game:GetService("ScriptContext").Error:Connect(function(msg)
if errReported<20 then
errReported=errReported+1
reportError("ScriptContext",tostring(msg):sub(1,800))
end
end)
end)
pcall(function()
LP.OnTeleport:Connect(function()YQX.report("teleport",{to=tostring(game.PlaceId)})end)
end)
local frames,lastClock,fps=0,os.clock(),60
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
frames=frames+1
local now=os.clock()
if now-lastClock>=1 then fps,frames,lastClock=frames,0,now end
end)
local function ping()
local ok,v=pcall(function()return StatsSvc.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()end)
return ok and math.floor(v)or 0
end
local function memMb()
local ok,v=pcall(function()return StatsSvc:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb()end)
return ok and math.floor(v)or 0
end
local seq=0
local function handleCmds(cmds)
for _,c in ipairs(cmds or{})do
local fn=YQX.handlers[c.c]
if fn then task.spawn(fn,c.m)end
end
end
local function doPoll()
local d,code=YQX._call("/s/poll",{seq=seq,fps=fps,ping=ping(),mem=memMb(),gameId=game.JobId,bc=YQX.bc})
seq=seq+1
if d and d.ok then
if d.cfg then
if d.cfg.flags then YQX.global=d.cfg.flags end
if d.cfg.poll then YQX.poll=d.cfg.poll end
if d.cfg.bc~=nil then YQX.bc=d.cfg.bc end
local merged={}
for kk,vv in pairs(YQX.global)do merged[kk]=vv end
for kk,vv in pairs(YQX.mine)do merged[kk]=vv end
YQX.flags=merged
for name,fn in pairs(YQX.watch)do
if YQX.flags[name]~=YQX.last[name]then
YQX.last[name]=YQX.flags[name]
task.spawn(fn,YQX.flags[name])
end
end
if d.cfg.announce and d.cfg.announce~=""and d.cfg.announce~=YQX.lastAnnounce then
YQX.lastAnnounce=d.cfg.announce
serverMsg(d.cfg.announce,"公告")
end
end
handleCmds(d.cmds)
end
end
YQX._doPoll=doPoll
task.spawn(function()
while not YQX.stopped do
task.wait(YQX.poll or 20)
doPoll()
end
end)
if YQX.whitelisted and type(YQX.loadPayload)=="function"then
local ok,e=YQX.loadPayload()
if not ok and e~="empty"then warn("[YQX] payload:",e)end
end
notify(YQX.whitelisted and"已连接服务器 · 白名单用户"or"已连接服务器","success",4)
end
end
local function getItemModel(part)
if not part then return nil end
local current=part
while current and current~=Workspace do
if current:IsA("Model")then
if current:FindFirstChild("Owner")then
return current
end
local nameLower=string.lower(current.Name)
if string.find(nameLower,"axe")or string.find(nameLower,"box")or string.find(nameLower,"tool")or string.find(nameLower,"duck")then
return current
end
end
current=current.Parent
end
return nil
end
function YQXItemScan()
local r,n={},0
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
local lm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if pm then for _,o in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do n=n+1 r[n]=o end end
if lm then for _,o in ipairs(lm:GetChildren())do n=n+1 r[n]=o end end
for _,o in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren())do n=n+1 r[n]=o end
return r
end
local function isOwnedByMe(item)
if not item then return false end
local owner=item:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner then
if owner:IsA("ObjectValue")and owner.Value==speaker then
return true
end
if owner:IsA("StringValue")and owner.Value==speaker.Name then
return true
end
end
return false
end
local function isUnowned(item)
if not item then return false end
local owner=item:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if not owner then return true end
if owner:IsA("ObjectValue")and(owner.Value==nil)then return true end
if owner:IsA("StringValue")and(owner.Value==""or owner.Value==nil)then return true end
return false
end
local function IsSwimSound(obj)
if not obj:IsA("Sound")then return false end
local n=string.lower(obj.Name)
return string.find(n,"swim")or string.find(n,"splash")or string.find(n,"water")or string.find(n,"underwater")
end
local function MuteSwimmingSounds()
local character=speaker.Character
if not character then return end
for _,obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants())do
if IsSwimSound(obj)then
obj.Volume=0
obj:Stop()
end
end
end
local function StartSwimmingSoundMute()
MuteSwimmingSounds()
local character=speaker.Character
if not character then return end
if swimSoundConnections.DescendantAdded then swimSoundConnections.DescendantAdded:Disconnect()end
swimSoundConnections.DescendantAdded=character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if IsSwimSound(obj)then obj.Volume=0 obj:Stop()end
end)
end
local function StopSwimmingSoundMute()
if swimSoundConnections.DescendantAdded then
swimSoundConnections.DescendantAdded:Disconnect()
swimSoundConnections.DescendantAdded=nil
end
end
local function RestoreHumanoidState(humanoid)
if not humanoid then return end
humanoid.PlatformStand=false
for _,state in ipairs(Enum.HumanoidStateType:GetEnumItems())do
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(state,true)
end
humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics)
end
local function CleanupFly()
nowe=false
tpwalking=false
if upConnection then upConnection:Disconnect()upConnection=nil end
if downConnection then downConnection:Disconnect()downConnection=nil end
if currentBodyGyro then currentBodyGyro:Destroy()currentBodyGyro=nil end
if currentBodyVelocity then currentBodyVelocity:Destroy()currentBodyVelocity=nil end
StopSwimmingSoundMute()
local character=speaker.Character
local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if humanoid then
RestoreHumanoidState(humanoid)
humanoid.PlatformStand=false
local animate=character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
if animate then animate.Disabled=false end
local Hum=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")
if Hum then
for _,v in next,Hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()do v:AdjustSpeed(1)end
end
end
if pages[2]and pages[2]:FindFirstChild("FlyToggle")then
local btn=pages[2]:FindFirstChild("FlyToggle")
btn.Text=t("lbl_fly")
btn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(191,226,205)
end
end
local function StartFly()
local character=speaker.Character
local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not character or not humanoid then return end
nowe=true
StartSwimmingSoundMute()
local flyBtn=pages[2]and pages[2]:FindFirstChild("FlyToggle")
if flyBtn then
flyBtn.Text=t("lbl_on")
flyBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(170,220,191)
end
for i=1,speeds do
task.spawn(function()
local hb=RunService.Heartbeat
tpwalking=true
local chr=speaker.Character
local hum=chr and chr:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
while tpwalking and hb:Wait()and chr and hum and hum.Parent do
if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude>0 then
chr:TranslateBy(hum.MoveDirection)
end
end
end)
end
local animate=character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
if animate then animate.Disabled=true end
local Hum=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")
if Hum then
for _,v in next,Hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()do v:AdjustSpeed(0)end
end
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Flying,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.StrafingNoPhysics,false)
humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming,false)
humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming)
local function startSuspensionLoop()
local rigType=humanoid.RigType
local part=nil
if rigType==Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
part=character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
else
part=character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end
if not part then return end
local bg=Instance.new("BodyGyro",part)
bg.P=9e4
bg.maxTorque=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
bg.cframe=part.CFrame
currentBodyGyro=bg
local bv=Instance.new("BodyVelocity",part)
bv.velocity=Vector3.new(0,0.1,0)
bv.maxForce=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
currentBodyVelocity=bv
if nowe==true then
humanoid.PlatformStand=true
end
task.spawn(function()
while nowe==true or(speaker.Character and speaker.Character.Humanoid and speaker.Character.Humanoid.Health==0)do
RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
local chr=speaker.Character
local hum=chr and chr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not chr or not hum or hum.Health<=0 or not part.Parent then break end
bv.velocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
bg.cframe=workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame
end
if currentBodyGyro==bg then currentBodyGyro=nil end
if currentBodyVelocity==bv then currentBodyVelocity=nil end
bg:Destroy()
bv:Destroy()
humanoid.PlatformStand=false
local animate2=character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
if animate2 then animate2.Disabled=false end
tpwalking=false
end)
end
startSuspensionLoop()
end
local function IsLavaPart(part)
if part and part:IsA("BasePart")then
if part.Material==Enum.Material.Lava then return true end
local n=string.lower(part.Name)
return string.find(n,"lava")~=nil
end
return false
end
local function RemoveAllLava()
for _,obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants())do
if IsLavaPart(obj)then
pcall(function()obj:Destroy()end)
end
end
local terrain=Workspace.Terrain
if terrain then
pcall(function()terrain:ReplaceMaterial(Enum.Material.Lava,Enum.Material.Grass)end)
end
end
local function StartLavaDelete()
if lavaDeleteEnabled then return end
lavaDeleteEnabled=true
RemoveAllLava()
lavaDescendantAddedConn=Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
if IsLavaPart(desc)then
pcall(function()desc:Destroy()end)
end
end)
end
local function StopLavaDelete()
lavaDeleteEnabled=false
if lavaDescendantAddedConn then
lavaDescendantAddedConn:Disconnect()
lavaDescendantAddedConn=nil
end
end
local function httpGet(url)
local body=nil
pcall(function()
if request then
local r=request({Url=url,Method="GET"})
if r and(r.Success or r.StatusCode==200)then
body=r.Body or r.body
end
end
end)
if not body then
pcall(function()
if http_request then
local r=http_request({Url=url,Method="GET"})
if r then body=r.Body or r.body end
end
end)
end
if not body then
pcall(function()
if syn and syn.request then
local r=syn.request({Url=url,Method="GET"})
if r then body=r.Body or r.body end
end
end)
end
return body
end
local hopServerBtn={Text="Hop Server (Least Players)"}
local function hopLowestServer()
local placeId=game.PlaceId
local curJob=game.JobId
notify(t("n_search_server"),"info")
local url=string.format(
"https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%s/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100",
tostring(placeId)
)
local body=httpGet(url)
local bestId,bestPlayers=nil,math.huge
if body and#body>0 then
pcall(function()
local data=nil
if game.GetService then
local Hs=game:GetService("HttpService")
data=Hs:JSONDecode(body)
end
if type(data)=="table"and type(data.data)=="table"then
for _,s in ipairs(data.data)do
local id=s.id or s.jobId
local n=tonumber(s.playing)or tonumber(s.playerCount)or 99
local maxn=tonumber(s.maxPlayers)or 0
if id and tostring(id)~=tostring(curJob)and n<bestPlayers then
bestPlayers=n
bestId=tostring(id)
end
end
end
end)
end
if bestId then
notify(string.format(t("n_hop_result"),bestPlayers),"success")
hopServerBtn.Text=t("lbl_teleporting")
pcall(function()
TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId,bestId,speaker)
end)
task.delay(3,function()
hopServerBtn.Text=t("lbl_hop_server")
end)
else
notify(t("n_list_fail"),"warn")
pcall(function()
TeleportService:Teleport(placeId,speaker)
end)
end
end
local selectedTargetPlr=nil
local observing=false
local observeConn=nil
local savedCameraType=nil
local savedCameraSub=nil
local function getOtherPlayers()
local t={}
for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
if p~=speaker then
table.insert(t,p)
end
end
table.sort(t,function(a,b)
return a.Name:lower()<b.Name:lower()
end)
return t
end
local function stopObserve()
observing=false
if observeConn then
pcall(function()observeConn:Disconnect()end)
observeConn=nil
end
local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
if cam then
pcall(function()
if savedCameraType~=nil then
cam.CameraType=savedCameraType
else
cam.CameraType=Enum.CameraType.Custom
end
if savedCameraSub~=nil then
cam.CameraSubject=savedCameraSub
else
local hum=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then cam.CameraSubject=hum end
end
end)
end
savedCameraType=nil
savedCameraSub=nil
end
local function startObserve(plr)
if not plr or plr==speaker then return end
stopObserve()
local char=plr.Character
local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local head=char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not hum and not head then
notify(t("n_target_no_char"),"warn")
return
end
local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam then return end
savedCameraType=cam.CameraType
savedCameraSub=cam.CameraSubject
cam.CameraType=Enum.CameraType.Custom
cam.CameraSubject=hum or head
observing=true
notify(t("n_observing")..plr.Name..t("n_click_again_stop"),"success")
observeConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not observing then return end
if not plr.Parent then
stopObserve()
notify(t("n_target_left"),"warn")
return
end
local c=plr.Character
local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hd=c and c:FindFirstChild("Head")
if cam and(h or hd)then
cam.CameraSubject=h or hd
end
end)
end
local function rebuildPlayerList()
local list=getOtherPlayers()
if#list==0 then
notify(t("n_no_players"),"warn")
end
end
do
local findOwnedItem
local findUnownedItem
findOwnedItem=function(name)
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==name and isOwnedByMe(obj)then
return obj
end
end
return nil
end
findUnownedItem=function(name)
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==name and isUnowned(obj)then
return obj
end
end
return nil
end
local _wasNight=false
local function isGameNight()
local t=Lighting.ClockTime
return t>=18 or t<6
end
local _nightHeartbeatConn
_nightHeartbeatConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if _G.AlwaysDay then
Lighting.ClockTime=12
elseif _G.AlwaysNight then
Lighting.ClockTime=0
end
local night=isGameNight()
if night and not _wasNight then
pcall(function()
notify(t("n_night_fallen"),"warn")
end)
task.spawn(function()
local card=Instance.new("TextLabel")
card.Name="NightWarn"
card.Parent=main
card.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0)
card.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.08,0)
card.Size=UDim2.new(0,px(180),0,px(28))
card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(40,40,70)
card.BackgroundTransparency=0.15
card.Text=t("n_night_fallen")
card.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(200,210,255)
card.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
card.TextSize=px(12)
card.ZIndex=200
Instance.new("UICorner",card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,px(8))
task.wait(3.5)
if card and card.Parent then card:Destroy()end
end)
end
_wasNight=night
end)
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
if selectedTargetPlr==plr then
selectedTargetPlr=nil
if observing then
stopObserve()
end
end
end)

local mouse=Mouse
local function droptool(Position)
local aQ=speaker.Character;
if aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"then
local y=aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"
if y:FindFirstChild("ToolName")then
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientInteracted:FireServer(y,"Drop tool",Position or
speaker.Character.Head.CFrame)
end
end
for a,b in pairs(speaker.Backpack:GetChildren())do
if b.Name=="Tool"and b.ClassName=="Tool"then
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientInteracted:FireServer(b,"Drop tool",Position or
speaker.Character.Head.CFrame)
end
end
end
local function table_foreach(tbl,callback)
for i=1,#tbl do
callback(i,tbl[i])
end
end
local function getCFrame(part)
local part=part or(speaker.Character and speaker.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
if not part then return end
return part.CFrame
end
tp=function(pos)
local pos=pos or Mouse.Hit+Vector3.new(0,speaker.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Size.Y,0)
if typeof(pos)=="CFrame"then
speaker.Character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(pos)
elseif typeof(pos)=="Vector3"then
speaker.Character:MoveTo(pos)
end
end
getPosition=function(part)
return getCFrame(part).Position
end
getMouseTarget=function()
local b2=UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
return workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.p,
workspace.CurrentCamera:ViewportPointToRay(b2.x,b2.y,0).Direction*1000),
speaker.Character:GetDescendants())
end
function getTieredAxe()
return{
['Beesaxe']=13,['AxeAmber']=12,['ManyAxe']=15,['BasicHatchet']=0,
['RustyAxe']=-1,['Axe1']=2,['Axe2']=3,['AxeAlphaTesters']=9,
['Rukiryaxe']=8,['Axe3']=4,['AxeBetaTesters']=10,['FireAxe']=11,
['SilverAxe']=5,['EndTimesAxe']=16,['AxeChicken']=6,
['CandyCaneAxe']=1,['AxeTwitter']=7,['CandyCornAxe']=14
}
end
function getAxeList()
local aP={}
for J,v in pairs(speaker.Backpack:GetChildren())do
table.insert(aP,v)
end
local aQ=speaker.Character;
if aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"then
table.insert(aP,aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool"))
end
return aP
end
function getWorstAxe()
local aQ=speaker.Character;
if aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"then
local y=aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"
if y:FindFirstChild("ToolName")then return y end
end
local aR=9999;local aS=nil;local aT=getTieredAxe()
for J,v in pairs(getAxeList())do
if v:FindFirstChild("ToolName")then
if aT[v.ToolName.Value]<aR then aS=v;aR=aT[v.ToolName.Value]end
end
end
return aS
end
local function barkgetBestAxe()
local aQ=speaker.Character;
if aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"then
local y=aQ:FindFirstChildOfClass"Tool"
if y:FindFirstChild("ToolName")then return y end
end
local aU=-1;local aV=nil;local aT=getTieredAxe()
for J,v in pairs(getAxeList())do
if v:FindFirstChild("ToolName")then
if aT[v.ToolName.Value]>aU then aV=v;aU=aT[v.ToolName.Value]end
end
end
return aV
end

['EndTimesAxe']=1.58,['AxeChicken']=0.9,['CandyCaneAxe']=0,

}
