local EXCLUDE_TOOLS={
BlueprintTool=true,
PaintTool=true,
Blueprint=true,
}
local function tryRequireAxeClass(toolName)
if not toolName or toolName==""then return nil end
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local names={
toolName.."AxeClass",
"AxeClass_"..toolName,
"Axe"..toolName,
toolName,
}
local function findMod()
local loaded=RS:FindFirstChild("LoadedAssets")
if loaded then
for _,n in ipairs(names)do
local m=loaded:FindFirstChild(n)
if m and m:IsA("ModuleScript")then return m end
end
end
local am=RS:FindFirstChild("AxeModules")
if am then
for _,n in ipairs(names)do
local m=am:FindFirstChild(n,true)
if m and m:IsA("ModuleScript")then return m end
end
end
for _,d in ipairs(RS:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("ModuleScript")then
local lower=string.lower(d.Name)
local tn=string.lower(toolName)
if lower==tn.."axeclass"
or lower=="axeclass_"..tn
or lower=="axe"..tn
or(string.find(lower,tn)and string.find(lower,"axe"))then
return d
end
end
end
return nil
end
local mod=findMod()
if not mod then return nil end
local ok,data=pcall(function()
local r=require(mod)
if typeof(r)=="table"and typeof(r.new)=="function"then
return r.new()
end
return r
end)
if ok and typeof(data)=="table"then
return data
end
return nil
end
local SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE={
Flame=200000,BlueFlame=200000,Celestial=200000,
Void=200000,Ice=200000,Magma=200000,
RainbowFlame=200000,Radioactive=200000,Shine=200000,
LoneCave=200000,Spirit=200000,
Crystal=200000,Scale=200000,Infernal=200000,Ethereal=200000,
}
_G.SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE=SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE
local function GetNil(Name,DebugId)
local ok,instances=pcall(getnilinstances)
if not ok or not instances then return nil end
for _,Object in ipairs(instances)do
if Object.Name==Name and Object:GetDebugId()==DebugId then
return Object
end
end
return nil
end
local function findOlReliableTool()
local lp=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local containers={lp.Character,lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")}
for _,container in ipairs(containers)do
if container then
local t=container:FindFirstChild("OlReliable")
if t and t:IsA("Tool")then return t end
for _,tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do
if tool:IsA("Tool")then
local tn=tool:FindFirstChild("ToolName")
local in_=tool:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
if(tn and tn.Value=="OlReliable")or(in_ and in_.Value=="OlReliable")then
return tool
end
end
end
end
end
return nil
end
function isOlReliable()
return findOlReliableTool()~=nil
end
local OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS={
cooldown=0.35648788452148439,
height=0.45678281784057617,
hitPoints=800,
cuttingClass="Axe",
}
function applyOlReliableOverrides(args)
if not isOlReliable()then return args end
args.cooldown=OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS.cooldown
args.height=OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS.height
args.hitPoints=OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS.hitPoints
args.cuttingClass=OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS.cuttingClass
local olTool=findOlReliableTool()
if olTool then
args.tool=olTool
end
return args
end
local function getAxeData(tool)
if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool")then return nil end
if EXCLUDE_TOOLS[tool.Name]then return nil end
local toolNameVal=tool:FindFirstChild("ToolName")
local toolName=toolNameVal and toolNameVal.Value or tool.Name
local itemNameVal=tool:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
local itemName=itemNameVal and itemNameVal.Value or toolName
local data=tryRequireAxeClass(toolName)
if not data then
data=tryRequireAxeClass(itemName)
end
if not data then return nil end
if not data.Damage then return nil end
return{
tool=tool,
toolName=toolName,
itemName=itemName,
Damage=data.Damage or 0,
Range=data.Range or 10,
SwingCooldown=data.SwingCooldown or 0.5,
SpecialTrees=data.SpecialTrees or{},
}
end
function get_axe_cooldown(tool)
local axeData=getAxeData(tool)
if axeData then return axeData.SwingCooldown end
return 1
end
function get_axe_swingdelay(tool)
local axe_cooldown=get_axe_cooldown(tool)
local start=tick()
ReplicatedStorage.TestPing:InvokeServer()
local ping=(tick()-start)/2
local swing_delay=0.65*axe_cooldown-ping
return swing_delay
end
function getBestSawmill()
local best=nil
for i,v in pairs(Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren())do
if v:FindFirstChild("Owner")and v:FindFirstChild("ItemName")and v.Owner.Value==speaker and
v.ItemName.Value:sub(1,7)=="Sawmill"then
if not best then best=v
else
if#v.ItemName.Value>#best.ItemName.Value then best=v
elseif tonumber(v.ItemName.Value:sub(8,8))>tonumber(best.ItemName.Value:sub(8,8))then best=v end
end
end
end
return best
end
function barkgetBestAxe2()
local best,bestDmg
for _,v in ipairs(getAxeList())do
if v.Name=="Tool"then
local axeData=getAxeData(v)
if axeData and(not best or axeData.Damage>bestDmg)then
best=v;bestDmg=axeData.Damage
end
end
end
return best
end
local function getTools()
local tools={}
table_foreach(speaker.Backpack:GetChildren(),function(_,v)
if v:FindFirstChild("ToolName")then
tools[#tools+1]=v
end
end)
return tools
end
local getTool=function()
return speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("Tool")or speaker.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Tool")
end
local SPECIAL_TREE_TYPES={
Flame=true,BlueFlame=true,Celestial=true,
Void=true,Ice=true,Magma=true,
RainbowFlame=true,Radioactive=true,Shine=true,
LoneCave=true,Spirit=true,
Crystal=true,Scale=true,Infernal=true,Ethereal=true,
}
getWeaponData=function(tool)
if not tool then return nil end
local axeData=getAxeData(tool)
if not axeData then return nil end
return{
cooldown=axeData.SwingCooldown,
height=0.4,
faceVector=Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
hitPoints=axeData.Damage,
cuttingClass="Axe",
specialTrees=axeData.SpecialTrees,
rejectSpecial=false,
rejectList=nil,
}
end
resolveHitPoints=function(weaponData,treeClass)
if not weaponData then return nil end
if(not isOlReliable())and SPECIAL_TREE_TYPES[treeClass]then
if weaponData.specialTrees and weaponData.specialTrees[treeClass]then
local st=weaponData.specialTrees[treeClass]
if type(st)=="table"and st.Damage then
return st.Damage
elseif type(st)=="number"then
return st
end
end
local minDmg=SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]
if minDmg and weaponData.hitPoints>=minDmg then
return weaponData.hitPoints
end
return nil,"reject"
end
return weaponData.hitPoints
end
getBestAxe=function(targetTreeType)
local _olTool=findOlReliableTool()
if _olTool then
local _axeData=getAxeData(_olTool)
if _axeData then return _axeData end
return{tool=_olTool,toolName="OlReliable",itemName="OlReliable",Damage=OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS.hitPoints,SwingCooldown=OL_RELIABLE_PARAMS.cooldown,SpecialTrees=nil}
end
local lp=Players.LocalPlayer
local candidates={}
local containers={lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack"),lp.Character}
for _,container in ipairs(containers)do
if container then
for _,tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do
if tool:IsA("Tool")and not EXCLUDE_TOOLS[tool.Name]then
local axeData=getAxeData(tool)
if axeData then
table.insert(candidates,axeData)
end
end
end
end
end
if#candidates==0 then return nil end
local minDmg=targetTreeType and SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[targetTreeType]
if minDmg then
local filtered={}
for _,c in ipairs(candidates)do
local effDmg=c.Damage
if c.SpecialTrees and c.SpecialTrees[targetTreeType]then
local st=c.SpecialTrees[targetTreeType]
if type(st)=="table"and st.Damage then
effDmg=st.Damage
elseif type(st)=="number"then
effDmg=st
end
end
if effDmg>=minDmg then
table.insert(filtered,c)
end
end
if#filtered==0 then
notify(t("n_no_axe_dmg")..tostring(targetTreeType),"warn")
return nil
end
candidates=filtered
end
table.sort(candidates,function(a,b)
local function effDmg(c)
if targetTreeType and c.SpecialTrees and c.SpecialTrees[targetTreeType]then
local st=c.SpecialTrees[targetTreeType]
if type(st)=="table"and st.Damage then return st.Damage end
if type(st)=="number"then return st end
end
return c.Damage
end
return effDmg(a)>effDmg(b)
end)
return candidates[1]
end
cutAllSections=function(sections,axeData,treeClass,cutEvent)
if not sections or#sections==0 then return end
if not axeData then
notify(t("n_no_weapon_data"),"warn")
return
end
local hp=axeData.Damage
if(not isOlReliable())and treeClass and SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]then
local effDmg=hp
if axeData.SpecialTrees and axeData.SpecialTrees[treeClass]then
local st=axeData.SpecialTrees[treeClass]
if type(st)=="table"and st.Damage then
effDmg=st.Damage
elseif type(st)=="number"then
effDmg=st
end
end
if effDmg<SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]then
notify(t("n_dmg_insufficient_chop")..tostring(treeClass),"warn")
return
end
hp=effDmg
end
local proxy=ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.RemoteProxy
local n=#sections
local function worker(startIdx)
local i=startIdx
while i<=n do
local sec=sections[i]
if sec and sec.Parent then
local secId=sec:FindFirstChild("ID")and sec.ID.Value or 1
local _cutArgs={
tool=axeData.tool,
faceVector=Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
height=0.4,
sectionId=secId,
hitPoints=hp,
cooldown=axeData.SwingCooldown,
cuttingClass="Axe",
}
applyOlReliableOverrides(_cutArgs)
proxy:FireServer(cutEvent,_cutArgs)
end
task.wait(0.1)
i=i+3
end
end
task.spawn(worker,1)
task.spawn(worker,2)
task.spawn(worker,3)
end
cutPart=function(event,section,height,axeData,treeClass,cutEventTree)
if not axeData then return end
local hp=axeData.Damage
if(not isOlReliable())and treeClass and SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]then
local effDmg=hp
if axeData.SpecialTrees and axeData.SpecialTrees[treeClass]then
local st=axeData.SpecialTrees[treeClass]
if type(st)=="table"and st.Damage then
effDmg=st.Damage
elseif type(st)=="number"then
effDmg=st
end
end
if effDmg<SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]then
return
end
hp=effDmg
end
local equippedTool=axeData.tool
local treeFaceVec=Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
if cutEventTree then
local pp=cutEventTree.PrimaryPart or cutEventTree:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
if pp then
treeFaceVec=-pp.CFrame.LookVector
end
end
local _cutPartArgs={
tool=equippedTool,
faceVector=treeFaceVec,
height=height or 0.4,
sectionId=1,
hitPoints=hp,
cooldown=axeData.SwingCooldown,
cuttingClass="Axe",
}
applyOlReliableOverrides(_cutPartArgs)
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.RemoteProxy:FireServer(event,_cutPartArgs)
end
local treeListener=function(treeClass,callback)
local childAdded
childAdded=workspace.LogModels.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
local owner=child:WaitForChild("Owner")
if owner.Value==lp and child.TreeClass.Value==treeClass then
childAdded:Disconnect()
callback(child)
end
end)
end
local getBiggestTree=function(treeClass)
for _,v in next,workspace:GetChildren()do
if tostring(v)=="TreeRegion"then
for _,g in next,v:GetChildren()do
if g:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")and tostring(g.TreeClass.Value)==treeClass and
g:FindFirstChild("Owner")then
if g.Owner.Value==nil or tostring(g.Owner.Value)==tostring(speaker)then
if g:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")then
local sectionCount=0
for _,s in next,g:GetChildren()do
if s:FindFirstChild("ID")then sectionCount=sectionCount+1 end
end
if sectionCount>=3 then
for h,j in next,g:GetChildren()do
if j:FindFirstChild("ID")and j.ID.Value==1 and j.Size.Y>.5 then
return j
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
return false
end
local function removeFlameKillers()
pcall(function()
local hlArea=workspace:FindFirstChild("HLArea")
if not hlArea then return end
local hlModel=hlArea:FindFirstChild("Model")
if not hlModel then return end
for _,v in ipairs(hlModel:GetChildren())do
if v.Name=="LavaKillingPart"then
v.Parent=nil
end
end
end)
end
bringTree=function(treeClass,skipReturn)
if tostring(treeClass)=="Flame"then removeFlameKillers()end
local axeData=getBestAxe(treeClass)
if not axeData then
return
end
notify(t("n_bring_started")..tostring(treeClass),"info")
local treeCut=false
local chopped=nil
local tree=nil
local treeModel=nil
local hrp0=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local myPos=hrp0 and hrp0.Position
if myPos then
local bestDist=math.huge
for _,region in pairs(workspace:GetChildren())do
if region.Name=="TreeRegion"then
for _,model in pairs(region:GetChildren())do
if model:IsA("Model")then
local tc=model:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
if tc and tostring(tc.Value)==tostring(treeClass)then
local sections={}
for _,part in pairs(model:GetChildren())do
if part.Name=="WoodSection"and part:IsA("BasePart")then
table.insert(sections,part)
end
end
if#sections>=2 then
local mag=(sections[1].Position-myPos).Magnitude
if mag<bestDist then
bestDist=mag
tree=sections[1]
treeModel=model
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
if not tree then
tree=getBiggestTree(treeClass)
if tree then
treeModel=tree.Parent
end
end
if not tree then
bai.bringtree=false
bai.autofarm=false
notify(t("n_no_chopable"),"warn")
return
end
local function inDangerZone(pos)
if pos.X>=-543 and pos.X<=-27.8 and pos.Y>=-196.7 and pos.Y<=-148.2 and pos.Z>=-2961.7 and pos.Z<=-2668.7 then
return true
end
return false
end
if inDangerZone(tree.Position)then
notify(t("n_tree_danger"),"warn")
if treeModel and treeModel.Parent then
pcall(function()
treeModel:Destroy()
end)
end
return
end
local originCF=speaker.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
local function returnToOrigin()
if not skipReturn then
local y=originCF.Position.Y
if y<-100 then
originCF=originCF+Vector3.new(0,math.abs(y)+10,0)
end
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
end
end
local targetCF=originCF
if bai.treecutset then
targetCF=bai.treecutset
end
local lockConn=nil
local function unlockLocal()
if lockConn then
lockConn:Disconnect()
lockConn=nil
end
end
local function lockLocal(cf)
unlockLocal()
local function hold()
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(cf.Position)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(hrp.Orientation.Y),0)
pcall(function()
hrp.Velocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
hrp.RotVelocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
end)
end
end
hold()
lockConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(hold)
end
local function getTreeTopCF(model)
local bestY=-math.huge
local best=nil
for _,part in pairs(model:GetChildren())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
local topY=part.Position.Y+part.Size.Y/2
if bestY<topY then
bestY=topY
best=CFrame.new(part.Position.X,topY+2,part.Position.Z)
end
end
end
if best then
return best
end
return model:GetPivot()+Vector3.new(0,2,0)
end
local function dragModelTo(model,destCF)
if not model or not model.Parent then
return
end
local PrimaryPart=model.PrimaryPart
if not PrimaryPart then
PrimaryPart=model:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
end
if not PrimaryPart then
PrimaryPart=model:FindFirstChild("Main")
end
if not PrimaryPart then
PrimaryPart=model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
end
if not PrimaryPart then
return
end
if not model.PrimaryPart then
model.PrimaryPart=PrimaryPart
end
_dragIDCounter=_dragIDCounter+1
local dragID=getNextDragID()
local ClientIsDragging=ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging
pcall(function()
local own=ReplicatedStorage.Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientRequestOwnership")
if own then
own:FireServer(PrimaryPart)
end
end)
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Begin",model,dragID)
end)
for i=1,29 do
if not model.Parent or not model.PrimaryPart then
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("End",model,dragID)
end)
unlockLocal()
return
end
local late=(i>=28)
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,dragID)
if late then
local own=ReplicatedStorage.Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientRequestOwnership")
if own then
own:FireServer(PrimaryPart)
end
model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(destCF)
end
end)
task.wait(0.01)
end
task.wait(0.01)
local ended=false
task.spawn(function()
for j=30,60 do
if not model.Parent or not model.PrimaryPart then
break
end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,dragID)
end)
if j<60 then
task.wait(0.01)
end
end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("End",model,dragID)
end)
ended=true
end)
local t=0
while t<1.5 and not ended do
task.wait(0.05)
t=t+0.05
end
for k=1,3 do
if not model.Parent or not model.PrimaryPart then
break
end
if(model.PrimaryPart.Position-destCF.Position).Magnitude<=3 then
break
end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,dragID)
end)
end
end
local treeConn
treeConn=workspace.LogModels.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
local owner=child:WaitForChild("Owner",10)
if owner and owner.Value==speaker and child:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")and child.TreeClass.Value==treeClass then
treeConn:Disconnect()
chopped=child
treeCut=true
end
end)
local startTime=tick()
task.spawn(function()
while bai.bringtree and not treeCut do
if tick()-startTime>=15 then
treeConn:Disconnect()
unlockLocal()
notify(t("n_timeout_return"),"warn")
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
return
end
task.wait(0.1)
end
end)
local cutEvent=nil
if treeModel then
cutEvent=treeModel:FindFirstChild("CutEvent")or treeModel:FindFirstChild("CutEvent",true)
end
if not cutEvent and tree.Parent then
cutEvent=tree.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent")or tree.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent",true)
end
local floorPad=nil
local padCF=nil
do
local sec1=nil
if treeModel then
for _,p in pairs(treeModel:GetDescendants())do
if p:IsA("BasePart")and p.Name=="WoodSection"then
local sid=p:GetAttribute("SectionId")
if sid==1 or sid==nil then
sec1=p
break
end
end
end
if not sec1 then
sec1=treeModel:FindFirstChild("WoodSection",true)
end
end
if not sec1 then sec1=tree end
local basePos=sec1.Position
padCF=CFrame.new(basePos.X+3.5,basePos.Y-1,basePos.Z)
floorPad=Instance.new("Part")
floorPad.Name="FloorPad"
floorPad.Size=Vector3.new(6,0.5,6)
floorPad.CFrame=padCF
floorPad.Anchored=true
floorPad.CanCollide=true
floorPad.Transparency=1
floorPad.CanQuery=false
floorPad.Parent=workspace
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(basePos.X+3.5,basePos.Y+0.5,basePos.Z)
end
end
local iterations=0
while bai.bringtree and not treeCut do
iterations=iterations+1
if iterations>1000 then
treeConn:Disconnect()
unlockLocal()
notify(t("n_too_many_return"),"warn")
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
return
end
if cutEvent then
cutPart(cutEvent,1,0.55,axeData,treeClass,treeModel)
else
if tree.Parent and tree.Parent:FindFirstChild("CutEvent")then
cutPart(tree.Parent.CutEvent,1,0.55,axeData,treeClass,treeModel)
end
end
task.wait(0.05)
end
unlockLocal()
if not bai.bringtree then
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
return
end
local waitStart=tick()
while not chopped do
if tick()-waitStart>5 then
unlockLocal()
notify(t("n_log_not_found"),"warn")
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
return
end
task.wait(0.1)
end
chopped:WaitForChild("Owner",10)
task.wait(0.2)
local woodPart=chopped:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
if not woodPart then
woodPart=chopped:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
end
if not woodPart then
unlockLocal()
notify(t("n_no_woodsection"),"warn")
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
return
end
if inDangerZone(woodPart.Position)then
unlockLocal()
notify(t("n_log_danger"),"warn")
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
pcall(function()
chopped:Destroy()
end)
return
end
if not chopped.PrimaryPart then
chopped.PrimaryPart=woodPart
end
dragModelTo(chopped,targetCF)
unlockLocal()
returnToOrigin()
if floorPad and floorPad.Parent then floorPad:Destroy()floorPad=nil end
notify(t("n_tree_brought"),"success")
end
getPlanks=function()
local plankList={};
for _,plank in next,Workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren()do
if plank:FindFirstChild('WoodSection')and plank:FindFirstChild('Owner')and plank.Owner.Value==
speaker and not table.find(plankList,plank)then
table.insert(plankList,plank)
end
end
return plankList;
end
shuaxinlb=function(zji)
bai.dropdown={}
if zji==true then
for p,I in next,game.Players:GetChildren()do table.insert(bai.dropdown,I.Name)end
else
for p,I in next,game.Players:GetChildren()do
if I~=lp then table.insert(bai.dropdown,I.Name)end
end
end
end
end
do
shuaxinlb(true)
scannedTreeClasses={}
selectedTreeClass="Generic"
treeClassIndex=1
scanTreeClasses=function()
local found={}
local seen={}
pcall(function()
for _,region in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren())do
if region and region.Name and(region.Name=="TreeRegion"or region.Name:lower():find("treeregion"))then
for _,tree in ipairs(region:GetChildren())do
local tc=tree:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
if tc and typeof(tc)=="Instance"and tc:IsA("StringValue")then
local val=tc.Value
if val and val~=""and not seen[val]then
seen[val]=true
table.insert(found,tostring(val))
end
end
end
end
end
end)
pcall(table.sort,found)
if#found==0 then found={"Generic"}end
scannedTreeClasses=found
selectedTreeClass=found[1]
treeClassIndex=1
end
pcall(scanTreeClasses)
if scannedTreeClasses==nil or#scannedTreeClasses==0 then
scannedTreeClasses={"Generic"}
selectedTreeClass="Generic"
treeClassIndex=1
end
selectedTree=nil
selectedSawmill=nil
sawPos=nil
isDecomposeActive=false
decomposeConn=nil
bai.cachedSawmill=nil
bai.cachedSawmillPos=nil
local function uprightCFrame(ref,target)
local r5,Angles=CFrame.new(target.Position),CFrame.Angles
return r5*Angles(0,math.rad(ref and ref.Orientation.Y or 0),0)
end
function safeTeleport(cf)
local HumanoidRootPart=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if HumanoidRootPart then
HumanoidRootPart.CFrame=uprightCFrame(HumanoidRootPart,cf)
task.wait(0.05)
if HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y<-100 then
HumanoidRootPart.CFrame=uprightCFrame(HumanoidRootPart,cf)+Vector3.new(0,10,0)
end
end
end
lockPlayerConn=nil
function unlockPlayer()
if lockPlayerConn then
lockPlayerConn:Disconnect()
lockPlayerConn=nil
end
end
function lockPlayerAt(cf)
unlockPlayer()
local function hold()
local HumanoidRootPart=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if HumanoidRootPart then
HumanoidRootPart.CFrame=uprightCFrame(HumanoidRootPart,cf)
HumanoidRootPart.Velocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
HumanoidRootPart.RotVelocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
end
end
hold()
lockPlayerConn=game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(hold)
end
local DRAG_TOTAL=60
local DRAG_SET_START=28
local DRAG_SET_COUNT=2
local DRAG_SET_INTERVAL=0.01
local DRAG_REFRESH_INTERVAL=0.01
local DRAG_AFTER_WAIT=0.01
function dragToPosition(model,targetCF,callback,timeout)
if not model or not model.Parent then
if callback then callback()end
return
end
local PrimaryPart=model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")or model:FindFirstChild("Main")or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
if not PrimaryPart then
if callback then callback()end
return
end
if not model.PrimaryPart then model.PrimaryPart=PrimaryPart end
lockPlayerAt(PrimaryPart.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
local dragID=getNextDragID()
local ClientIsDragging=ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientIsDragging
pcall(function()
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientRequestOwnership:FireServer(PrimaryPart)
end)
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Begin",model,dragID)
end)
for i_0=1,29 do
if not model.Parent or not model.PrimaryPart then
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("End",model,dragID)
end)
if not callback then return end
callback()
return
end
local isLast=i_0>=28
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,dragID)
if isLast then
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientRequestOwnership:FireServer(PrimaryPart)
model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(targetCF)
end
end)
if not(i_0<29)then
elseif isLast then
task.wait(0.01)
else
task.wait(0.01)
end
end
task.wait(0.01)
local done=false
task.spawn(function()
for i_0=30,60 do
if not model.Parent or not model.PrimaryPart then break end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,dragID)
end)
if i_0<60 then task.wait(0.01)end
end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("End",model,dragID)
end)
done=true
end)
if timeout then
local waited=0
while waited<1.5 do
if done then break end
task.wait(0.05)
waited+=0.05
end
for i_1=1,3 do
if not model.Parent or not model.PrimaryPart or(model.PrimaryPart.Position-targetCF.Position).Magnitude<=3 then
break
end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,dragID)
end)
end
end
task.wait(0.75)
local retries=0
while true do
if not model.Parent then break end
if not model.PrimaryPart or not(retries<2)or(model.PrimaryPart.Position-targetCF.Position).Magnitude<=3 then
break
end
retries+=1
lockPlayerAt(PrimaryPart.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
task.wait(0.15)
local newID=getNextDragID()
pcall(function()
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientRequestOwnership:FireServer(PrimaryPart)
end)
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Begin",model,newID)
end)
for i_2=1,10 do
if not model.Parent then break end
pcall(function()
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.ClientRequestOwnership:FireServer(PrimaryPart)
end)
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("Refresh",model,newID)
end)
pcall(function()
model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(targetCF)
end)
task.wait(0.05)
end
pcall(function()
ClientIsDragging:FireServer("End",model,newID)
end)
task.wait(0.15)
end
unlockPlayer()
if callback then callback()end
end

local function getTreeClassFromModel(model)
local TreeClass=model:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
if TreeClass then return TreeClass.Value end
local Name=model.Name
for _,r7 in ipairs({"SpookyNeon","Spooky","GoldSwampy","Cherry","CaveCrawler","MuckySewer"})do
if Name:find(r7)then return r7 end
end
return"Generic"
end
local function getTreeTopCFrame(model)
local bestY=-math.huge
local bestCF
for _,part in pairs(model:GetChildren())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
local topY=part.Position.Y+part.Size.Y/2
if bestY<topY then
bestY=topY
bestCF=CFrame.new(part.Position.X,topY+2,part.Position.Z)
end
end
end
if not bestCF then bestCF=model:GetPivot()+Vector3.new(0,2,0)end
return bestCF
end
local function resolveAxeTool(axeData)
if not axeData then return nil end
if axeData.tool and axeData.tool.Parent then return axeData.tool end
local char=speaker.Character
if char then
for _,t in ipairs(char:GetChildren())do
if t:IsA("Tool")and getAxeData(t)then return t end
end
end
local bp=speaker:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
if bp then
local want=axeData.toolName or axeData.itemName
if want then
local f=bp:FindFirstChild(want)
if f and f:IsA("Tool")then return f end
end
for _,t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren())do
if t:IsA("Tool")then
local tn=t:FindFirstChild("ToolName")or t:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
if want and tn and tn.Value==want then return t end
if getAxeData(t)then return t end
end
end
end
return axeData.tool
end
local function chopTreeSections(treeModel,existingLogs,timeoutSec)
_G.YQXForceStop=false
timeoutSec=timeoutSec or 10
existingLogs=existingLogs or{}
if not treeModel or not treeModel.Parent then
return false,{},nil,"tree_gone"
end
local sections={}
local rootSection=nil
for _,child in pairs(treeModel:GetChildren())do
if child.Name=="WoodSection"and child:IsA("BasePart")and child:FindFirstChild("ID")then
local id=child.ID.Value
if id>=2 then
table.insert(sections,{id=id,part=child})
elseif id==1 then
rootSection=child
end
end
end
if#sections==0 then
return false,{},rootSection,"no_sections"
end
table.sort(sections,function(a,b)return a.id>b.id end)
local cutEvent=treeModel:FindFirstChild("CutEvent")
if not cutEvent then
return false,{},rootSection,"no_cutevent"
end
local treeClass=getTreeClassFromModel(treeModel)
local axeData=getBestAxe(treeClass)
if not axeData then
return false,{},rootSection,"no_axe"
end
local Tool=resolveAxeTool(axeData)
if not Tool then
return false,{},rootSection,"no_tool"
end
local Damage=axeData.Damage or 10000
local SwingCooldown=axeData.SwingCooldown or 0.3
if axeData.SpecialTrees and axeData.SpecialTrees[treeClass]then
local st=axeData.SpecialTrees[treeClass]
if type(st)=="table"then
Damage=st.Damage or Damage
SwingCooldown=st.SwingCooldown or SwingCooldown
elseif type(st)=="number"then
Damage=st
end
end
if(not isOlReliable())and _G.SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE and _G.SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]and Damage<_G.SPECIAL_TREE_MIN_DAMAGE[treeClass]then
return false,{},rootSection,"dmg_low"
end
local Interaction=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
local RemoteProxy=Interaction and Interaction:FindFirstChild("RemoteProxy")
if not RemoteProxy then
return false,{},rootSection,"no_remote"
end
notify(string.format(t("n_starting_decomp")or"开始分解 %d 段",#sections),"info")
local threads={}
for _,sec in ipairs(sections)do
local secPart=sec.part
local secID=sec.id
local th=task.spawn(function()
while true do
local _decompArgs={
height=0.11,
faceVector=Vector3.new(-1,0,0),
cooldown=SwingCooldown,
sectionId=secID,
hitPoints=Damage,
tool=Tool,
cuttingClass="Axe",
}
pcall(function()
RemoteProxy:FireServer(cutEvent,_decompArgs)
end)
task.wait(0.01)
if _G.YQXForceStop then break end
if not secPart or not secPart.Parent then break end
end
end)
threads[th]=true
end
local function hasHighIDSections()
if not treeModel or not treeModel.Parent then return false end
for _,child in pairs(treeModel:GetChildren())do
if child.Name=="WoodSection"and child:FindFirstChild("ID")and child.ID.Value>=2 then
return true
end
end
return false
end
local function collectNewLogs()
local list={}
local folder=workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if not folder then return list end
for _,log in pairs(folder:GetChildren())do
if not existingLogs[log]then
local owner=log:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner and owner.Value==speaker then
table.insert(list,log)
end
end
end
return list
end
local startTime=tick()
while hasHighIDSections()and tick()-startTime<10 and not _G.YQXForceStop do
task.wait(0.01)
end
for th in pairs(threads)do
pcall(task.cancel,th)
end
if hasHighIDSections()then
notify("砍树超时","warn")
return false,{},rootSection,"timeout"
end
local newLogs=collectNewLogs()
return true,newLogs,rootSection,"ok"
end
decomposeOnly=function(treeModel)
_G.stopDrag=false
if not treeModel or not treeModel.Parent then
notify("无效的树","warn")
return
end
local existingLogs={}
local folder=workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if folder then
for _,log in pairs(folder:GetChildren())do existingLogs[log]=true end
end
local allDone,newLogs,_,reason=chopTreeSections(treeModel,existingLogs,10)
if reason=="no_sections"then
notify(t("n_no_decomp_parts")or"没有可分解的树段","warn")
return
elseif reason=="no_cutevent"then
notify(t("n_no_cutevent")or"找不到 CutEvent","warn")
return
elseif reason=="timeout"then
return
elseif reason=="no_axe"or reason=="no_tool"then
notify(t("n_no_usable_axe")or"没有可用斧头","warn")
return
elseif reason=="dmg_low"then
notify(t("n_dmg_insufficient")or"斧头伤害不足","warn")
return
end
if allDone then
notify(t("n_decompose_complete")or"分解完成","success")
else
notify(string.format("分解未完成（新木头 %d）",#newLogs),"warn")
reportError("decomposeOnly","partial_or_timeout",{logs=#newLogs})
end
end
decomposeAndProcess=function(treeModel,sawmillModel,sawmillCF)
if not sawmillCF then
notify(t("n_sawmill_invalid")or"锯木机位置无效","warn")
return
end
_G.stopDrag=false
if not treeModel or not treeModel.Parent then
notify("无效的树","warn")
return
end
local existingLogs={}
local folder=workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if folder then
for _,log in pairs(folder:GetChildren())do existingLogs[log]=true end
end
local allDone,newLogs,rootSection,reason=chopTreeSections(treeModel,existingLogs,10)
if reason=="no_cutevent"then
notify(t("n_no_cutevent")or"找不到 CutEvent","warn")
return
elseif reason=="no_sections"then
notify(t("n_no_decomp_parts")or"没有可分解的树段","warn")
return
elseif reason=="timeout"then
return
elseif reason=="no_axe"or reason=="no_tool"then
notify(t("n_no_usable_axe")or"没有可用斧头","warn")
return
elseif reason=="dmg_low"then
notify(t("n_dmg_insufficient")or"斧头伤害不足","warn")
return
end
if allDone then
notify("分解完成，开始拖拽","success")
else
notify(string.format("分解部分完成（木头 %d），继续拖拽",#newLogs),"warn")
end
local dragList={}
for _,log in ipairs(newLogs)do
if log and log.Parent then table.insert(dragList,log)end
end
if#dragList==0 then
notify(t("n_no_wood_found")or"没有可拖拽的木头","warn")
reportError("decomposeAndProcess","no_logs_after_complete",{reason=reason})
return
end
if treeModel and treeModel.Parent then
local root=rootSection
if not root then
for _,child in pairs(treeModel:GetChildren())do
if child.Name=="WoodSection"and child:FindFirstChild("ID")and child.ID.Value==1 then
root=child
break
end
end
end
if root and root.Parent then
if not treeModel.PrimaryPart then treeModel.PrimaryPart=root end
table.insert(dragList,treeModel)
end
end
if#dragList==0 then
notify(t("n_no_wood_found")or"没有可拖拽的木头","warn")
reportError("decomposeAndProcess","no_wood_after_chop",{reason=reason})
return
end
notify(string.format("拖拽 %d 段到锯木机",#dragList),"info")
local offset=Vector3.new(0.7,0,0)
local sawTarget=CFrame.new(sawmillCF.p+sawmillCF:VectorToWorldSpace(offset))*(sawmillCF-sawmillCF.p)
sawTarget=sawTarget*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90),math.rad(90),0)
for i,log in ipairs(dragList)do
if _G.stopDrag then break end
if log and log.Parent then
local pp=log.PrimaryPart or log:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")or log:FindFirstChild("Main")or log:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
if pp then
if not log.PrimaryPart then log.PrimaryPart=pp end
notify(string.format("拖拽 %d / %d",i,#dragList),"info")
pcall(safeTeleport,pp.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,2,0))
task.wait(0.08)
pcall(dragToPosition,log,sawTarget)
task.wait(0.12)
end
end
end
notify(t("n_all_dragged")or"已全部拖到锯木机","success")
end
YQXPipeline={running=false,thread=nil,cancel=false,phase=nil}
YQXPipeline.collect=function(center,half,classFilter)
local list={}
local lm=workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if lm then
for _,m in ipairs(lm:GetChildren())do
if m:IsA("Model")and isOwnedByMe(m)and m:FindFirstChild("CutEvent")then
local sections=0
for _,c in ipairs(m:GetChildren())do
if c.Name=="WoodSection"then sections=sections+1 end
end
local root=m.PrimaryPart or m:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
local tcv=m:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
local classOk=(not classFilter)or(tcv~=nil and tcv.Value==classFilter)
if sections>=2 and root and classOk then
if center then
local d=root.Position-center
if math.abs(d.X)<=half and math.abs(d.Z)<=half then
table.insert(list,{model=m,dist=d.Magnitude})
end
else
table.insert(list,{model=m,dist=0})
end
end
end
end
end
table.sort(list,function(x,y)return x.dist<y.dist end)
return list
end
-- 先把全部树分解完，再一个个拖进锯木机
YQXPipeline.decomposeAndDrag=function(trees,sawmillCF)
local P=YQXPipeline
_G.stopDrag=false
local dragList={}
local total=#trees
P.phase="decompose"
for i,info in ipairs(trees)do
if P.cancel then return false end
local treeModel=info.model
if treeModel and treeModel.Parent then
notify(string.format(LANG=="en"and"Decompose %d / %d"or"分解 %d / %d",i,total),"info")
local existingLogs={}
local folder=workspace:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if folder then
for _,log in pairs(folder:GetChildren())do existingLogs[log]=true end
end
local allDone,newLogs,rootSection,reason=chopTreeSections(treeModel,existingLogs,10)
if reason=="ok"then
for _,log in ipairs(newLogs)do
if log and log.Parent then table.insert(dragList,log)end
end
if treeModel and treeModel.Parent then
local root=rootSection
if not root then
for _,child in pairs(treeModel:GetChildren())do
if child.Name=="WoodSection"and child:FindFirstChild("ID")and child.ID.Value==1 then
root=child
break
end
end
end
if root and root.Parent then
if not treeModel.PrimaryPart then treeModel.PrimaryPart=root end
table.insert(dragList,treeModel)
end
end
end
end
end
if P.cancel then return false end
if#dragList==0 then
notify(t("n_no_wood_found")or"没有可拖拽的木头","warn")
return false
end
P.phase="drag"
notify(string.format(LANG=="en"and"Dragging %d pieces to the sawmill"or"拖拽 %d 段到锯木机",#dragList),"info")
local offset=Vector3.new(0.7,0,0)
local sawTarget=CFrame.new(sawmillCF.p+sawmillCF:VectorToWorldSpace(offset))*(sawmillCF-sawmillCF.p)
sawTarget=sawTarget*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90),math.rad(90),0)
for i,log in ipairs(dragList)do
if P.cancel or _G.stopDrag then break end
if log and log.Parent then
local pp=log.PrimaryPart or log:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")or log:FindFirstChild("Main")or log:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
if pp then
if not log.PrimaryPart then log.PrimaryPart=pp end
notify(string.format(LANG=="en"and"Drag %d / %d"or"拖拽 %d / %d",i,#dragList),"info")
pcall(safeTeleport,pp.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,2,0))
task.wait(0.08)
pcall(dragToPosition,log,sawTarget)
task.wait(0.12)
end
end
end
return true
end
YQXPipeline.chopClass=function(cls,startCF)
local P=YQXPipeline
local prevCut=bai.treecutset
bai.treecutset=startCF
bai.autofarm=true
bai.bringtree=true
local fastFails=0
while bai.autofarm and not P.cancel do
local t0=os.clock()
safeCall("bringTree_multi",bringTree,cls,true)
if os.clock()-t0<0.5 then
fastFails=fastFails+1
if fastFails>=5 then break end
else
fastFails=0
end
task.wait(0.1)
end
bai.autofarm=false
bai.bringtree=false
bai.treecutset=prevCut
local h=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h and not P.cancel then
local cf=startCF
if cf.Position.Y<-100 then cf=cf+Vector3.new(0,math.abs(cf.Position.Y)+10,0)end
h.CFrame=cf
end
end
function YQXPipelineStop()
local P=YQXPipeline
if not P.running then return end
P.cancel=true
_G.YQXForceStop=true
_G.stopDrag=true
bai.autofarm=false
bai.bringtree=false
pcall(unlockPlayer)
-- 等待/分解/拖拽阶段可以直接杀掉任务线程；砍树阶段由 bringTree 自己收尾，避免它的锁定连接残留
if P.phase=="decompose"or P.phase=="drag"or P.phase=="wait"then
if P.thread then pcall(task.cancel,P.thread)end
P.running=false
P.phase=nil
pcall(unlockPlayer)
end
notify(LANG=="en"and"Force stopped"or"已强制停止","warn")
end
function YQXPipelineStart(mode,getClass)
local P=YQXPipeline
if P.running then
YQXPipelineStop()
return
end
if isDecomposeActive then
notify(t("n_decomp_active"),"warn")
return
end
if not bai.cachedSawmill or not bai.cachedSawmill.Parent then
notify(t("n_mem_sawmill_first"),"warn")
return
end
if not bai.cachedSawmillPos then
notify(t("n_sawmill_invalid"),"warn")
return
end
if bai.autofarm or bai.bringtree then
notify(LANG=="en"and"Stop auto chop / bring tree first"or"请先停止自动砍树或带来树","warn")
return
end
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return end
local startCF=hrp.CFrame
local sawCF=bai.cachedSawmillPos
local trees=nil
local classes=nil
if mode=="process"then
trees=P.collect(hrp.Position,50)
if#trees==0 then
notify(LANG=="en"and"No felled trees of yours in range"or"范围内没有属于自己且已砍倒的树","warn")
return
end
elseif mode=="afk"then
classes={}
for _,c in ipairs(getClass and getClass()or{})do table.insert(classes,c)end
if#classes==0 then
notify(LANG=="en"and"Select at least one tree species first"or"请先在树种下拉里至少选择一个树种","warn")
return
end
end
P.running=true
P.cancel=false
P.phase=(mode=="chop"or mode=="afk")and"chop"or nil
_G.YQXForceStop=false
P.thread=task.spawn(function()
local ok,err=pcall(function()
if mode=="afk"then
local GROW=300
local readyAt={}
local lastNotify=0
while not P.cancel do
for idx,cls in ipairs(classes)do
if P.cancel then break end
P.phase="wait"
local starLampDoneThisWait=false
while not P.cancel and os.clock()<(readyAt[cls]or 0)do
if os.clock()-lastNotify>=30 then
lastNotify=os.clock()
notify(string.format(LANG=="en"and"Waiting for %s to regrow: %ds"or"等待 %s 生长：剩余 %d 秒",tostring(cls),math.ceil(readyAt[cls]-os.clock())),"info",3)
end
-- 空闲时间自动合成星星灯（剩余生长期>10秒才做）
local remain=(readyAt[cls]or 0)-os.clock()
if not starLampDoneThisWait and remain>10 and tostring(cls)=="Celestial" then
starLampDoneThisWait=true
pcall(function()
local lampOk=YQXRunStarLamp()
if lampOk then
local targetCF=CFrame.new(-7488.54492,314.934692,4391.23047)
local lamp=nil
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="StarLantern"and(isOwnedByMe(obj)or isUnowned(obj))then
lamp=obj
break
end
end
if lamp and lamp.Parent then
teleportOneItem(lamp,targetCF)
notify("空闲时间已合成星星灯并放置到指定坐标","success",3)
end
end
pcall(function()
local h=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h then safeTeleport(startCF)end
end)
end)
end
task.wait(0.5)
end
if P.cancel then break end
notify(string.format(LANG=="en"and"AFK: chopping %s (%d/%d)"or"挂机：砍 %s（%d/%d）",tostring(cls),idx,#classes),"info")
P.phase="chop"
P.chopClass(cls,startCF)
if P.cancel then break end
readyAt[cls]=os.clock()+GROW
local list=P.collect(nil,nil,cls)
if#list>0 then
P.decomposeAndDrag(list,sawCF)
else
notify(LANG=="en"and"No felled trees of this species"or"这个树种没有已砍倒的树","warn")
end
if P.cancel then break end
pcall(unlockPlayer)
pcall(function()
local h=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h then safeTeleport(startCF)end
end)
P.phase="wait"
task.wait(3)
end
end
return
end
if mode=="chop"then
P.chopClass(getClass and getClass()or"Generic",startCF)
if P.cancel then return end
trees=P.collect(nil,nil)
if#trees==0 then
notify(LANG=="en"and"No felled trees of yours"or"没有属于自己的已砍倒的树","warn")
return
end
end
if P.cancel then return end
P.decomposeAndDrag(trees,sawCF)
end)
if not ok then
warn("[YQX] pipeline",err)
pcall(function()notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error")end)
end
bai.autofarm=false
bai.bringtree=false
pcall(unlockPlayer)
local wasCancelled=P.cancel
local phaseAtEnd=P.phase
P.running=false
P.phase=nil
if wasCancelled and phaseAtEnd=="chop"then
pcall(function()
local h=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h then safeTeleport(startCF)end
end)
end
if not wasCancelled and mode~="afk"then
pcall(function()
local h=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h then safeTeleport(startCF)end
end)
notify(LANG=="en"and"Auto process finished"or"自动加工结束","success")
end
end)
end
end
-- 合成期间临时穿墙，避免人物把材料顶掉
_G.YQXSetNoclip=function(on)
local old=_G.__YQXNoclipConn
if old then pcall(function()old:Disconnect()end)_G.__YQXNoclipConn=nil end
local saved=_G.__YQXNoclipSaved
if saved then
for part,v in pairs(saved)do
pcall(function()if part and part.Parent then part.CanCollide=v end end)
end
_G.__YQXNoclipSaved=nil
end
if on then
saved={}
_G.__YQXNoclipSaved=saved
_G.__YQXNoclipConn=game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Connect(function()
local c=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
if not c then return end
for _,part in ipairs(c:GetDescendants())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
if saved[part]==nil then saved[part]=part.CanCollide end
part.CanCollide=false
end
end
end)
end
end
-- ==================== EvilHeart 破碎之心合成 ====================
local evilHeartMaterials={"HappyBall","Egg","Cocoa"}
local evilHeartDragTargets={
[1]=CFrame.new(-515.637939,-86.914101,-2013.382812),
[2]=CFrame.new(-526.625244,-86.957069,-2017.517578),
[3]=CFrame.new(-536.980713,-86.994179,-2021.368164),
}
local evilHeartStopFlag=false
local evilHeartAutoRunning=false
local evilHeartCount=1

local function findItemByKey(key,allowFuzzy)
if not key then return nil end
local keyLower=string.lower(key)
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if pm then
for _,obj in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and isOwnedByMe(obj)then
if obj.Name==key then return obj end
if allowFuzzy and string.find(string.lower(obj.Name),keyLower,1,true)then
return obj
end
end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and isOwnedByMe(obj)then
if obj.Name==key then return obj end
if allowFuzzy and string.find(string.lower(obj.Name),keyLower,1,true)then
return obj
end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and isUnowned(obj)then
if obj.Name==key then return obj end
if allowFuzzy and string.find(string.lower(obj.Name),keyLower,1,true)then
return obj
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function openItem(item)
if not item or not item.Parent then return false end
local inter=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
local clientInteracted=inter and inter:FindFirstChild("ClientInteracted")
local clientDragging=inter and inter:FindFirstChild("ClientIsDragging")
if not clientInteracted then return false end
local target=item
pcall(function()
if not target.Parent then
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if pm then
for _,c in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
if c:IsA("Model")and isOwnedByMe(c)and c.Name==item.Name then
target=c
break
end
end
end
end
end)
local _dragID=getNextDragID()
pcall(function()
if clientDragging then
clientDragging:FireServer("Begin",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("Refresh",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("End",target,_dragID)
end
end)
task.wait(0.2)
local _dragID=getNextDragID()
pcall(function()
if clientDragging then
clientDragging:FireServer("Begin",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("Refresh",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("End",target,_dragID)
end
end)
task.wait(0.25)
pcall(function()
clientInteracted:FireServer(target,"Open box")
end)
task.wait(0.2)
pcall(function()
clientInteracted:FireServer(target,"Open box")
end)
task.wait(0.25)
return true
end

local function waitItemReady(item,timeout)
if not item then return false end
timeout=timeout or 10
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<timeout do
if evilHeartStopFlag then return false end
if item and item.Parent then
local pp=item.PrimaryPart or item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
if pp then return true end
end
task.wait(0.1)
end
return false
end

local function waitEvilHeartAppear(timeout,preSet)
timeout=timeout or 30
preSet=preSet or{}
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<timeout do
if evilHeartStopFlag then return nil end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="EvilHeart"and isUnowned(obj)then
if not preSet[obj]then
return obj
end
end
end
task.wait(0.1)
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="EvilHeart"and isUnowned(obj)then
if not preSet[obj]then
return obj
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function evilHeartNameMatches(name,matName)
name=string.lower(tostring(name or""))
matName=string.lower(tostring(matName or""))
return name==matName or(name:gsub("%s",""))==(matName:gsub("%s",""))
end
local function findEvilHeartOwnedMat(matName)
do
local pmFast=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if pmFast then
for _,obj in ipairs(pmFast:GetChildren())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and evilHeartNameMatches(obj.Name,matName)and isOwnedByMe(obj)then
return obj
end
end
end
end
local containers={speaker.Character,speaker:FindFirstChild("Backpack"),Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")}
for _,container in ipairs(containers)do
if container then
for _,obj in ipairs(container:GetDescendants())do
if (obj:IsA("Model")or obj:IsA("BasePart"))and evilHeartNameMatches(obj.Name,matName)then
if obj:IsA("BasePart")and obj.Parent and obj.Parent:IsA("Model")then
obj=obj.Parent
end
if isOwnedByMe(obj)or obj.Parent==speaker.Character or obj.Parent==speaker:FindFirstChild("Backpack")then
return obj
end
end
end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and evilHeartNameMatches(obj.Name,matName)and isOwnedByMe(obj)then
return obj
end
end
return nil
end
local function buyEvilHeartMat(matName,returnPos)
local owned=findEvilHeartOwnedMat(matName)
if owned then return owned end
-- 快速查找自己的材料（只看 PlayerModels / 背包 / 角色第一层，不扫全图）
local function findOwnedFast()
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if pm then
for _,obj in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and evilHeartNameMatches(obj.Name,matName)and isOwnedByMe(obj)then return obj end
end
end
for _,container in ipairs({speaker.Character,speaker:FindFirstChild("Backpack")})do
if container then
for _,obj in ipairs(container:GetChildren())do
if evilHeartNameMatches(obj.Name,matName)then return obj end
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function findShopProduct()
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
if not stores then return nil,nil end
for _,store in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local items=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,it in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if it:IsA("Model")and evilHeartNameMatches(it.Name,matName)and isUnowned(it)then
return it,store.Name
end
end
end
end
return nil,nil
end
-- 确认购买放进子线程并加超时，避免某次远程调用不返回导致整个流程卡死
local function confirmWithTimeout(storeName,timeout)
local confirmPurchase=_G.YQXSilentConfirm
if type(confirmPurchase)~="function"and type(silentConfirmPurchase)=="function"then confirmPurchase=silentConfirmPurchase end
if type(confirmPurchase)~="function"then
notify("购买确认函数未初始化: "..tostring(matName),"error",3)
return false
end
local moneyBefore=getMoney()
local done,okCall,bought=false,false,false
task.spawn(function()
okCall,bought=pcall(confirmPurchase,storeName,moneyBefore)
done=true
end)
local t0=os.clock()
while not done and os.clock()-t0<(timeout or 15)do
if evilHeartStopFlag then return false end
task.wait(0.1)
end
if done and okCall and bought then return true end
local m=getMoney()
return type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(m)=="number"and m<moneyBefore-0.5
end
local function resetChat(storeName)
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
if type(resetChatState)=="function"then resetChatState(storeName)end
end)
end)
end
for attempt=1,3 do
if evilHeartStopFlag then return nil end
local prod,storeName=findShopProduct()
if not prod then
local again=findOwnedFast()
if again then return again end
if attempt<3 then
task.wait(0.6)
else
notify("商店找不到: "..tostring(matName),"warn",3)
end
else
storeName=storeName or"WoodRUs"
notify("购买 "..tostring(matName).." @"..tostring(storeName),"info",2)
local counter=getStoreCounter(storeName)
if not counter or not teleportOneItem(prod,counter)then
notify("材料无法放到柜台: "..tostring(matName),"warn",3)
task.wait(0.4)
else
task.wait(0.15)
local hrp2=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(storeName)
resetChat(storeName)
task.wait(0.2)
local bought=confirmWithTimeout(storeName,15)
if not bought and prod.Parent and isOwnedByMe(prod)then bought=true end
if bought then
local item=nil
local deadline=os.clock()+5
while os.clock()<deadline do
if evilHeartStopFlag then return nil end
if prod.Parent and isOwnedByMe(prod)then
item=prod
break
end
item=findOwnedFast()
if item then break end
task.wait(0.15)
end
if not item then item=findEvilHeartOwnedMat(matName)end
if item then
return item
end
notify("购买后未找到材料: "..tostring(matName),"warn",3)
return nil
end
notify("购买失败，重试("..attempt.."/3): "..tostring(matName),"warn",2)
resetChat(storeName)
task.wait(0.5)
end
end
end
return nil
end

local function startEvilHeartCombine(basePos)
    local character = speaker.Character
    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        notify("无角色", "error", 2)
        return false
    end
    local originalPos = basePos or hrp.CFrame
    pcall(unlockPlayer)

    -- 记录合成前已有的 EvilHeart
    local beforeSet = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "EvilHeart" and isUnowned(obj) then
            beforeSet[obj] = true
        end
    end

    local ok = false
    for attempt = 1, 3 do
        if evilHeartStopFlag then
            safeTeleport(originalPos)
            return false
        end
        if attempt > 1 then
            notify("未出现新EvilHeart，重新拖拽（第 " .. attempt .. " 轮）", "warn", 3)
        end

        -- ① 获取材料（先找自己背包，没有就买）
        local mats = {}
        local allGot = true
        for i, matName in ipairs(evilHeartMaterials) do
            if evilHeartStopFlag then safeTeleport(originalPos); return false end
            notify("获取材料 " .. i .. "/3: " .. matName, "info", 2)
            local item = buyEvilHeartMat(matName)
            if not item then
                notify("无法获取材料: " .. matName, "error", 3)
                allGot = false
                break
            end
            mats[i] = item
        end
        if not allGot then
            safeTeleport(originalPos)
            return false
        end

        -- ② 把材料盒子传送到各自的合成坐标（和星星灯的 moveBox 一样用 teleportOneItem）
        for i = 1, 3 do
            if evilHeartStopFlag then safeTeleport(originalPos); return false end
            local item = mats[i]
            if item and item.Parent then
                teleportOneItem(item, evilHeartDragTargets[i])
                task.wait(0.4)
                notify(evilHeartMaterials[i] .. " 已放到 " .. i .. " 号位", "info", 2)
            end
        end

        -- ③ 逐个打开触发合成（人物悬停在材料上方，用 openItem 打开）
        for i = 1, 3 do
            if evilHeartStopFlag then safeTeleport(originalPos); return false end
            local item = mats[i]
            if not item or not item.Parent then
                for _, obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Model") and isOwnedByMe(obj) and obj.Name == evilHeartMaterials[i] then
                        item = obj; break
                    end
                end
            end
            if item and item.Parent then
                lockPlayerAt(CFrame.new(evilHeartDragTargets[i].Position + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)))
                task.wait(0.3)
                openItem(item)
                task.wait(0.4)
            end
        end
        pcall(unlockPlayer)

        -- ④ 等待 EvilHeart 出现（最多10秒）
        local newHeart = waitEvilHeartAppear(3, beforeSet)
        if newHeart then
            ok = true
            local sent = false
            for _ = 1, 2 do
                if newHeart.Parent then
                    sent = teleportOneItem(newHeart, originalPos) or sent
                    task.wait(0.4)
                end
            end
            if sent then
                notify("EvilHeart 已带回原位", "success", 3)
            end
            break
        end
    end

    if ok then
        notify("破碎之心合成完成！", "success", 3)
    else
        notify("破碎之心合成未完成", "warn", 3)
    end
    safeTeleport(originalPos)
    return ok
end

win=library:new("YQX-木材大亨","dark")

local m=getMoney()

moneyLabel.Text=t("lbl_money")..tostring(m)
else
moneyLabel.Text=t("lbl_money_unknown")
end

local playerFeatureSec=homeTab:section("玩家功能",true)
local noclipConn
local antiAfkConn
local selfGlowLight=nil
local function setNoclip(enabled)
if noclipConn then noclipConn:Disconnect();noclipConn=nil end
if enabled then
noclipConn=RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
local character=speaker.Character
if character then
for _,part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then part.CanCollide=false end
end
end
end)
else
local character=speaker.Character
if character then
for _,part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then part.CanCollide=true end
end
end
end
end
local function setAntiAfk(enabled)
if antiAfkConn then antiAfkConn:Disconnect();antiAfkConn=nil end
if enabled then
antiAfkConn=speaker.Idled:Connect(function()
pcall(function()
local camera=Workspace.CurrentCamera
VirtualUser:Button2Down(Vector2.new(0,0),camera and camera.CFrame or CFrame.new())
task.wait(0.1)
VirtualUser:Button2Up(Vector2.new(0,0),camera and camera.CFrame or CFrame.new())
end)
end)
end
end
local function currentHumanoidValue(property,fallback)
local character=speaker.Character
local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local value=humanoid and humanoid[property]
return type(value)=="number"and value or fallback
end
local speedOverride,jumpOverride,fovOverride=nil,nil,nil
local sliderInitializing=true
local initialWalkSpeed=currentHumanoidValue("WalkSpeed",16)
local initialJumpPower=currentHumanoidValue("JumpPower",50)
local initialFov=Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView or 70
playerFeatureSec:Slider("人物速度","playerSpeed",initialWalkSpeed,0,500,true,function(value)
if not sliderInitializing then speedOverride=tonumber(value)end
end)
playerFeatureSec:Slider("跳跃高度","playerJump",initialJumpPower,0,1000,true,function(value)
if not sliderInitializing then jumpOverride=tonumber(value)end
end)
playerFeatureSec:Slider("相机焦距","playerFov",initialFov,1,120,true,function(value)
if not sliderInitializing then fovOverride=tonumber(value)end
end)
sliderInitializing=false
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local character=speaker.Character
local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if humanoid and speedOverride~=nil then humanoid.WalkSpeed=speedOverride end
if humanoid and jumpOverride~=nil then humanoid.JumpPower=jumpOverride end
if Workspace.CurrentCamera and fovOverride~=nil then Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView=fovOverride end
end)
playerFeatureSec:Toggle("穿墙","playerNoclip",false,function(on)setNoclip(on)end)
playerFeatureSec:Toggle("防挂机","playerAntiAfk",false,function(on)setAntiAfk(on)end)
playerFeatureSec:Toggle("自身发光","playerGlow",false,function(on)
_G.SelfGlow=on
local character=speaker.Character
if on then
if character then
local hrp=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and not selfGlowLight then
selfGlowLight=Instance.new("PointLight")
selfGlowLight.Brightness=2
selfGlowLight.Range=500
selfGlowLight.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,240,200)
selfGlowLight.Parent=hrp
end
end
else
if selfGlowLight then
selfGlowLight:Destroy()
selfGlowLight=nil
end
end
end)
local playerEspOn=false
local playerEspFolder=Instance.new("Folder")
playerEspFolder.Name="PlayerMarkers"
playerEspFolder.Parent=Workspace
local playerEspMap={}
local function clearPlayerEsp()
for plr,data in pairs(playerEspMap)do
pcall(function()if data.bb then data.bb:Destroy()end end)
playerEspMap[plr]=nil
end
for _,ch in ipairs(playerEspFolder:GetChildren())do
pcall(function()ch:Destroy()end)
end
end
local function makePlayerEsp(plr)
if plr==speaker or playerEspMap[plr]then return end
local char=plr.Character
if not char then return end
local head=char:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not head then return end
local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
bb.Name="Marker_"..plr.Name
bb.Adornee=head
bb.Size=UDim2.new(0,90,0,70)
bb.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,2.8,0)
bb.AlwaysOnTop=true
bb.MaxDistance=2000
bb.Parent=playerEspFolder
local frame=Instance.new("Frame")
frame.Parent=bb
frame.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
frame.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(30,25,35)
frame.BackgroundTransparency=0.35
frame.BorderSizePixel=0
Instance.new("UICorner",frame).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
local img=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
img.Parent=frame
img.Size=UDim2.new(0,28,0,28)
img.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-14,0,4)
img.BackgroundTransparency=1
img.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit
task.spawn(function()
local ok,content=pcall(function()
return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(plr.UserId,Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48)
end)
if ok and img and img.Parent then img.Image=content end
end)
local nameLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
nameLbl.Parent=frame
nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1
nameLbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,2,0,32)
nameLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,14)
nameLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
nameLbl.TextSize=10
nameLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,230,245)
nameLbl.Text=plr.Name
nameLbl.TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
local distLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
distLbl.Parent=frame
distLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1
distLbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,2,0,46)
distLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-4,0,14)
distLbl.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
distLbl.TextSize=10
distLbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(180,220,255)
distLbl.Text="..."
playerEspMap[plr]={bb=bb,dist=distLbl,img=img,thumbLoading=false}
end
local function refreshPlayerEsp()
if not playerEspOn then return end
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local myPos=hrp and hrp.Position
for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do
if plr~=speaker then
if not playerEspMap[plr]or not playerEspMap[plr].bb or not playerEspMap[plr].bb.Parent then
playerEspMap[plr]=nil
makePlayerEsp(plr)
else
local head=plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
if head and playerEspMap[plr].bb.Adornee~=head then playerEspMap[plr].bb.Adornee=head end
end
local data=playerEspMap[plr]
if data and data.dist and myPos then
local ohrp=plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
data.dist.Text=ohrp and string.format("%.0fm",(ohrp.Position-myPos).Magnitude)or"..."
end
end
end
for plr,data in pairs(playerEspMap)do
if not plr.Parent then
pcall(function()if data.bb then data.bb:Destroy()end end)
playerEspMap[plr]=nil
end
end
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
local data=playerEspMap[plr]
if data then pcall(function()if data.bb then data.bb:Destroy()end end);playerEspMap[plr]=nil end
end)
playerFeatureSec:Toggle("玩家透视","playerESP",false,function(on)
playerEspOn=on
if on then
for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())do makePlayerEsp(plr)end
notify("玩家透视已开","success")
else
clearPlayerEsp()
notify("玩家透视已关","info")
end
end)
task.spawn(function()
while main and main.Parent do
if playerEspOn then pcall(refreshPlayerEsp)end
task.wait(0.2)
end
end)
speaker.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
if _G.SelfGlow then
local character=speaker.Character
local hrp=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and not selfGlowLight then
selfGlowLight=Instance.new("PointLight")
selfGlowLight.Brightness=2
selfGlowLight.Range=500
selfGlowLight.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,240,200)
selfGlowLight.Parent=hrp
end
end
if playerEspOn then refreshPlayerEsp()end
end)

local mjOn=false

local mjCount=0
playerFeatureSec:Toggle("多段跳 / 无限跳","yqxMultiJump",false,function(on)
mjOn=on
mjCount=0
if on then
notify("多段跳已开（上限 "..(mjMax>0 and tostring(mjMax)or"无限").."）","success",3)
end
end)
playerFeatureSec:Slider("跳数上限（0=无限）","yqxMultiJumpMax",2,0,10,false,function(v)
mjMax=math.floor(tonumber(v)or 2)
if mjMax<0 then mjMax=0 end
end)
RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if mjCount~=0 then
local char=speaker.Character
local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum or hum.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then mjCount=0 end
end
end)
game:GetService("UserInputService").JumpRequest:Connect(function()
if not mjOn then return end
local char=speaker.Character
if not char then return end
local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hum or not hrp then return end
if hum.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then return end
mjCount=mjCount+1
if mjMax>0 and mjCount>mjMax then return end
pcall(function()hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)end)
pcall(function()
local v=hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(v.X,hum.JumpPower,v.Z)
end)
end)

-- ==================== 性能模式 ====================
local perfSec=homeTab:section("性能优化",true)
local perfSaved=nil
local function perfCollect()
local saved={lighting={},fx={},deco={},quality=nil,scanned=0}
pcall(function()
local s=settings and settings()
if s and s.Rendering then saved.quality=s.Rendering.QualityLevel end
end)
for _,p in ipairs({"GlobalShadows","FogEnd","FogStart","Brightness","ShadowSoftness","EnvironmentDiffuseScale"})do
local ok,v=pcall(function()return Lighting[p]end)
if ok and v~=nil then saved.lighting[p]=v end
end
for _,ch in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren())do
if ch:IsA("PostEffect")then
table.insert(saved.fx,{obj=ch,enabled=ch.Enabled})
end
end
for _,d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants())do
saved.scanned=saved.scanned+1
if saved.scanned>120000 then break end
if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter")or d:IsA("Trail")or d:IsA("Beam")or d:IsA("Smoke")or d:IsA("Fire")or d:IsA("Sparkles")then
table.insert(saved.fx,{obj=d,enabled=d.Enabled})
elseif d:IsA("Decal")or d:IsA("Texture")then
table.insert(saved.deco,{obj=d,tr=d.Transparency})
end
end
return saved
end
local function perfApply(on)
if on then
if perfSaved then return end
perfSaved=perfCollect()
pcall(function()
local s=settings and settings()
if s and s.Rendering then s.Rendering.QualityLevel=Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end
end)
pcall(function()Lighting.GlobalShadows=false end)
pcall(function()Lighting.FogEnd=100000;Lighting.FogStart=0 end)
for _,e in ipairs(perfSaved.fx)do pcall(function()e.obj.Enabled=false end)end
for _,e in ipairs(perfSaved.deco)do pcall(function()e.obj.Transparency=1 end)end
notify(string.format("性能模式已开：关掉 %d 个特效 / %d 张贴图",#perfSaved.fx,#perfSaved.deco),"success",3)
else
if not perfSaved then return end
local saved=perfSaved
perfSaved=nil
pcall(function()
local s=settings and settings()
if s and s.Rendering and saved.quality~=nil then s.Rendering.QualityLevel=saved.quality end
end)
for p,v in pairs(saved.lighting)do pcall(function()Lighting[p]=v end)end
for _,e in ipairs(saved.fx)do pcall(function()e.obj.Enabled=e.enabled end)end
for _,e in ipairs(saved.deco)do pcall(function()e.obj.Transparency=e.tr end)end
notify("性能模式已关，已还原原始效果","info",3)
end
end
perfSec:Toggle("性能模式（低配 / 挂机用）","yqxPerfMode",false,function(on)
task.spawn(function()
local ok,err=pcall(perfApply,on)
if not ok then notify("性能模式出错: "..tostring(err),"error",4)end
end)
end)
perfSec:Button("手动清理一次特效（不改画质）",function()
task.spawn(function()
local n=0
for _,d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter")or d:IsA("Trail")or d:IsA("Beam")or d:IsA("Smoke")or d:IsA("Fire")or d:IsA("Sparkles")then
pcall(function()d.Enabled=false end)
n=n+1
if n>=20000 then break end
end
end
notify("已清理 "..n.." 个特效","success",3)
end)
end)
perfSec:Label("开启后关闭粒子/贴图/后效，并把画质压到最低；关掉自动还原。低端机和挂机建议长开。")

function findMyPropertyPosition()
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")or obj:IsA("Folder")then
local nameLower=string.lower(obj.Name)
if string.find(nameLower,"property")or string.find(nameLower,"base")or string.find(nameLower,"plot")then
local owner=obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner then
local isMine=false
if owner:IsA("ObjectValue")and owner.Value==speaker then
isMine=true
elseif owner:IsA("StringValue")and owner.Value==speaker.Name then
isMine=true
end
if isMine then
local part=obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
if part then
return part.Position+Vector3.new(0,5,0)
end
local ok,pivot=pcall(function()return obj:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok and pivot then
return pivot+Vector3.new(0,5,0)
end
end
end
end
end
end
local properties=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Properties")
if properties then
for _,prop in ipairs(properties:GetChildren())do
local owner=prop:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner then
local isMine=(owner:IsA("ObjectValue")and owner.Value==speaker)
or(owner:IsA("StringValue")and owner.Value==speaker.Name)
if isMine then
local part=prop:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
if part then return part.Position+Vector3.new(0,5,0)end
end
end
end
end
return Vector3.new(188.8,2.3,56.6)
end

local itemTpSec=tpTab:section(YQXtr("传送物品 (选择模式)"),true)
do
local function YQXItemTeleportMain()
local lp=speaker
local ws=workspace
local rep=ReplicatedStorage
local mouse=Mouse
local bai={}
local StoresFolder=workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
local notify=function(title,desc,dur)YQXnotifyTr(desc,dur)end
local selectionCountLabel
local SectionTeleport=YQXsectionProxy(itemTpSec)
 teleportTargetCF=nil
 teleportTargetPlayer=lp.Name
 teleportMarker=nil

 selectingMode=false
 selectedItems={}
local selectionBoxes={}
 selectionConn=nil

local function setTeleportPoint()
if teleportMarker then pcall(function()teleportMarker:Destroy()end);teleportMarker=nil end
local hrp=lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then notify("","无法获取玩家位置",3)return end
teleportTargetCF=hrp.CFrame
local marker=Instance.new("Part")
marker.Name="baiBasedropCord"
marker.Anchored=true
marker.Parent=workspace
marker.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
marker.Size=Vector3.new(2,2,2)
marker.Color=Color3.fromRGB(0,217,255)
marker.Material=Enum.Material.ForceField
marker.CFrame=teleportTargetCF
teleportMarker=marker
notify("","传送点已设置（球体标记）",3)
end

local function clearTeleportPoint()
if teleportMarker then pcall(function()teleportMarker:Destroy()end);teleportMarker=nil end
teleportTargetCF=nil
notify("","🗑️ 传送点已清除",3)
end

local function isSelectable(model)
if not model or not model:IsA("Model")then return false end

if model:FindFirstChild("BlueprintWoodClass")then
return false
end

if model:FindFirstChild("ButtonRemote_SpawnButton")then
return false
end

local name=model.Name or""
local hasPurchasedBoxItem=model:FindFirstChild("PurchasedBoxItemName")
local hasBoxItem=model:FindFirstChild("BoxItemName")
local hasType=model:FindFirstChild("Type")

if name:find("Crate")or name:find("Box")or name:find("Gift")then

local hasValidItem=false

if hasPurchasedBoxItem then
local val=hasPurchasedBoxItem.Value
if val and val~=""and val~=" "then
hasValidItem=true
end
end

if not hasValidItem and hasBoxItem then
local val=hasBoxItem.Value
if val and val~=""and val~=" "then
hasValidItem=true
end
end

if not hasValidItem and hasType then
local val=hasType.Value
if val and val~=""and val~=" "then
hasValidItem=true
end
end

if not hasValidItem then
return false
end
end

local targetPlayer=game.Players:FindFirstChild(teleportTargetPlayer)
if not targetPlayer then return false end

local owner=model:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner and owner.Value==targetPlayer then
return true
end

if workspace.LogModels:FindFirstChild(model.Name)==model then
if owner and owner.Value==targetPlayer then
return true
end
end

return false
end

local function highlightItem(model)
for _,e in ipairs(selectionBoxes)do
if e.model==model then return end
end
local box=Instance.new("SelectionBox")
box.Adornee=model
box.Color3=Color3.fromRGB(0,100,255)
box.LineThickness=0.05
box.Parent=model
table.insert(selectionBoxes,{model=model,box=box})
end

local function unhighlightItem(model)
for i,e in ipairs(selectionBoxes)do
if e.model==model then
pcall(function()e.box:Destroy()end)
table.remove(selectionBoxes,i)
break
end
end
end

local function clearSelection()
for model,_ in pairs(selectedItems)do
unhighlightItem(model)
end
selectedItems={}
if selectionCountLabel then
selectionCountLabel:SetText("已选中: 0")
end
end

local function selectItem(model)
if not isSelectable(model)then
notify("","该物品不可选择或不属于所选玩家",2)
return
end
if selectedItems[model]then
unhighlightItem(model)
selectedItems[model]=nil
notify("","已取消选中: "..(model.Name or"物品"),2)
if selectionCountLabel then
local count=0
for _ in pairs(selectedItems)do count=count+1 end
selectionCountLabel:SetText("已选中: "..count)
end
return
end
selectedItems[model]=true
highlightItem(model)
notify("","已选中: "..(model.Name or"物品"),2)
if selectionCountLabel then
local count=0
for _ in pairs(selectedItems)do count=count+1 end
selectionCountLabel:SetText("已选中: "..count)
end
end

local function selectSameNameItems(sourceModel)
local firstModel=sourceModel or next(selectedItems)
if not firstModel then
notify("","请先至少选中一个物品",3)
return
end
local targetName=firstModel.Name
if not targetName then
notify("","无法获取物品名称",3)
return
end

local count=0

if sourceModel and not selectedItems[sourceModel]then
selectedItems[sourceModel]=true
highlightItem(sourceModel)
count=count+1
end

for _,model in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetChildren())do
if model:IsA("Model")and isSelectable(model)then
if model.Name==targetName and not selectedItems[model]then
selectedItems[model]=true
highlightItem(model)
count=count+1
end
end
end

for _,log in pairs(workspace.LogModels:GetChildren())do
if isSelectable(log)then
if log.Name==targetName and not selectedItems[log]then
selectedItems[log]=true
highlightItem(log)
count=count+1
end
end
end

if count>0 then
notify("",string.format("已添加 %d 个同名物品",count),3)
else
notify("","没有找到其他同名物品",3)
end
if selectionCountLabel then
local total=0
for _ in pairs(selectedItems)do total=total+1 end
selectionCountLabel:SetText("已选中: "..total)
end
end

local function teleportSelectedItems()
if not teleportTargetCF then
pcall(function()notify(t("n_tp_point_first"),"warn")end)
return
end
if not next(selectedItems)then
pcall(function()notify(t("n_select_mode_first"),"warn")end)
return
end
selectMode=false
local character=speaker.Character
local hrp=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then
pcall(function()notify(t("n_no_char_short"),"error")end)
return
end
local originalCF=hrp.CFrame
local dragRemote=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
and ReplicatedStorage.Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientIsDragging")
if not dragRemote then
pcall(function()notify(t("n_no_client_dragging"),"error")end)
return
end
pcall(function()notify(t("n_teleporting_x")..(function()local n=0 for _ in pairs(selectedItems)do n=n+1 end return n end)(),"info")end)
local moved=0
local _items={}
for _k,_v in pairs(selectedItems)do table.insert(_items,_k)end
for idx=#_items,1,-1 do
local item=_items[idx]
if item and item.Parent then
if item:IsA("BasePart")and item.Parent:IsA("Model")then
item=item.Parent
end
local okItem=false
pcall(function()
if not item:IsA("Model")then error("not a model")end
dragToPosition(item,teleportTargetCF)
okItem=true
end)
if okItem then
moved=moved+1
else
pcall(function()notify(t("n_failed_prefix")..tostring(item.Name),"warn")end)
end
unhighlightItem(item)
selectedItems[item]=nil
task.wait(0.08)
else
selectedItems[item]=nil
end
end
clearSelection()
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then hrp.CFrame=originalCF end
pcall(function()notify(t("n_tp_complete")..moved..t("n_tp_complete_items"),moved>0 and"success"or"warn")end)
end


 teleportPlayerDropdown=nil

local function isItem(m)
return m and m:IsA("Model")and(m:FindFirstChild("Owner")~=nil or m:FindFirstChild("Type")~=nil)
end

local function groupKey(m)
local owner=m:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if owner and owner.Value~=nil then
return m.Name,owner.Value
end
local t=m:FindFirstChild("Type")
return m.Name,(t and t.Value)or nil
end

local function scanSameName(sourceModel)
if not sourceModel then return 0 end
local tn,to=groupKey(sourceModel)
if not tn then return 0 end
local count=0
local function trySel(m)
if isItem(m)then
local n,o=groupKey(m)
if n==tn and o==to and not selectedItems[m]then
selectedItems[m]=true
highlightItem(m)
count=count+1
end
end
end
trySel(sourceModel)
for _,m in pairs(workspace.PlayerModels:GetDescendants())do trySel(m)end
for _,m in pairs(workspace.LogModels:GetDescendants())do trySel(m)end
pcall(function()
for _,store in pairs(StoresFolder:GetChildren())do
local si=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if si then
for _,m in pairs(si:GetDescendants())do trySel(m)end
end
end
end)
return count
end

local function cancelSameName(sourceModel)
local tn,to=groupKey(sourceModel)
local n=0
for m,_ in pairs(selectedItems)do
local mn,mo=groupKey(m)
if mn==tn and mo==to then
unhighlightItem(m)
selectedItems[m]=nil
n=n+1
end
end
return n
end
