-- ==================== 坐标传送 / 鼠标传送 ====================
local coordSec=tpTab:section("坐标传送",true)
local coordInput="0,0,0"
local coordLift=3
local rightTpConn=nil
local function parseCoord(text)
if type(text)~="string"then return nil end
local x,y,z=text:match("([%-%d%.]+)%s*[,%s]%s*([%-%d%.]+)%s*[,%s]%s*([%-%d%.]+)")
if not x then return nil end
x,y,z=tonumber(x),tonumber(y),tonumber(z)
if x and y and z then return Vector3.new(x,y,z)end
return nil
end
local function tpToPos(pos,label)
if not pos then
notify("坐标无效","warn",3)
return
end
local char=speaker.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then
notify("角色未加载","warn",3)
return
end
local target=pos+Vector3.new(0,coordLift,0)
local ok=pcall(safeTeleport,target)
if not ok then
pcall(function()hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(target)end)
end
notify(string.format("已传送 → %s (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)",label or"坐标",pos.X,pos.Y,pos.Z),"success",3)
end
local coordBox=coordSec:Textbox("坐标 X,Y,Z（例 188.8,2.3,56.6）","yqxTpCoord","0,0,0",function(text)
coordInput=text
end)
coordSec:Slider("落点抬高（防卡地）","yqxTpLift",3,0,20,false,function(v)
coordLift=tonumber(v)or 3
end)
coordSec:Button("传送到输入坐标",function()
local pos=parseCoord(coordInput)
if not pos then
notify("格式不对，示例：188.8,2.3,56.6","warn",4)
return
end
tpToPos(pos,"输入坐标")
end)
coordSec:Button("填入当前坐标",function()
local char=speaker.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then notify("角色未加载","warn",3)return end
local p=hrp.Position
local str=string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f",p.X,p.Y,p.Z)
coordInput=str
if coordBox then coordBox:SetValue(str)end
notify("已填入 "..str,"info",3)
end)
coordSec:Button("复制当前坐标",function()
local char=speaker.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then notify("角色未加载","warn",3)return end
local p=hrp.Position
local str=string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f",p.X,p.Y,p.Z)
local okc=false
pcall(function()if setclipboard then setclipboard(str)okc=true end end)
if not okc then pcall(function()if toclipboard then toclipboard(str)okc=true end end)end
notify(okc and("已复制 "..str)or"剪贴板不可用","info",3)
end)
coordSec:Button("传送到鼠标指向位置",function()
local hit=Mouse.Hit
if not hit then
notify("鼠标没指到东西","warn",3)
return
end
tpToPos(hit.Position,"鼠标位置")
end)
coordSec:Toggle("鼠标右键快速传送","yqxTpMouseRight",false,function(on)
if rightTpConn then rightTpConn:Disconnect();rightTpConn=nil end
if not on then return end
rightTpConn=game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(input,gp)
if gp then return end
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
local hit=Mouse.Hit
if hit then tpToPos(hit.Position,"右键位置")end
end
end)
end)

end
do
local function YQXSellMain()
local lp=speaker
local ws=workspace
local rep=ReplicatedStorage
local mouse=Mouse
local bai={}
local StoresFolder=workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
local notify=function(title,desc,dur)YQXnotifyTr(desc,dur)end
local SELL_CF=CFrame.new(314.508514,-0.303692758,86.5952911,0.999575555,-2.51872869e-08,
-0.0291328784,2.55842831e-08,1,1.32543034e-08,0.0291328784,-1.39940219e-08,0.999575555)

local sellStopFlag=false

-- 是不是我自己的原木(地上散落的 Loose_ 木头)
local function isMyLog(m)
if not m or not m:IsA("Model")then return false end
local owner=m:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if not owner or owner.Value~=lp then return false end
local tc=m:FindFirstChild("TreeClass")
return tc~=nil
end

-- 是不是我自己的木板。这个 mod 没有 PlankModels 文件夹, 木板是购买后生成在
-- PlayerModels 里的普通物品, 所以按 Type/名字判断, 不写死路径。
local function isMyPlank(m)
if not m or not m:IsA("Model")then return false end
local owner=m:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if not owner or owner.Value~=lp then return false end
local ty=m:FindFirstChild("Type")
if ty and string.lower(tostring(ty.Value))=="plank"then return true end
local pn=m:FindFirstChild("PurchasedBoxItemName")or m:FindFirstChild("ItemName")
if pn and string.find(string.lower(tostring(pn.Value)),"plank")then return true end
return string.find(string.lower(m.Name),"plank")~=nil
end

-- 收集要卖的模型
local function collectSellTargets(kind)
local list={}
local seen={}
local function push(m)
if m and not seen[m]then seen[m]=true table.insert(list,m)end
end
if kind=="wood"then
local lm=ws:FindFirstChild("LogModels")
if lm then for _,m in pairs(lm:GetChildren())do if isMyLog(m)then push(m)end end end
else
local pm=ws:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if pm then for _,m in pairs(pm:GetChildren())do if isMyPlank(m)then push(m)end end end
end
return list
end

-- 把一个模型拖到卖点并等它被卖掉
local function sellOne(m)
local primary=m.PrimaryPart or m:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")or m:FindFirstChild("Main")
or m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
if not primary then return false end
if not m.PrimaryPart then m.PrimaryPart=primary end

-- 1) 锁在物品上方, 而且要"一直锁"而不是传送一下就完事。
--    safeTeleport 是一次性的, 人一走动就丢掉网络所有权, 服务器不再跟随,
--    模型就再也拖不回来。lockPlayerAt 用 Heartbeat 每帧把人钉回原位, 全程不能解锁。
lockPlayerAt(primary.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
task.wait(0.2)
if sellStopFlag then unlockPlayer() return false end

-- 2) 保持锁定的前提下开始拖。
--    waitFinish 必须传 false: 传 true 时 dragToPosition 会在发出 "End" 之后
--    再额外做 3 轮 Refresh + SetPrimaryPartCFrame, 视觉上就是"拖了两次",
--    而且那些 Refresh 发生在 End 之后, 服务端可能当成又开了一次新拖拽。
--    不传时主循环本身已经把模型稳定送到落点(第 28 帧起持续 Set)。
local target=SELL_CF+Vector3.new(0,1.5,0)
dragToPosition(m,target,nil,false)

-- 3) dragToPosition 结束时会自己解锁, 这里把锁挪到卖点:
--    成交要在卖点附近才判定, 和购买流程"拖完锁到柜台"是同一个道理
lockPlayerAt(SELL_CF+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
task.wait(1)
local deadline=os.clock()+2
repeat
if not m.Parent then return true end
task.wait(0.1)
until os.clock()>=deadline
unlockPlayer()
return m.Parent==nil
end

local function sellKind(kind,label)
if sellStopFlag then return 0 end
local list=collectSellTargets(kind)
if #list==0 then notify("雪糕","没有可卖的"..label,3) return 0 end
notify("雪糕","开始卖"..label.." 共"..#list.."个",3)

-- 记下原来的位置, 卖完送回去(和"传送物品"一样)
local hrp=lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originalPos=hrp and hrp.CFrame

local sold=0
for i,m in ipairs(list)do
if sellStopFlag then break end
if m.Parent then
-- pcall 的第一个返回值是"有没有报错", 不是 sellOne 的返回值。
-- 原来直接 if ok then 当成卖出成功, 等于把"报错"算成"卖掉了", 计数一直是错的。
local callOk,success=pcall(sellOne,m)
if callOk and success then sold=sold+1 end
end
task.wait(0.1)
end

unlockPlayer()
if originalPos and lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")then
pcall(function()safeTeleport(originalPos)end)
end
notify("雪糕","卖出"..label.." "..sold.."/"..#list,3)
return sold
end

function sellAllWood() sellStopFlag=false return sellKind("wood","木头")end
function sellAllPlanks() sellStopFlag=false return sellKind("plank","木板")end
function sellWoodAndPlanks() sellStopFlag=false
sellKind("wood","木头")
sellKind("plank","木板")
end
function stopSelling() sellStopFlag=true unlockPlayer()end

-- 3829 行那个按钮引用的函数全文没有定义, 点一下就报错, 这里补上
function bringAllMyLogsToFeet()
local list=collectSellTargets("wood")
if #list==0 then notify("雪糕","你身上没有木头",3) return end
local hrp=lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originalPos=hrp and hrp.CFrame
notify("雪糕","正在把"..#list.."根木头拉过来",3)
for _,m in ipairs(list)do
local p=m.PrimaryPart or m:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
if p then
-- 同样必须全程锁定, 人一跑掉就拿不到网络所有权, 木头拖不动回来
lockPlayerAt(p.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
task.wait(0.15)
dragToPosition(m,(originalPos or p.CFrame)+Vector3.new(0,2,0),nil,false)
end
task.wait(0.05)
end
unlockPlayer()
if originalPos and lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")then
pcall(function()safeTeleport(originalPos)end)
end
notify("雪糕","已拉过来",3)
end
end
YQXSellMain()
end
do
local woodTab=win:Tab(t("tab_wood"),ASSETS.tab.wood)
local treeSec=woodTab:section(t("sec_tree"),true)
if not scannedTreeClasses or#scannedTreeClasses==0 then
scannedTreeClasses={"Generic"}
end
local treeDisplayToRaw={}
local function getTreeDisplayOptions()
local options={}
for _,rawName in ipairs(scannedTreeClasses)do
local displayName=treeDisplayName(rawName)
if treeDisplayToRaw[displayName]and treeDisplayToRaw[displayName]~=rawName then
displayName=tostring(displayName).." ("..tostring(rawName)..")"
end
treeDisplayToRaw[displayName]=rawName
table.insert(options,displayName)
end
return options
end
local selectedTreeClasses={}
local treeDropdown=treeSec:Dropdown(t("dd_select_tree"),"treeClass",getTreeDisplayOptions(),function(selectedList)
local raws={}
local names={}
for _,disp in ipairs(selectedList)do
local raw=treeDisplayToRaw[disp]or disp
table.insert(raws,raw)
table.insert(names,treeDisplayName(raw))
end
selectedTreeClasses=raws
if#raws>0 then
selectedTreeClass=raws[1]
treeClassIndex=1
for i,v in ipairs(scannedTreeClasses)do
if v==raws[1]then treeClassIndex=i break end
end
notify(t("n_tree_info")..table.concat(names,", "),"info")
end
end,true)
treeDropdown:SetRefresh(function()
pcall(scanTreeClasses)
if not scannedTreeClasses or#scannedTreeClasses==0 then
scannedTreeClasses={"Generic"}
end
if#selectedTreeClasses>0 then selectedTreeClass=selectedTreeClasses[1]end
return getTreeDisplayOptions()
end)

return
end
local Model=Target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")or Target
local function isSawmill(m)
local itemName=m:GetAttribute("ItemName")
if itemName and type(itemName)=="string"and itemName:lower():find("saw")then return true end
for _,d in pairs(m:GetDescendants())do
if d.Name=="ItemName"then
local v=d.Value
if v and type(v)=="string"and v:lower():find("saw")then return true end
end
end
if m.Name and m.Name:lower():find("saw")then return true end
local typeChild=m:FindFirstChild("Type")
if typeChild and typeChild.Value=="Sawmill"then return true end
return false
end
if not isSawmill(Model)then
notify(t("n_not_sawmill"),"warn")
return
end
local conveyorPart=nil
local partType=nil
for _,d in pairs(Model:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("BasePart")and d.Name=="Conveyor"then
conveyorPart=d
partType="Conveyor"
break
end
end
if not conveyorPart then
for _,d in pairs(Model:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("BasePart")and d.Name=="Main"then
conveyorPart=d
partType="Main"
break
end
end
end
if not conveyorPart then
notify(t("n_no_conveyor"),"warn")
return
end
bai.cachedSawmill=Model
bai.cachedSawmillPos=conveyorPart.CFrame
sawmillBtnLabel.Text=t("lbl_locked")..Model.Name
notify(t("n_sawmill_memorized")..partType..")","success")
if bai.sawmillSelectConn then
bai.sawmillSelectConn:Disconnect()
bai.sawmillSelectConn=nil
end
end)
if not ok then
notify("Sawmill select error: "..tostring(err),"warn")
end
end)
end)

local toolsSec=woodTab:section(t("sec_tools"),true)
local function YQXDragMain()
local lp=speaker
local ws=workspace
local rep=ReplicatedStorage
local mouse=Mouse
local bai={}
local StoresFolder=workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
local notify=function(title,desc,dur)YQXnotifyTr(desc,dur)end
local Sectiontuozhuai=YQXsectionProxy(woodTab:section(YQXtr("拖拽"),true))
local defaultDragAttempts=50
local defaultDragInterval=0.01
 dragAttempts=defaultDragAttempts
 dragInterval=defaultDragInterval

local CONFIG_FILE="拖拽配置文件.txt"
local function loadDragConfig()
if writefile then
local ok,data=pcall(readfile,CONFIG_FILE)
if ok and data then
local att,inter=data:match("(%d+):([%d.]+)")
if att and inter then
dragAttempts=tonumber(att)or defaultDragAttempts
dragInterval=tonumber(inter)or defaultDragInterval
end
end
end
end
loadDragConfig()
local function saveDragConfig()
if writefile then
local content=tostring(dragAttempts)..":"..tostring(dragInterval)
local ok,err=pcall(writefile,CONFIG_FILE,content)
if ok then notify("","配置已保存",2)else notify("","保存失败: "..tostring(err),3)end
else
notify("","当前环境不支持保存配置",3)
end
end
 dragEnhanceConnection=nil
local function setDragEnhance(state)
if dragEnhanceConnection then dragEnhanceConnection:Disconnect()end
dragEnhanceConnection=workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(Dragger)
if tostring(Dragger)=="Dragger"then
local BodyGyro=Dragger:WaitForChild("BodyGyro")
local BodyPosition=Dragger:WaitForChild("BodyPosition")
repeat task.wait()until workspace:FindFirstChild("Dragger")
if state then
BodyPosition.P=120000
BodyPosition.D=1000
BodyPosition.maxForce=Vector3.new(1,1,1)*1000000
BodyGyro.maxTorque=Vector3.new(1,1,1)*200
BodyGyro.P=1200
BodyGyro.D=140
else
BodyPosition.P=10000
BodyPosition.D=800
BodyPosition.maxForce=Vector3.new(17000,17000,17000)
BodyGyro.maxTorque=Vector3.new(200,200,200)
BodyGyro.P=1200
BodyGyro.D=140
end
end
end)
end
setDragEnhance(false)
Sectiontuozhuai:Toggle("拖拽器增强",'Toggleflag',false,function(state)
setDragEnhance(state)
end)
Sectiontuozhuai:Textbox("次数",'TextBoxfalg',"5",function(v)
local num=tonumber(v);if num and num>0 then dragAttempts=num else notify("","请输入大于0的数字",2)end
end)
Sectiontuozhuai:Textbox("间隔",'TextBoxfalg',"0.04",function(v)
local num=tonumber(v);if num and num>0 then dragInterval=num else notify("","请输入大于0的数字",2)end
end)
Sectiontuozhuai:Button("保存配置",function()
saveDragConfig()
end)
Sectiontuozhuai:Button("删除配置",function()
if writefile then
local success,err=pcall(function()

if isfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE)then
delfile(CONFIG_FILE)
end
end)
if success then

dragAttempts=defaultDragAttempts
dragInterval=defaultDragInterval
notify("","配置已删除，已恢复默认值 (次数="..defaultDragAttempts..", 间隔="..defaultDragInterval..")",3)
else
notify("","删除失败: "..tostring(err),3)
end
else
notify("","当前环境不支持删除配置文件",3)
end
end)
end
YQXDragMain()
toolsSec:Button(t("btn_sawmill_max"),function()
local connection,sawmillModel
notify(t("n_click_sawmill"),"info")
connection=Mouse.Button1Down:Connect(function(b)

if target then
local sawmill=target.Parent
if sawmill.Name:find('Sawmill')then

notify(t("n_sawmill_selected"),"success")

end
end)

if connection then connection:Disconnect();connection=nil end
spawn(function()
for i=1,50 do
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.RemoteProxy:FireServer(sawmillModel:FindFirstChild('ButtonRemote_XUp'))

end
end)
end)
end
do
npcIdCache=npcIdCache or{}
npcIdConfirmed=npcIdConfirmed or{}
npcCtxCache=npcCtxCache or{}
local STORE_CN={
SeaSide="Seaside Shop",
HLStand="HL Stand",
MountainSide="Mountain Shop",
BlackMarket="Black Market",
FurnitureStore="Furniture Store",
CarStore="Car Store",
FineFinds="Fine Finds",
TravelingTrader="Traveling Trader",
SaplingCart="Sapling Cart",
PlanterStore="Planter Store",
WoodRUs="Wood Shop",
SallysSeasonal="Sally's Seasonal",
StoneRUs="Stone Shop",
Igloo="Igloo",
PlantomicsChoice="Plantomics Choice",
VIPSHOP="VIP Shop",
LandStore="Land Store",
LogicStore="Logic Store",
FineArt="Fine Art",
MusicStore="Music Store",
AutumnCatalog="Autumn Catalog",
}
for key,value in pairs(STORE_TRANSLATIONS)do
if STORE_CN[key]then STORE_CN[key]=value end
end
local function storeDisplayName(name)
local cn=STORE_TRANSLATIONS[name]or STORE_CN[name]
if cn then
return string.format("%s (%s)",cn,name)
end
return tostring(name)
end
pcall(function()
local npcDlg=ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("NPCDialog",10)
if not npcDlg then return end
local prompt=npcDlg:FindFirstChild("PromptChat")
if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("RemoteEvent")then return end
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
local function handleEntry(data)
if type(data)~="table"then return end
if type(data.ID)~="number"then return end
if not data.Character then return end
pcall(function()
if not stores then return end
local store=data.Character.Parent
if not store then return end
if store.Parent~=stores then return end
local name=store.Name
if npcIdConfirmed[name]and npcIdCache[name]then return end
npcIdCache[name]=data.ID
npcIdConfirmed[name]=true
npcCtxCache[name]={
ID=data.ID,
Character=data.Character,
Name=data.Name or data.Character.Name,
Dialog=data.Dialog,
}
end)
end
prompt.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
local args={...}
for _,v in ipairs(args)do
if type(v)=="table"then
if type(v.ID)=="number"then
handleEntry(v)
else
for _,entry in ipairs(v)do
handleEntry(entry)
end
end
end
end
end)
end)
teleportOneItem=function(item,targetPos)
local character=speaker.Character
local hrp=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hrp or not item or not item.Parent then return false end
if targetPos==nil then return false end
if item:IsA("BasePart")and item.Parent:IsA("Model")then
item=item.Parent
end
if not(item:IsA("Model")or item:IsA("BasePart"))then
return false
end
local dragRemote=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
and ReplicatedStorage.Interaction:FindFirstChild("ClientIsDragging")
if not dragRemote then return false end
local targetCF
if typeof(targetPos)=="CFrame"then
targetCF=targetPos
else
local tp=typeof(targetPos)=="Vector3"and targetPos or Vector3.new(targetPos.X,targetPos.Y,targetPos.Z)
targetCF=CFrame.new(tp.X,tp.Y,tp.Z)
end
local function zeroVel()
pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
end)
pcall(function()
if hum then
hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
end
end)
end
local pivot=item:IsA("Model")and item:GetPivot()or item.CFrame
local primary=item
if item:IsA("Model")then
primary=item.PrimaryPart or item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
end
if primary then
lockPlayerAt(primary.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
else
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(pivot.Position+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
end
zeroVel()
task.wait(0.06)
for _=1,20 do
if not item.Parent then break end
if item:IsA("Model")then
pcall(function()item:PivotTo(targetCF)end)
else
pcall(function()item.CFrame=targetCF end)
end
local _dragID=getNextDragID()
pcall(function()dragRemote:FireServer("Begin",item,_dragID)end)
pcall(function()dragRemote:FireServer("Refresh",item,_dragID)end)
pcall(function()dragRemote:FireServer("End",item,_dragID)end)
zeroVel()
task.wait(0.005)
end
if item.Parent then
if item:IsA("Model")then
pcall(function()item:PivotTo(targetCF)end)
else
pcall(function()item.CFrame=targetCF end)
end
end
zeroVel()
pcall(unlockPlayer)
return true
end
local autoBuyTab=win:Tab(t("tab_autobuy"),ASSETS.tab.autobuy)
do
local function findStoreCounterPart(storeName)
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
local store=stores and stores:FindFirstChild(storeName)
local counter=store and store:FindFirstChild("Counter")
if counter and counter:IsA("BasePart")then return counter end
return nil
end
getStoreCounter=function(storeName)
local counter=findStoreCounterPart(storeName)
if not counter then
pcall(function()notify("商店没有找到Counter部件: "..tostring(storeName),"error",3)end)
return nil
end
return(counter.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,0.6,0)).Position
end
function getStoreCounterLockCF(storeName)
local counter=findStoreCounterPart(storeName)
if not counter then return nil end
return counter.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0)
end
-- 锁在柜台旁，直到确认购买结束（silentConfirmPurchase 结束时自动解锁）
function YQXLockAtCounter(storeName)
local lockCF=getStoreCounterLockCF(storeName)
if not lockCF then return false end
lockPlayerAt(lockCF)
task.wait(0.1)
return true
end
shopCatalog={}
selectedShopIndex=1
selectedProductIndex=1
buyQuantity=1
local function getModelPos(model)
if not model then return nil end
local ok,piv=pcall(function()return model:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok and piv then return piv end
local p=model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
return p and p.Position
end
local PRODUCT_CN={
["Battery"]="Battery",["HappyBall"]="Happy Ball",["Egg"]="Egg",
["NESController"]="NES Controller",["MagicLamp"]="Magic Lamp",["LightBulb"]="Light Bulb",
["Duck"]="Duck",["WhiteDuck"]="White Duck",
["Trident"]="Trident",["DemonTrident"]="Demon Trident",
["Milk"]="Milk",["Cola"]="Cola",["Soda"]="Soda",
["HotCocoa"]="Hot Cocoa",["Cocoa"]="Cocoa",["TigerEye"]="Tiger Eye",
["BasicHatchet"]="Basic Hatchet",["BlurpleAxe"]="Blurple Axe",["AxeBlurple"]="Axe Blurple",
["HardenedAxe"]="Hardened Axe",["Sawmill"]="Sawmill",
["4SeatTruck"]="4-Seat Truck",["Truck"]="Truck",["Car"]="Car",
["Box"]="Box",["Crate"]="Crate",["Wood"]="Wood",["Plank"]="Plank",
["Painting"]="Painting",["Chair"]="Chair",["Table"]="Table",
["Wall"]="Wall",["Floor"]="Floor",["Door"]="Door",
["Wire"]="Wire",["Lever"]="Lever",["Button"]="Button",
["Sign"]="Sign",["Bag"]="Bag",["Seed"]="Seed",["Sapling"]="Sapling",
["Conveyor"]="Conveyor",["Vehicle"]="Vehicle",
}
for key,value in pairs(STORE_TRANSLATIONS)do
if PRODUCT_CN[key]then PRODUCT_CN[key]=value end
end
local function productDisplayName(name)
if STORE_TRANSLATIONS[name]then return STORE_TRANSLATIONS[name]end
if PRODUCT_CN[name]then return PRODUCT_CN[name]end
for en,cn in pairs(STORE_TRANSLATIONS)do
if string.find(name,en,1,true)then return cn.."("..name..")"end
end
for en,cn in pairs(PRODUCT_CN)do
if string.find(name,en,1,true)then
return cn.."("..name..")"
end
end
return name
end
scanAllShops=function()
shopCatalog={}
local storesFolder=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
if not storesFolder then
storesFolder=Workspace:WaitForChild("Stores",5)
end
if not storesFolder then
warn("[YQX] Workspace.Stores 不存在")
pcall(function()notify(t("n_stores_not_found"),"error")end)
return shopCatalog
end
for _,store in ipairs(storesFolder:GetChildren())do
if store:IsA("Folder")or store:IsA("Model")then
local products={}
local seenName={}
local shopItems=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
or store:FindFirstChild("Items")
or store:FindFirstChild("Products")
local function addModel(model)
if not model or not model:IsA("Model")then return end
local name=model.Name
if name=="ShopItems"or name=="Items"or name==store.Name then return end
if seenName[name]then return end
if model:FindFirstChild("Dialog")or model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")then
return
end
seenName[name]=true
local pos=getModelPos(model)
if not pos then
local part=model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
pos=part and part.Position or Vector3.new(0,0,0)
end
local disp=name
pcall(function()disp=productDisplayName(name)end)
table.insert(products,{
name=name,
display=disp,
model=model,
pos=pos,
})
end
if shopItems then
for _,child in ipairs(shopItems:GetChildren())do
if child:IsA("Model")then
addModel(child)
elseif child:IsA("Folder")then
for _,sub in ipairs(child:GetChildren())do
if sub:IsA("Model")then addModel(sub)end
end
end
end
if#products==0 then
for _,d in ipairs(shopItems:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("Model")and d.Parent==shopItems then
addModel(d)
elseif d:IsA("Model")and d.Parent and d.Parent.Parent==shopItems then
addModel(d)
end
end
end
if#products==0 then
for _,d in ipairs(shopItems:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("Model")then
addModel(d)
end
end
end
else
for _,child in ipairs(store:GetChildren())do
if child:IsA("Model")then addModel(child)end
end
end
table.sort(products,function(a,b)
return tostring(a.display or a.name)<tostring(b.display or b.name)
end)
local counterPos=nil
do
local cpart=store:FindFirstChild("Counter")
if cpart and cpart:IsA("BasePart")then counterPos=(cpart.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,0.6,0)).Position end
end
table.insert(shopCatalog,{
name=store.Name,
center=getModelPos(store)or counterPos,
counterPos=counterPos,
storeModel=store,
products=products,
})
end
end
table.sort(shopCatalog,function(a,b)return a.name<b.name end)
return shopCatalog
end
end
local autoBuySec=autoBuyTab:section(t("sec_shop"),true)
local autoBuyAllSec=autoBuyTab:section(t("sec_autobuy_all"),true)
local statusLbl=autoBuySec:Label(t("lbl_ready"))
local currentShop,currentProduct,rebuildProductList,rebuildShopList,doScan
findStoreNpc,resetChatState,moneyDropped,silentConfirmPurchase=nil,nil,nil,nil
local buying,buyStop,bpBuying,bpBuyStop
buying=false
buyStop=false
bpBuying=false
bpBuyStop=false
local shopDrop=autoBuySec:Dropdown(t("dd_select_shop"),"shopSelect",{},function(selected)
for i,s in ipairs(shopCatalog)do
local label=storeDisplayName(s.name)
if label==selected then
selectedShopIndex=i
selectedProductIndex=1
break
end
end
rebuildProductList()
end)

local s=currentShop()
if not s then return end
for i,pr in ipairs(s.products)do
if pr.display==selected then
selectedProductIndex=i
break
end
end
end)

local n=tonumber(text)
if n and n>=1 then
buyQuantity=math.floor(n)
end
end)

pcall(function()notify(t("n_select_shop"),"warn")end)

safeTeleport(counter.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,2.5,0))

pcall(function()notify("商店没有找到Counter部件","error",3)end)
end

autoBuySec:Button(t("btn_buy_now"),function()

statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_stopping")

statusLbl.Text=t("n_select_item")

buyStop=false
buying=true
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_buying")
task.spawn(function()
local okBuy,errBuy=pcall(function()
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originCF=hrp and hrp.CFrame
local counter=getStoreCounter(s.name)
local successCount=0
local function resolveUnownedProduct()
if p.model and p.model.Parent and isUnowned(p.model)then
return p.model
end
local name=p.name
local store=s.storeModel
local items=store and store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,obj in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==name and isUnowned(obj)then
return obj
end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==name and isUnowned(obj)then
return obj
end
end
return nil
end
for q=1,buyQuantity do
if buyStop then break end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_waiting_item"),q,buyQuantity)
local model=nil
local waitProdT0=tick()
while tick()-waitProdT0<30 do
model=resolveUnownedProduct()
if model then break end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_waiting_unowned"),q,buyQuantity,tostring(p.name))
task.wait(0.35)
end
p.model=model
if not model then
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_timeout_item")..tostring(p.name)
pcall(function()notify(t("n_no_unowned_item")..tostring(p.name),"warn")end)
break
end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_tp_counter"),q,buyQuantity)
teleportOneItem(model,counter)
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(s.name or"WoodRUs")
local storeName=s.name or"WoodRUs"
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_waiting_npc"),q,buyQuantity)
pcall(function()notify(storeName..t("n_store_npc_wait"),"info")end)
local tCache=tick()
while tick()-tCache<3 do
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then break end
pcall(function()
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName)
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]and ctx and type(ctx.ID)=="number"then
npcIdCache[storeName]=ctx.ID
end
end)
task.wait(0.15)
end
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_no_npc_return")
pcall(function()notify(storeName..t("n_store_npc_3s"),"warn")end)
if originCF then
local h=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if h then h.CFrame=originCF end
end
break
end
end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_buying_status"),q,buyQuantity)
local moneyBefore=nil
pcall(function()moneyBefore=getMoney()end)
local bought,hitId=false,nil
local okSC,r1,r2=pcall(function()
return silentConfirmPurchase(storeName,moneyBefore)
end)
if okSC then
bought,hitId=r1,r2
else
warn("[YQX] silentConfirm",r1)
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_buy_error")
end
local moneyAfter=getMoney()
if type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(moneyAfter)=="number"and moneyAfter<moneyBefore then
bought=true
end
pcall(function()
local npcDlg=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("NPCDialog")
local playerChatted=npcDlg and npcDlg:FindFirstChild("PlayerChatted")
local setVal=npcDlg and npcDlg:FindFirstChild("SetChattingValue")
local ctx=getThomContext and getThomContext(storeName)
if playerChatted and ctx then
playerChatted:InvokeServer(ctx,"EndChat")
end
if setVal then setVal:InvokeServer(0)end
end)
if bought then
successCount=successCount+1
pcall(function()notify(t("n_buy_success")..p.name,"success")end)
if originCF then teleportOneItem(model,originCF.Position)end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_success_count"),successCount)
p.model=nil
else
pcall(function()notify(t("n_buy_fail")..p.name,"warn")end)
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_failed_charge")
p.model=nil
break
end
task.wait(0.15)
end
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_done_count"),successCount,buyQuantity)
end)
if not okBuy then
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_error_prefix")..tostring(errBuy)
warn("[YQX] buy error",errBuy)
pcall(function()notify(t("n_buy_err"),"error")end)
end
buying=false
buyStop=false
end)
end)
autoBuySec:Button(t("btn_rescan"),function()
task.spawn(doScan)
end)
currentShop=function()
return shopCatalog[selectedShopIndex]
end
currentProduct=function()
local s=currentShop()
return s and s.products[selectedProductIndex]
end
rebuildProductList=function()
local s=currentShop()
if not s or#s.products==0 then
itemDrop:SetOptions({})
selectedProductIndex=1
return
end
if selectedProductIndex>#s.products then selectedProductIndex=1 end
local names={}
for _,pr in ipairs(s.products)do
table.insert(names,pr.display)
end
itemDrop:SetOptions(names)
end
rebuildShopList=function()
local shopNames={}
for _,s in ipairs(shopCatalog)do
table.insert(shopNames,storeDisplayName(s.name))
end
shopDrop:SetOptions(shopNames)
rebuildProductList()
end
doScan=function()
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_scanning")
pcall(function()notify(t("n_scanning"),"info")end)
local okScan,errScan=pcall(scanAllShops)
if not okScan then
warn("[YQX] scanAllShops",errScan)
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_scan_error")..tostring(errScan)
pcall(function()notify(t("n_scan_fail"),"error")end)
return
end
selectedShopIndex=1
selectedProductIndex=1
local total=0
for _,s in ipairs(shopCatalog)do total=total+#s.products end
local okR,errR=pcall(rebuildShopList)
if not okR then
warn("[YQX] rebuildShopList",errR)
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_list_error")..tostring(errR)
return
end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_shops_items"),#shopCatalog,total)
pcall(function()notify(string.format(t("n_scan_done"),#shopCatalog,total),"success")end)
end
findStoreNpc=function(store)
if not store then return nil end
for _,n in ipairs({"Thom","NPC","Shopkeeper","Seller","Clerk"})do
local t=store:FindFirstChild(n)
if t then return t end
end
for _,c in ipairs(store:GetChildren())do
if c:FindFirstChild("Dialog")then return c end
end
return nil
end
getThomContext=function(storeName)
storeName=storeName or"WoodRUs"
if npcCtxCache[storeName]and npcCtxCache[storeName].Character then
local c=npcCtxCache[storeName]
return c,c.Character
end
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
local store=stores and stores:FindFirstChild(storeName)
local thom=findStoreNpc(store)
if not thom and stores then
local counter=getStoreCounter(storeName)
local bestDist=math.huge
for _,s in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local npc=findStoreNpc(s)
if npc then
local ok,pos=pcall(function()return npc:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok and pos then
local d=(pos-counter).Magnitude
if d<bestDist then
bestDist=d
thom=npc
store=s
end
end
end
end
if thom then
end
end
if not thom then
return nil
end
local dialog=thom:FindFirstChild("Dialog")
local id=npcIdCache[storeName]or npcIdCache[thom.Name]or 24
local ctx={
Character=thom,
Name=thom.Name,
ID=id,
Dialog=dialog,
}
npcCtxCache[storeName]=ctx
return ctx,thom
end
resetChatState=function(storeName)
pcall(function()
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName or"WoodRUs")
local npcDlg=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("NPCDialog")
local playerChatted=npcDlg and npcDlg:FindFirstChild("PlayerChatted")
local setVal=npcDlg and npcDlg:FindFirstChild("SetChattingValue")
if ctx and playerChatted then
pcall(function()playerChatted:InvokeServer(ctx,"EndChat")end)
end
task.wait(0.05)
if setVal then
pcall(function()setVal:InvokeServer(0)end)
end
end)
pcall(function()
local cg=PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChatGUI")
if not cg then return end
local chat=cg:FindFirstChild("Chat")
if chat then
local choices=chat:FindFirstChild("Choices")
if choices then
for _,c in ipairs(choices:GetChildren())do
pcall(function()c.Visible=false end)
end
end
pcall(function()chat.Visible=false end)
end
end)
pcall(function()
local ig=PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("InteractionGUI")
if ig then ig.Enabled=true end
end)
end
moneyDropped=function(before)
if type(before)~="number"then return false end
local m=getMoney()
return type(m)=="number"and m<before-0.5
end
silentConfirmPurchase=function(storeName,moneyBefore)
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName)
if not ctx then
return false,nil
end
local npcDlg=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("NPCDialog")
if not npcDlg then return false,nil end
local playerChatted=npcDlg:FindFirstChild("PlayerChatted")
local setVal=npcDlg:FindFirstChild("SetChattingValue")
if not playerChatted then return false,nil end
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
notify(storeName..t("n_store_npc_cache"),"info")
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<5 do
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then break end
task.wait(0.1)
end
end
local cachedId=npcIdCache[storeName]
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]or not cachedId then
notify(storeName..t("n_store_npc_no_cache"),"warn")
return false,nil
end
ctx.ID=cachedId
if type(moneyBefore)~="number"then
moneyBefore=getMoney()
end
local lastFunds=moneyBefore
local fundsConn
pcall(function()
local tx=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Transactions")
local stc=tx and tx:FindFirstChild("ServerToClient")
local fc=stc and stc:FindFirstChild("FundsChanged")
if fc then
fundsConn=fc.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(a,b)
if type(a)=="number"then lastFunds=a currentMoney=a
elseif type(b)=="number"then lastFunds=b currentMoney=b end
end)
end
end)
local function endChatClean()
pcall(function()playerChatted:InvokeServer(ctx,"EndChat")end)
pcall(function()if setVal then setVal:InvokeServer(0)end end)
end
local tDeadline=tick()+5
local hit=false
while tick()<tDeadline do
endChatClean()
task.wait(0.05)
local before=getMoney()or lastFunds or moneyBefore
pcall(function()if setVal then setVal:InvokeServer(1)end end)
pcall(function()playerChatted:InvokeServer(ctx,"Initiate")end)
task.wait(0.06)
pcall(function()playerChatted:InvokeServer(ctx,"ConfirmPurchase")end)
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<0.4 do
local m=getMoney()or lastFunds
if type(before)=="number"and type(m)=="number"and m<before-0.5 then
hit=true
break
end
task.wait(0.04)
end
if hit then break end
task.wait(0.08)
end
endChatClean()
pcall(function()if fundsConn then fundsConn:Disconnect()end end)
if hit then
return true,cachedId
end
return false,nil
end
do
local rawConfirm=silentConfirmPurchase
silentConfirmPurchase=function(...)
local r=table.pack(pcall(rawConfirm,...))
pcall(unlockPlayer)
if not r[1]then error(r[2],0)end
return table.unpack(r,2,r.n)
end
end
_G.YQXSilentConfirm=silentConfirmPurchase
local ALL_BLUEPRINTS={
"Wall1","Wall1Thin","Wall1Corner","Wall1Short","Wall1ShortThin","Wall1ShortCorner",
"Wall1Tall","Wall1TallThin","Wall1TallCorner",
"Wall2","Wall2Thin","Wall2Corner","Wall2Short","Wall2ShortThin","Wall2ShortCorner",
"Wall2Tall","Wall2TallThin","Wall2TallCorner","Wall2TallThinTall","Wall2Stub",
"Wall3","Wall3Thin","Wall3Corner","Wall3Tall","Wall3TallThin","Wall3TallCorner",
"Floor1","Floor1Tiny","Floor1Small","Floor1Large",
"Floor2","Floor2Tiny","Floor2Small","Floor2Large",
"Wedge1","Wedge1Corner","Wedge2","Wedge2_Thin","Wedge2Corner",
"Wedge3","Wedge3_Thin","Wedge3Corner","Wedge4","Wedge4_Thin","Wedge4Corner",
"Wedge5","Wedge5_Thin","Wedge5Corner","Wedge6","Wedge6_Thin","Wedge6Corner",
"Wedge7","Wedge7_Thin","Wedge7Corner","Wedge8","Wedge8_Thin","Wedge8Corner",
"Wedge9","Wedge9_Thin","Wedge9Corner","Wedge10","Wedge10_Thin","Wedge10Corner",
"Ramp",
}
autoBuyAllSec:Toggle(t("tog_autobuy_all"),"autoBuyAll",false,function(on)
if not on then
bpBuyStop=true
return
end
if bpBuying or buying then
bpBuyStop=true
return
end
bpBuyStop=false
bpBuying=true
task.spawn(function()
local okbp,errbp=pcall(function()
local storeName="WoodRUs"
local counter=getStoreCounter(storeName)
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originCF=hrp and hrp.CFrame
local okCount=0
for bi,bpName in ipairs(ALL_BLUEPRINTS)do
if bpBuyStop then break end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_bp_progress"),bi,#ALL_BLUEPRINTS,bpName)
pcall(function()notify(t("n_buying_bp")..bpName,"info")end)
local model=nil
local tWait=tick()
while tick()-tWait<25 do
local store=Workspace.Stores and Workspace.Stores:FindFirstChild("WoodRUs")
local items=store and store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,obj in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==bpName and isUnowned(obj)then
model=obj
break
end
end
end
if model then break end
task.wait(0.4)
end
if not model then
pcall(function()notify(t("n_skip_stock")..bpName,"warn")end)
else
teleportOneItem(model,counter)
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(storeName)
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
local tCache=tick()
while tick()-tCache<3 do
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then break end
pcall(function()
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName)
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]and ctx and type(ctx.ID)=="number"then npcIdCache[storeName]=ctx.ID end
end)
task.wait(0.15)
end
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
pcall(function()notify(t("n_no_npc_cache_skip")..bpName,"warn")end)
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
break
end
end
local moneyBefore=getMoney()
local bought=select(1,silentConfirmPurchase(storeName,moneyBefore))
local moneyAfter=getMoney()
if type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(moneyAfter)=="number"and moneyAfter<moneyBefore then
bought=true
end
if bought then
okCount=okCount+1
if originCF then teleportOneItem(model,originCF.Position)end
pcall(function()notify(t("n_bp_success")..bpName,"success")end)
else
pcall(function()notify(t("n_bp_failed")..bpName,"warn")end)
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
end
task.wait(0.2)
end
end
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
statusLbl.Text=string.format(t("lbl_bp_done"),okCount,#ALL_BLUEPRINTS)
notify(string.format(t("n_bp_purchase_done"),okCount,#ALL_BLUEPRINTS),"success")
end)
if not okbp then
warn("[YQX] bp buy",errbp)
statusLbl.Text=t("n_bp_buy_err")
end
bpBuying=false
bpBuyStop=false
end)
end)
-- ==================== 购物车队列 ====================
local cartSec=autoBuyTab:section("购物车队列",true)
local cartList={}
local cartRunning=false
local cartStop=false
local cartStatus=cartSec:Label("队列为空（先加物品再执行）")
local cartNameInput=""
local cartQtyInput="1"
local function cartSummary()
if #cartList==0 then return"队列为空" end
local total=0
local names={}
for _,e in ipairs(cartList)do
total=total+e.qty
table.insert(names,string.format("%s×%d",e.display or e.product,e.qty))
end
local s=table.concat(names,"、")
if #s>66 then s=string.sub(s,1,66).."…" end
return string.format("队列 %d 项 / 共 %d 件：%s",#cartList,total,s)
end
local function cartRefresh()
pcall(function()cartStatus.Text=cartSummary()end)
end
local function cartFindModel(storeName,productName)
local store=nil
for _,s in ipairs(shopCatalog or{})do
if s.name==storeName then store=s break end
end
if not store then return nil,nil end
for _,pr in ipairs(store.products or{})do
if pr.name==productName and pr.model and pr.model.Parent and isUnowned(pr.model)then return store,pr.model end
end
local items=store.storeModel and store.storeModel:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,obj in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==productName and isUnowned(obj)then return store,obj end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==productName and isUnowned(obj)then return store,obj end
end
return store,nil
end
local function cartBuyOne(storeName,productName,qty)
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originCF=hrp and hrp.CFrame
local counter=getStoreCounter(storeName)
local got=0
local reason=nil
for q=1,qty do
if cartStop then break end
pcall(function()cartStatus.Text=string.format("执行中 %s ×%d（%d/%d）",productName,qty,q,qty)end)
local store,model=cartFindModel(storeName,productName)
if not model then
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<25 do
if cartStop then break end
store,model=cartFindModel(storeName,productName)
if model then break end
pcall(function()cartStatus.Text=string.format("等货 %s（%d 秒）",productName,math.floor(tick()-t0))end)
task.wait(0.4)
end
end
if not model then
reason="缺货: "..productName
break
end
teleportOneItem(model,counter)
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(storeName)
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
local tCache=tick()
while tick()-tCache<3 do
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then break end
pcall(function()
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName)
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]and ctx and type(ctx.ID)=="number"then
npcIdCache[storeName]=ctx.ID
end
end)
task.wait(0.15)
end
end
local moneyBefore=getMoney()
local okBuy,b1=pcall(function()return silentConfirmPurchase(storeName,moneyBefore)end)
local bought=(okBuy and b1)and true or false
local moneyAfter=getMoney()
if type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(moneyAfter)=="number"and moneyAfter<moneyBefore then
bought=true
end
pcall(function()
local npcDlg=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("NPCDialog")
local playerChatted=npcDlg and npcDlg:FindFirstChild("PlayerChatted")
local setVal=npcDlg and npcDlg:FindFirstChild("SetChattingValue")
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName)
if playerChatted and ctx then playerChatted:InvokeServer(ctx,"EndChat")end
if setVal then setVal:InvokeServer(0)end
end)
if bought then
got=got+1
pcall(function()notify("购物车买到: "..productName,"success",2)end)
if originCF then teleportOneItem(model,originCF.Position)end
else
reason="购买失败: "..productName
if originCF and hrp then pcall(function()hrp.CFrame=originCF end)end
break
end
task.wait(0.2)
end
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if originCF and hrp then pcall(function()hrp.CFrame=originCF end)end
return got,reason
end
cartSec:Textbox("物品名（按名字加入时用）","yqxCartName","",function(text)
cartNameInput=text
end)
cartSec:Textbox("数量","yqxCartQty","1",function(text)
local n=tonumber(text)
if n and n>=1 then cartQtyInput=tostring(math.floor(n))end
end)
cartSec:Button("加入当前选中的商店物品",function()
local s=currentShop()
local p=currentProduct()
if not s or not p then
notify("先在上面选好商店和物品","warn",3)
return
end
local qty=tonumber(cartQtyInput)or 1
if qty<1 then qty=1 end
table.insert(cartList,{shop=s.name,product=p.name,display=p.display,qty=qty})
cartRefresh()
notify(string.format("已加入购物车: %s ×%d",p.display or p.name,qty),"success",2)
end)
cartSec:Button("按名字加入（全商店搜索）",function()
local want=string.gsub(cartNameInput or"","^%s+","")
want=string.gsub(want,"%s+$","")
if want==""then
notify("先填物品名","warn",3)
return
end
local qty=tonumber(cartQtyInput)or 1
if qty<1 then qty=1 end
local low=string.lower(want)
local hitShop,hitProduct,hitDisplay=nil,nil,nil
for _,s in ipairs(shopCatalog or{})do
for _,pr in ipairs(s.products or{})do
if string.lower(pr.name)==low or string.lower(pr.display or"")==low then
hitShop,hitProduct,hitDisplay=s.name,pr.name,pr.display
break
end
end
if hitShop then break end
end
if not hitShop then
for _,s in ipairs(shopCatalog or{})do
for _,pr in ipairs(s.products or{})do
if string.find(string.lower(pr.name),low,1,true)then
hitShop,hitProduct,hitDisplay=s.name,pr.name,pr.display
break
end
end
if hitShop then break end
end
end
if not hitShop then
notify("商店列表里没找到 "..want.."，先点上面的刷新商店","warn",5)
return
end
table.insert(cartList,{shop=hitShop,product=hitProduct,display=hitDisplay,qty=qty})
cartRefresh()
notify(string.format("已加入: %s ×%d @%s",hitDisplay or hitProduct,qty,storeDisplayName(hitShop)),"success",3)
end)
cartSec:Button("移除最后一项",function()
if #cartList==0 then return end
table.remove(cartList)
cartRefresh()
end)
cartSec:Button("清空队列",function()
cartList={}
cartRefresh()
notify("购物车已清空","info",2)
end)
cartSec:Button("开始执行购物车",function()
if cartRunning then
notify("队列正在跑，先等它跑完","info",3)
return
end
if #cartList==0 then
notify("队列是空的","warn",3)
return
end
cartRunning=true
cartStop=false
task.spawn(function()
local totalOk=0
local totalWant=0
local errs={}
for _,e in ipairs(cartList)do totalWant=totalWant+e.qty end
for i,e in ipairs(cartList)do
if cartStop then break end
pcall(function()cartStatus.Text=string.format("第 %d/%d 项：%s",i,#cartList,e.display or e.product)end)
local a,reason=cartBuyOne(e.shop,e.product,e.qty)
totalOk=totalOk+(a or 0)
if reason then table.insert(errs,reason)end
if cartStop then break end
task.wait(0.3)
end
cartRunning=false
local msg=string.format("购物车完成：%d/%d 件",totalOk,totalWant)
if #errs>0 then msg=msg.."｜"..errs[1] end
pcall(function()cartStatus.Text=msg end)
notify(msg,#errs==0 and"success"or"warn",5)
end)
end)
cartSec:Button("停止执行购物车",function()
if not cartRunning then
notify("现在没在跑","info",2)
return
end
cartStop=true
notify("已请求停止购物车","warn",3)
end)
cartRefresh()

doScan()
end

identifyMaterials=function()
local result={}
local texts={}
local stoneParts=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")and Workspace.Stores:FindFirstChild("StoneRUs")and Workspace.Stores.StoneRUs:FindFirstChild("Parts")
if stoneParts then
for _,d in ipairs(stoneParts:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("TextLabel")then table.insert(texts,d.Text)end
end
end
for i,v in ipairs(texts)do
local bestMatch=nil
local bestLength=0
for code,materials in pairs(codeToMaterial)do
local codeLen=#code
if codeLen>bestLength and string.sub(v,-codeLen)==code then
bestLength=codeLen
bestMatch=materials[1]
end
end
result[i]=bestMatch or"未知"
end
if#result>=4 then
result[1],result[4]=result[4],result[1]
result[2],result[3]=result[3],result[2]
end
return result
end

local function matchName(itemName,matName)
local list=alias[matName]or{matName}
local low=string.lower(itemName)
for _,a in ipairs(list)do
if itemName==a or low==string.lower(a)then return true end
if string.find(low,string.lower(a),1,true)then return true end
end
return false
end
local function findOwnedMat(matName)
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and isOwnedByMe(obj)and matchName(obj.Name,matName)then
return obj
end
end
local list=alias[matName]or{matName}
for _,n in ipairs(list)do
local it=findOwnedItem(n)
if it then return it end
end
return nil
end
local function findShopProduct(matName)
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
if not stores then return nil,nil end
for _,store in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local items=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,it in ipairs(items:GetChildren())do
if matchName(it.Name,matName)and isUnowned(it)then
return it,store.Name
end
end
for _,it in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if it:IsA("Model")and matchName(it.Name,matName)and isUnowned(it)then
return it,store.Name
end
end
end
end
return nil,nil
end

else
pcall(function()notify("购买失败 "..matName,"warn")end)

return findOwnedMat(matName)
end

end

pcall(function()
if clientDragging then
clientDragging:FireServer("Begin",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("Refresh",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("End",target,_dragID)
end
end)
task.wait(0.2)
local _dragID=getNextDragID()
pcall(function()
if clientDragging then
clientDragging:FireServer("Begin",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("Refresh",target,_dragID)
clientDragging:FireServer("End",target,_dragID)
end
end)
task.wait(0.25)
pcall(function()
clientInteracted:FireServer(target,"Open box")
end)
task.wait(0.2)
pcall(function()
clientInteracted:FireServer(target,"Open box")
end)
task.wait(0.25)
return true
end
btn.Text="开盒..."
for i=1,3 do
if not(contDemonDuck or demonSingleRun) then break end
local pos=placePos[i]
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if pos then lockPlayerAt(CFrame.new(pos+Vector3.new(0,3,0)))end
task.wait(0.2)
selectThenOpen(owned[i],need[i]or("材料"..i))
task.wait(0.35)
end
pcall(unlockPlayer)
if not(contDemonDuck or demonSingleRun) then
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then hrp.CFrame=originalCF end
btn.Text="自动合成恶魔鸭"
btn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
local function findNewDuckEvil()
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="DuckEvil"and isUnowned(obj)then
if not beforeDucks[obj]then
return obj
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function waitNewDuck(seconds)
local newDuck=nil
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<seconds do
if not(contDemonDuck or demonSingleRun) then return nil end
newDuck=findNewDuckEvil()
if newDuck then return newDuck end
task.wait(0.1)
end
return findNewDuckEvil()
end
local newDuck=nil
local retryCount=0
local MAX_RETRIES=15
newDuck=waitNewDuck(1.0)
while not newDuck do
if not(contDemonDuck or demonSingleRun) then break end
retryCount=retryCount+1
if retryCount>MAX_RETRIES then break end
btn.Text=string.format("重放材料 #%d",retryCount)
pcall(function()notify(string.format("未出鸭，第%d次重放",retryCount),"warn")end)
for i=1,3 do
if owned[i]and owned[i].Parent then
teleportOneItem(owned[i],placePos[i])
task.wait(0.05)
end
end
newDuck=waitNewDuck(0.8)
end
if not(contDemonDuck or demonSingleRun) then
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then hrp.CFrame=originalCF end
btn.Text="自动合成恶魔鸭"
btn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
if newDuck then
btn.Text="带回新恶魔鸭"
teleportOneItem(newDuck,originalCF.Position)
notify("新恶魔鸭已带回","success")
btn.Text="合成成功"
btn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
else
notify("仍无新DuckEvil，返回原地","warn")
btn.Text="无新鸭子"
btn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
end
task.wait(0.35)
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then hrp.CFrame=originalCF end
btn.Text="自动合成恶魔鸭"
btn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
end)
pcall(unlockPlayer)
if not ok then
warn("[YQX] autoDemonDuckTestBtn",err)
pcall(function()notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error")end)
end
demonSynthesisBusy=false
demonSingleRun=false
end)
return true
end

do
local CRATE_BTN_POS=Vector3.new(-656.9,-36.3,483.4)
local DUCK_NAME="2018CGift_Duck"
local CRATE_STORE="FineFinds"
local CRATE_ITEM="Crate"
local giftDuckQty=synthDuckSec:Textbox("普通鸭数量","synthGiftQty","1")
local giftDuckBtn=synthDuckSec:Button("自动购买普通鸭",function()end)
giftDuckBtn.Name="AutoGiftDuck"
giftDuckBtn.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
local giftDuckPad=Instance.new("UIPadding")
giftDuckPad.PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,12)
giftDuckPad.Parent=giftDuckBtn
local giftRunning=false
local giftStop=false
local function countGiftDucks()
local n=0
pcall(function()
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==DUCK_NAME then
local owned=false
pcall(function()
if isOwnedByMe and isOwnedByMe(obj)then owned=true end
end)
if not owned then
local ow=obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if ow and ow.Value==speaker then owned=true end
end
if owned then n=n+1 end
end
end
end)
return n
end
local function findUnownedCrate()
local store=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")and Workspace.Stores:FindFirstChild(CRATE_STORE)
local items=store and store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,obj in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==CRATE_ITEM and isUnowned(obj)then
return obj
end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==CRATE_ITEM and isUnowned(obj)then
return obj
end
end
return nil
end
local function resolveOwnedCrate(prefer)
if prefer and prefer.Parent then return prefer end
local best=nil
pcall(function()
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and(obj.Name==CRATE_ITEM or obj.Name:find("Crate"))then
local ow=obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if ow and ow.Value==speaker then
best=obj
break
end
end
end
if not best then
for _,obj in getnilinstances()do
if typeof(obj)=="Instance"and obj:IsA("Model")then
if obj.Name==CRATE_ITEM or obj.Name:find("Crate")then
best=obj
break
end
end
end
end
end)
return best
end
local function openBoxModel(model)
local inter=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
local clientInteracted=inter and inter:FindFirstChild("ClientInteracted")
if not clientInteracted or not model then return end
for _=1,2 do
pcall(function()
clientInteracted:FireServer(model,"Open box")
end)
task.wait(0.12)
end
end
local function pressCrateButton(model)
local inter=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
local proxy=inter and inter:FindFirstChild("RemoteProxy")
if not proxy then return false end
local btn=nil
pcall(function()
if model then
btn=model:FindFirstChild("ButtonRemote_Main",true)
if not btn then
for _,d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants())do
if d.Name=="ButtonRemote_Main"or d.Name:find("ButtonRemote")then
btn=d
break
end
end
end
end
if not btn then
for _,obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants())do
if obj.Name=="ButtonRemote_Main"then
local p=obj:IsA("BasePart")and obj or obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
if p and(p.Position-CRATE_BTN_POS).Magnitude<25 then
btn=obj
break
end
end
end
end
end)
if not btn then
return false
end
for _=1,3 do
pcall(function()
proxy:FireServer(btn)
end)
task.wait(0.08)
end
return true
end
local function snapshotDucks()
local set={}
pcall(function()
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==DUCK_NAME then
local id=nil
pcall(function()id=obj:GetDebugId()end)
set[id or tostring(obj)]=true
end
end
end)
return set
end
local function findNewGiftDuck(beforeSet)
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name==DUCK_NAME then
local id=nil
pcall(function()id=obj:GetDebugId()end)
local key=id or tostring(obj)
if not beforeSet[key]then
return obj
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function notifyStats(opened,got)
local rate=opened>0 and(got/opened*100)or 0
notify(
string.format("已开 %d 个箱子\n已出 %d 个鸭子（概率 %.2f%%）",opened,got,rate),
"success"
)
end
giftDuckBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if giftRunning then
giftStop=true
giftDuckBtn.Text="正在停止..."
notify("正在停止普通鸭购买","warn")
return
end
local need=tonumber(library.flags.synthGiftQty)or 1
if need<1 then need=1 end
need=math.floor(need)
giftRunning=true
giftStop=false
giftDuckBtn.Text="购买中...(再点停止)"
task.spawn(function()
local okRun,errRun=pcall(function()
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originCF=hrp and hrp.CFrame
local startCount=countGiftDucks()
local opened=0
local got=0
notify(string.format("目标鸭子 %d（当前已有 %d）",need,startCount),"info")
local counter=getStoreCounter and getStoreCounter(CRATE_STORE)
if not counter then
counter=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
pcall(function()
local st=Workspace.Stores:FindFirstChild(CRATE_STORE)
if st then
local p=st:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
if p then counter=p.Position end
end
end)
end
while got<need and not giftStop do
local crate=nil
local t0=tick()
while tick()-t0<25 and not giftStop do
crate=findUnownedCrate()
if crate then break end
giftDuckBtn.Text="等Crate...(再点停止)"
task.wait(0.35)
end
if giftStop then break end
if not crate then
notify("FineFinds 没有 Crate","warn")
break
end
if not npcIdConfirmed[CRATE_STORE]then
giftDuckBtn.Text="等NPC缓存..."
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(CRATE_STORE)
local tc=tick()
while tick()-tc<3 and not giftStop do
if npcIdConfirmed[CRATE_STORE]then break end
pcall(function()
local ctx=getThomContext(CRATE_STORE)
if npcIdConfirmed[CRATE_STORE]and ctx and type(ctx.ID)=="number"then npcIdCache[CRATE_STORE]=ctx.ID end
end)
task.wait(0.15)
end
if not npcIdConfirmed[CRATE_STORE]then
notify("FineFinds 无NPC缓存，回原位","warn")
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
break
end
end
if giftStop then break end
giftDuckBtn.Text="购买Crate...(再点停止)"
teleportOneItem(crate,counter)
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(CRATE_STORE)
local moneyBefore=getMoney()
local bought=false
if _G.YQXSilentConfirm then
bought=select(1,_G.YQXSilentConfirm(CRATE_STORE,moneyBefore))
elseif type(silentConfirmPurchase)=="function"then
bought=select(1,silentConfirmPurchase(CRATE_STORE,moneyBefore))
end
local moneyAfter=getMoney()
if type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(moneyAfter)=="number"and moneyAfter<moneyBefore then
bought=true
end
if not bought then
notify("Crate 购买失败","warn")
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
task.wait(0.4)
else
opened=opened+1
local beforeSet=snapshotDucks()
local crateModel=resolveOwnedCrate(crate)
giftDuckBtn.Text="开箱1...(再点停止)"
openBoxModel(crateModel)
task.wait(0.2)
crateModel=resolveOwnedCrate(crateModel)
giftDuckBtn.Text="传坐标...(再点停止)"
if crateModel and crateModel.Parent then
teleportOneItem(crateModel,CRATE_BTN_POS)
else
crateModel=resolveOwnedCrate(nil)
if crateModel then teleportOneItem(crateModel,CRATE_BTN_POS)end
end
task.wait(0.2)
crateModel=resolveOwnedCrate(crateModel)
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(CRATE_BTN_POS+Vector3.new(3,2.2,0),CRATE_BTN_POS)
pcall(function()hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero end)
end
task.wait(0.15)
giftDuckBtn.Text="按按钮...(再点停止)"
pressCrateButton(crateModel)
task.wait(0.15)
giftDuckBtn.Text="开箱2...(再点停止)"
crateModel=resolveOwnedCrate(crateModel)
openBoxModel(crateModel)
task.wait(0.3)
local duckObj=nil
for _=1,25 do
if giftStop then break end
duckObj=findNewGiftDuck(beforeSet)
if duckObj then break end
task.wait(0.12)
end
if duckObj and originCF then
giftDuckBtn.Text="拖鸭回家..."
teleportOneItem(duckObj,originCF.Position)
got=got+1
end
notifyStats(opened,got)
end
task.wait(0.12)
end
hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if originCF and hrp then hrp.CFrame=originCF end
local rate=opened>0 and(got/opened*100)or 0
notify(
string.format("%s\n已开 %d 个箱子\n已出 %d 个鸭子（概率 %.2f%%）",
giftStop and"已停止"or"结束",opened,got,rate),
"info"
)
end)
if not okRun then
warn("[YQX] gift duck",errRun)
notify("普通鸭流程出错","error")
end
giftDuckBtn.Text="自动购买普通鸭"
giftRunning=false
giftStop=false
end)
end)
end

local autoEternalBtn=createOtherBtn(
"AutoEternal",
"自动合成永恒剑",
Color3.fromRGB(220,200,255),
Color3.fromRGB(100,70,140)
)
local function findOwnedItemAny(names)
for _,name in ipairs(names)do
local item=findOwnedItem(name)
if item then
return item
end
end
return nil
end
autoEternalBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
task.spawn(function()
local ok,err=pcall(function()
autoEternalBtn.Text="检查材料..."
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
local character=speaker.Character
local hrp=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then
autoEternalBtn.Text="角色不存在"
task.wait(1)
autoEternalBtn.Text="自动合成永恒剑"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
local originalCF=hrp.CFrame
local originalPos=hrp.Position
local trident=findOwnedItemAny({
"DemonTrident"
})
local vengeance=findOwnedItemAny({
"Vengeance",
"VengeanceSword"
})
local duckEvil=findOwnedItemAny({
"DuckEvil"
})
if not trident or not vengeance or not duckEvil then
local missing={}
if not trident then
table.insert(missing,"三叉戟")
end
if not vengeance then
table.insert(missing,"复仇剑")
end
if not duckEvil then
table.insert(missing,"恶魔鸭")
end
autoEternalBtn.Text="缺少："..table.concat(missing,"、")
task.wait(1.5)
autoEternalBtn.Text="自动合成永恒剑"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
local eternalStation=Vector3.new(-373.8,12.0,-1340.5)
local tridentPos=Vector3.new(-360.293,11.539,-1333.393)
local vengeancePos=Vector3.new(-371.751,11.939,-1330.322)
local duckEvilPos=Vector3.new(-383.2,13.2,-1327.8)
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(eternalStation)
task.wait(0.5)
autoEternalBtn.Text="放置三叉戟..."
if not teleportOneItem(trident,tridentPos)then
hrp.CFrame=originalCF
autoEternalBtn.Text="三叉戟传送失败"
task.wait(1.2)
autoEternalBtn.Text="自动合成永恒剑"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
task.wait(0.5)
autoEternalBtn.Text="放置复仇剑..."
if not teleportOneItem(vengeance,vengeancePos)then
hrp.CFrame=originalCF
autoEternalBtn.Text="复仇剑传送失败"
task.wait(1.2)
autoEternalBtn.Text="自动合成永恒剑"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
task.wait(0.5)
autoEternalBtn.Text="放置恶魔鸭..."
if not teleportOneItem(duckEvil,duckEvilPos)then
hrp.CFrame=originalCF
autoEternalBtn.Text="恶魔鸭传送失败"
task.wait(1.2)
autoEternalBtn.Text="自动合成永恒剑"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
return
end
task.wait(0.8)
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(eternalStation)
task.wait(1)
autoEternalBtn.Text="等待合成..."
local eternalSword=nil
for attempt=1,20 do
eternalSword=findUnownedItem("EternalSword")
if not eternalSword then
eternalSword=findUnownedItem("Eternal")
end
if eternalSword then break end
task.wait(0.25)
end
if eternalSword then
autoEternalBtn.Text="获取永恒剑..."
task.wait(0.3)
teleportOneItem(eternalSword,originalPos)
task.wait(0.3)
hrp.CFrame=originalCF
autoEternalBtn.Text="合成成功！"
notify("永恒剑合成成功","success")
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
task.wait(1.5)
else
hrp.CFrame=originalCF
autoEternalBtn.Text="已放置材料"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
task.wait(1.5)
end
autoEternalBtn.Text="自动合成永恒剑"
autoEternalBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
end)
if not ok then
warn("[YQX] autoEternalBtn",err)
pcall(function()notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error")end)
end
end)
end)

local autoStarLampBtn=setmetatable({},{__newindex=function(_,k,v)if k=="Text"and YQXLampStatus then pcall(function()YQXLampStatus.Text="星星灯: "..tostring(v)end)end end})
local starLampBusy=false
local starLampBulbCF=CFrame.new(-1718.60461,71.4643402,-2887.04053,0.341943681,0,-0.939720511,0,1,0,0.939720511,0,0.341943681)
local starLampWorkLightCF=CFrame.new(-1707.89514,71.4643402,-2910.50879,-0.86605227,0,-0.499954134,0,1,0,0.499954134,0,-0.86605227)
local function starLampNameMatch(name,aliases)
local low=string.lower(tostring(name or""))
for _,aliasName in ipairs(aliases)do
local a=string.lower(aliasName)
if low==a then return true end
end
return false
end
local function findStarLampMaterial(aliases)
local character=speaker.Character
local backpack=speaker:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
for _,container in ipairs({character,backpack,Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")})do
if container then
for _,obj in ipairs(container:GetDescendants())do
if (obj:IsA("Model")or obj:IsA("BasePart"))and starLampNameMatch(obj.Name,aliases)then
local candidate=obj
if candidate:IsA("BasePart")and candidate.Parent and candidate.Parent:IsA("Model")then candidate=candidate.Parent end
if isOwnedByMe(candidate)or candidate.Parent==character or candidate.Parent==backpack then return candidate end
end
end
end
end
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and starLampNameMatch(obj.Name,aliases)and isOwnedByMe(obj)then return obj end
end
return nil
end
local function findStarLampShopProduct(aliases)
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
if not stores then return nil,nil end
for _,store in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local items=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if items then
for _,obj in ipairs(items:GetDescendants())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and starLampNameMatch(obj.Name,aliases)and isUnowned(obj)then
return obj,store.Name
end
end
end
end
return nil,nil
end
local function getOrBuyStarLampMaterial(aliases,label)
local owned=findStarLampMaterial(aliases)
if owned then return owned end
local confirmPurchase=_G.YQXSilentConfirm
if type(confirmPurchase)~="function"and type(silentConfirmPurchase)=="function"then confirmPurchase=silentConfirmPurchase end
if type(confirmPurchase)~="function"then
notify("购买确认函数未初始化: "..tostring(label),"error",3)
return nil
end
-- 确认购买放进子线程并加超时，避免某次远程调用不返回导致整个流程卡死
local function confirmWithTimeout(storeName,timeout)
local moneyBefore=getMoney()
local done,okCall,bought=false,false,false
task.spawn(function()
okCall,bought=pcall(confirmPurchase,storeName,moneyBefore)
done=true
end)
local t0=os.clock()
while not done and os.clock()-t0<(timeout or 15)do
task.wait(0.1)
end
if done and okCall and bought then return true end
local m=getMoney()
return type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(m)=="number"and m<moneyBefore-0.5
end
local function resetChat(storeName)
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
if type(resetChatState)=="function"then resetChatState(storeName)end
end)
end)
end
for attempt=1,3 do
local product,storeName=findStarLampShopProduct(aliases)
if not product then
local again=findStarLampMaterial(aliases)
if again then return again end
if attempt<3 then
task.wait(0.6)
else
notify("商店找不到"..tostring(label),"warn",3)
end
else
storeName=storeName or"WoodRUs"
notify("购买 "..tostring(label).." @"..tostring(storeName),"info",2)
local counter=getStoreCounter(storeName)
if not counter or not teleportOneItem(product,counter)then
notify(tostring(label).."无法放到柜台","warn",3)
task.wait(0.4)
else
task.wait(0.15)
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
YQXLockAtCounter(storeName)
resetChat(storeName)
task.wait(0.2)
local bought=confirmWithTimeout(storeName,15)
if not bought and product.Parent and isOwnedByMe(product)then bought=true end
if bought then
local deadline=os.clock()+5
while os.clock()<deadline do
if product.Parent and isOwnedByMe(product)then return product end
local item=findStarLampMaterial(aliases)
if item then return item end
task.wait(0.15)
end
notify(tostring(label).."购买后未找到","warn",3)
return nil
end
notify("购买失败，重试("..attempt.."/3): "..tostring(label),"warn",2)
resetChat(storeName)
task.wait(0.5)
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function findNewStarLamp(before)
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and not before[obj]and isUnowned(obj)and obj.Name=="StarLantern"then return obj end
end
return nil
end
function YQXRunStarLamp()
if starLampBusy then return false end
starLampBusy=true
local lampSuccess=false
do
local homeCF=nil
local ok,err=pcall(function()
autoStarLampBtn.Text="检查星星灯材料..."
autoStarLampBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
local character=speaker.Character
local hrp=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then notify("角色不存在","warn",3)return end
local originalCF=hrp.CFrame
homeCF=originalCF
local originalPos=hrp.Position
local bulb=getOrBuyStarLampMaterial({"LightBulb"},"灯泡")
if not bulb then return end
local workLight=getOrBuyStarLampMaterial({"WorkLight"},"工作灯")
if not workLight then return end
-- 开始前记录已有的星星灯（开盒后材料落到坐标就会自动合成，必须先记录再开盒）
local lampsBefore={}
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="StarLantern"then lampsBefore[obj]=true end
end
local function findLamp(full)
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
for _,root in ipairs({pm,Workspace})do
if root then
for _,obj in ipairs(root:GetChildren())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="StarLantern"and not lampsBefore[obj]and(isUnowned(obj)or isOwnedByMe(obj))then return obj end
end
end
end
if full then
for _,obj in ipairs(YQXItemScan())do
if obj:IsA("Model")and obj.Name=="StarLantern"and not lampsBefore[obj]and(isUnowned(obj)or isOwnedByMe(obj))then return obj end
end
end
return nil
end
local function waitLamp(sec)
local deadline=os.clock()+sec
repeat
local l=findLamp(false)
if l then return l end
task.wait(0.2)
until os.clock()>=deadline
return findLamp(true)
end
local function holdAbove(pos)
lockPlayerAt(CFrame.new(pos+Vector3.new(0,8,0)))
end
_G.YQXSetNoclip(true)
-- 材料盒子先传送到各自的合成坐标，传完才发包开盒
local function moveBox(box,cf,label)
if not box or not box.Parent then return false end
autoStarLampBtn.Text="传送"..label.."盒子到坐标..."
teleportOneItem(box,cf)
task.wait(0.4)
return true
end
if not moveBox(bulb,starLampBulbCF,"灯泡")or not moveBox(workLight,starLampWorkLightCF,"工作灯")then
notify("盒子传送失败，已取消","warn",3)
return
end
-- 打开灯泡盒子（人物悬停在盒子上方，不碰材料）
autoStarLampBtn.Text="打开灯泡盒子..."
holdAbove(starLampBulbCF.Position)
task.wait(0.3)
openItem(bulb)
task.wait(0.4)
local bulbItem=nil
local bulbDeadline=os.clock()+5
while os.clock()<bulbDeadline do
bulbItem=findStarLampMaterial({"LightBulb"})
if bulbItem and bulbItem~=bulb then break end
if findLamp(false)then break end
task.wait(0.15)
end
if not bulbItem then bulbItem=bulb end
-- 打开工作灯盒子
autoStarLampBtn.Text="打开工作灯盒子..."
holdAbove(starLampWorkLightCF.Position)
task.wait(0.3)
openItem(workLight)
task.wait(0.4)
local wlItem=nil
local wlDeadline=os.clock()+5
while os.clock()<wlDeadline do
wlItem=findStarLampMaterial({"WorkLight"})
if wlItem and wlItem~=workLight then break end
if findLamp(false)then break end
task.wait(0.15)
end
if not wlItem then wlItem=workLight end
local stationPos=(starLampBulbCF.Position+starLampWorkLightCF.Position)/2
holdAbove(stationPos)
autoStarLampBtn.Text="等待StarLantern..."
local product=waitLamp(3)
if not product then
for attempt=1,3 do
if bulbItem and bulbItem.Parent then
autoStarLampBtn.Text="放置灯泡（第"..attempt.."轮）..."
teleportOneItem(bulbItem,starLampBulbCF)
task.wait(0.35)
end
product=findLamp(false)
if product then break end
if wlItem and wlItem.Parent then
autoStarLampBtn.Text="放置工作灯（第"..attempt.."轮）..."
teleportOneItem(wlItem,starLampWorkLightCF)
task.wait(0.35)
end
holdAbove(stationPos)
autoStarLampBtn.Text="等待StarLantern（第"..attempt.."轮）..."
product=waitLamp(4)
if product then break end
if attempt<3 then
notify("未检测到StarLantern，重试第"..(attempt+1).."轮","warn",3)
end
end
end
unlockPlayer()
if product then
autoStarLampBtn.Text="带回星星灯..."
local dropPos=originalPos+originalCF.LookVector*5
local sent=false
for _=1,2 do
if product.Parent then
sent=teleportOneItem(product,dropPos)or sent
task.wait(0.4)
end
end
if sent then
lampSuccess=true
notify("星星灯合成成功并已带回","success",3)
else
notify("星星灯已生成，但带回失败","warn",3)
end
else
notify("未检测到StarLantern，材料已放置","warn",3)
end
if speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")then
speaker.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame=originalCF
end
end)
pcall(unlockPlayer)
_G.YQXSetNoclip(false)
if homeCF then
local hh=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hh then pcall(function()hh.CFrame=homeCF end)end
end
if not ok then
warn("[YQX] autoStarLampBtn",err)
pcall(function()notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error")end)
end
autoStarLampBtn.Text="自动合成星星灯"
autoStarLampBtn.BackgroundColor3=library.currentColors.zy
starLampBusy=false
end
return lampSuccess
end

local function pinkCarFindSpots()
local spots={}
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if not pm then return spots end
for _,obj in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
if obj:FindFirstChild("ButtonRemote_SpawnButton")then
local ownerVal=obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
if ownerVal and ownerVal:IsA("ObjectValue")then
if ownerVal.Value==speaker then
table.insert(spots,obj)
end
else
table.insert(spots,obj)
end
end
end
return spots
end
local function pinkCarWaitForNewCar(timeout)
local pm=Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlayerModels")
if not pm then return nil end
local snapshot={}
for _,obj in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
local pp=obj:FindFirstChild("PaintParts")
if pp then
for _,part in ipairs(pp:GetChildren())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
snapshot[obj]=part.BrickColor.Name
break
end
end
end
end
local t=0
while t<timeout do
task.wait(0.2)
t=t+0.2
for _,obj in ipairs(pm:GetChildren())do
local pp=obj:FindFirstChild("PaintParts")
if pp then
for _,part in ipairs(pp:GetChildren())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
local oldColor=snapshot[obj]
if oldColor==nil or oldColor~=part.BrickColor.Name then
return obj
end
break
end
end
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function pinkCarIsPink(vehicle)
local paintParts=vehicle:FindFirstChild("PaintParts")
if paintParts then
for _,part in ipairs(paintParts:GetChildren())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
if part.BrickColor.Name=="Hot pink"then
return true
end
end
end
end
local settings=vehicle:FindFirstChild("Settings")
if settings then
local colorVal=settings:FindFirstChild("Color")
if colorVal and colorVal.Value==1032 then
return true
end
end
return false
end
local function pinkCarGetColorName(vehicle)
local paintParts=vehicle:FindFirstChild("PaintParts")
if paintParts then
for _,part in ipairs(paintParts:GetChildren())do
if part:IsA("BasePart")then
return part.BrickColor.Name
end
end
end
local settings=vehicle:FindFirstChild("Settings")
if settings then
local colorVal=settings:FindFirstChild("Color")
if colorVal then
return"Color:"..tostring(colorVal.Value)
end
end
return"Unknown"
end

local function pinkClearSpotButtons()
for _,entry in ipairs(pinkCarSpotButtons)do
if entry.button and entry.button.Parent then
entry.button:Destroy()
end
end
pinkCarSpotButtons={}
end
local function pinkHighlightSpotButton(btn)
for _,entry in ipairs(pinkCarSpotButtons)do
entry.button.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,250)
entry.button.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(50,50,58)
end
btn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(30,30,36)
btn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
end
local function pinkRenderSpots()
pinkClearSpotButtons()
local spots=pinkCarFindSpots()
if#spots==0 then
local empty=Instance.new("TextLabel")
empty.Parent=pinkSpotList
empty.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,px(14))
empty.BackgroundTransparency=1
empty.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
empty.TextSize=px(9)
empty.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(80,80,88)
empty.Text="未找到车位，点刷新重试"
return
end
for i,spot in ipairs(spots)do
local btn=Instance.new("TextButton")
btn.Parent=pinkSpotList
btn.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,px(18))
btn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,250)
btn.BorderSizePixel=0
btn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamMedium
btn.TextSize=px(9)
btn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(50,50,58)
btn.Text=spot.Name
btn.AutoButtonColor=false
Instance.new("UICorner",btn).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,px(3))
do local s=Instance.new("UIStroke")s.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,220,228)s.Thickness=0.5 s.Parent=btn end
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
pinkCarSelectedSpot=spot
pinkHighlightSpotButton(btn)
end)
table.insert(pinkCarSpotButtons,{button=btn,spot=spot})
end
end

pinkRenderSpots()

local function pinkUpdateStatus(colorText,countText,runText,statusColor)
pinkColorRow.Text="当前颜色: "..colorText
pinkCountRow.Text="已刷次数: "..tostring(countText)
pinkRunRow.Text="运行: "..runText
if statusColor then
pinkStatusCard.BackgroundColor3=statusColor
else
pinkStatusCard.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
end
end
local function pinkRunFarmLoop()
if not pinkCarSelectedSpot then
pinkUpdateStatus("—",pinkCarAttemptCount,"请先选择车位!",Color3.fromRGB(255,230,230))
return
end
pinkCarRunning=true
pinkCarAttemptCount=0
pinkUpdateStatus("—",pinkCarAttemptCount,"运行中...",Color3.fromRGB(220,235,255))
while pinkCarRunning do
local spot=pinkCarSelectedSpot
if not spot or not spot.Parent then
pinkUpdateStatus("—",pinkCarAttemptCount,"车位已消失，停止",Color3.fromRGB(255,230,230))
pinkCarRunning=false
break
end
local fireBtn=spot:FindFirstChild("ButtonRemote_SpawnButton")
if not fireBtn then
pinkUpdateStatus("—",pinkCarAttemptCount,"车位按钮丢失!",Color3.fromRGB(255,230,230))
pinkCarRunning=false
break
end
pcall(function()
ReplicatedStorage.Interaction.RemoteProxy:FireServer(fireBtn)
end)
local newCar=pinkCarWaitForNewCar(15)
if not newCar then
pinkUpdateStatus("超时",pinkCarAttemptCount,"等待超时，重试...",Color3.fromRGB(220,235,255))
task.wait(0.5)
else
local colorName=pinkCarGetColorName(newCar)
pinkCarAttemptCount=pinkCarAttemptCount+1
if pinkCarIsPink(newCar)then
pinkUpdateStatus("Hot pink ★",pinkCarAttemptCount,"Found! Hot pink car spawned!",Color3.fromRGB(220,245,230))
pinkCarRunning=false
break
else
pinkUpdateStatus(colorName,pinkCarAttemptCount,"运行中...",Color3.fromRGB(220,235,255))
task.wait(0.5)
end
end
end
if not pinkCarRunning then
if not pinkColorRow.Text:find("Hot pink")then
pinkUpdateStatus(pinkColorRow.Text:gsub("^当前颜色: ",""),pinkCarAttemptCount,"已停止",Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255))
end
end
end
pinkStartBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if pinkCarRunning then return end
task.spawn(function()
local ok,err=pcall(function()
pinkRunFarmLoop()
end)
if not ok then
warn("[YQX] pinkStartBtn",err)
pcall(function()notify("功能错误，报错已上传服务器","error")end)
end
end)
end)
pinkStopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
pinkCarRunning=false
pinkUpdateStatus(pinkColorRow.Text:gsub("^当前颜色: ",""),pinkCarAttemptCount,"已停止",Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255))
end)
pinkUpdateStatus("—",0,"待机",Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255))
end
otherPage.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,currentY+px(20))
end
do
local buildTab=win:Tab("建造",ASSETS.tab.build)
do
local bp_ok,bp_err=pcall(function()
local bp_allBlueprints={}
local bp_ownedSet={}
local bp_selected=nil
local bp_stopBuy=false
local bp_dropdown_funcs=nil
local bp_listSec=buildTab:section("蓝图列表",true)
local bp_statusLbl=bp_listSec:Label("正在加载蓝图...")
bp_listSec:Textbox("搜索蓝图名称","bp_search","",function(text)
pcall(function()
local lower=string.lower(text)
local visible={}
for _,bp in ipairs(bp_allBlueprints)do
if text==""or string.find(string.lower(bp.name),lower,1,true)then
table.insert(visible,(bp.owned and"✓ "or"○ ")..bp.name)
end
end
if bp_dropdown_funcs and bp_dropdown_funcs.SetOptions then
bp_dropdown_funcs:SetOptions(visible)
end
bp_statusLbl.Text=string.format("匹配 %d / %d 个蓝图",#visible,#bp_allBlueprints)
end)
end)
bp_listSec:Button("刷新蓝图列表",function()
pcall(function()
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
bp_ownedSet={}
local pb=player:FindFirstChild("PlayerBlueprints")
if pb then pb=pb:FindFirstChild("Blueprints")end
if pb then
for _,child in ipairs(pb:GetChildren())do
bp_ownedSet[child.Name]=true
end
end
bp_allBlueprints={}
local allBpRemote=RS:FindFirstChild("Interaction")and RS.Interaction:FindFirstChild("AllBlueprints")
local remoteOk=false
if allBpRemote then
local okRes,result=pcall(function()return allBpRemote:InvokeServer()end)
if okRes and type(result)=="table"then
for _,item in ipairs(result)do
local itemName=item
if type(item)=="table"then
itemName=item.Name or item.InstanceName or tostring(item)
end
table.insert(bp_allBlueprints,{
name=tostring(itemName),
owned=bp_ownedSet[itemName]~=nil
})
end
remoteOk=true
end
end
if not remoteOk or#bp_allBlueprints==0 then
bp_allBlueprints={}
for _,name in ipairs(ALL_BLUEPRINTS)do
table.insert(bp_allBlueprints,{
name=name,
owned=bp_ownedSet[name]~=nil
})
end
end
