local bp_buySec=buildTab:section("批量购买",true)
bp_buySec:Button("一键购买所有蓝图",function()
task.spawn(function()
local okBuy,errBuy=pcall(function()
bp_stopBuy=false
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local hrp=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local originCF=hrp and hrp.CFrame
local Blueprints=player:FindFirstChild("PlayerBlueprints")
if Blueprints then Blueprints=Blueprints:FindFirstChild("Blueprints")end
local stores=workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
if not stores then
notify("未找到 Stores","warn")
return
end
local missing={}
local foundSet={}
for _,store in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local shopItems=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if shopItems then
for _,item in ipairs(shopItems:GetChildren())do
local typeVal=item:FindFirstChild("Type")
local itemType=typeVal and typeVal.Value or""
if itemType=="Blueprint"then
local bpName=item.Name
if not foundSet[bpName]then
foundSet[bpName]=true
if not(Blueprints and Blueprints:FindFirstChild(bpName))then
table.insert(missing,bpName)
end
end
end
end
end
end
table.sort(missing)
if#missing==0 then
notify("已拥有全部蓝图，或没找到可购买蓝图","info")
return
end
notify(string.format("还缺 %d 种蓝图，开始购买",#missing),"info")
local okCount=0
for _,bpName in ipairs(missing)do
if bp_stopBuy then break end
local model=nil
for _,store in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local shopItems=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
if shopItems then
for _,item in ipairs(shopItems:GetChildren())do
if item.Name==bpName and isUnowned(item)then
model=item
break
end
end
end
if model then break end
end
if not model then
notify("缺货: "..bpName,"warn")
else
local storeName="WoodRUs"
local counter=getStoreCounter(storeName)
if counter then
teleportOneItem(model,counter)
hrp=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
pcall(function()
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(counter+Vector3.new(0,4,0))
pcall(function()hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero end)
end)
end
if not npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then
local tCache=tick()
while tick()-tCache<3 do
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]then break end
pcall(function()
local ctx=getThomContext(storeName)
if npcIdConfirmed[storeName]and ctx and type(ctx.ID)=="number"then
npcIdCache[storeName]=ctx.ID
end
end)
task.wait(0.15)
end
end
local moneyBefore=getMoney()
local bought=select(1,silentConfirmPurchase(storeName,moneyBefore))
local moneyAfter=getMoney()
if type(moneyBefore)=="number"and type(moneyAfter)=="number"and moneyAfter<moneyBefore then
bought=true
end
if bought then
local waitT=0
while waitT<5 do
if Blueprints and Blueprints:FindFirstChild(bpName)then break end
task.wait(0.3)
waitT=waitT+0.3
if bp_stopBuy then break end
end
if Blueprints and Blueprints:FindFirstChild(bpName)then
okCount=okCount+1
notify("已得到蓝图: "..bpName,"success")
else
notify("购买后未检测到: "..bpName,"warn")
end
else
notify("购买失败: "..bpName,"warn")
end
else
notify("找不到柜台: "..storeName,"warn")
end
end
task.wait(0.2)
end
if originCF and hrp then
pcall(function()hrp.CFrame=originCF end)
end
notify(string.format("购买完成，本次新增 %d / %d 种蓝图",okCount,#missing),"success")
end)
if not okBuy then
notify("蓝图购买出错: "..tostring(errBuy),"error")
end
end)
end)
bp_buySec:Button("停止购买",function()
bp_stopBuy=true
notify("已请求停止购买","warn")
end)
bp_buySec:Button("打开蓝图盒（鼠标指向）",function()
local okOpen,errOpen=pcall(function()
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse=player:GetMouse()
local target=mouse.Target
if not target then

local model=target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

end

if not okOpen then

pcall(function()
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer

if pb then pb=pb:FindFirstChild("Blueprints")end
bp_ownedSet={}

})

end
end
local opts={}

if bp_dropdown_funcs and bp_dropdown_funcs.SetOptions then
bp_dropdown_funcs:SetOptions(opts)

local ownedCount=0
for _ in pairs(bp_ownedSet)do ownedCount=ownedCount+1 end
bp_statusLbl.Text=string.format("共 %d 个蓝图（已拥有 %d）",#bp_allBlueprints,ownedCount)
-- ==================== 网格吸附 / 90° 对齐 / 镜像放置 ====================
local snapSec=buildTab:section("吸附与对齐",true)
local gridSize=2
local snapOn=true
local alignOn=true
local mirrorOn=false
local manualYaw=0
local function placeBlueprintAt(bpName,pos,yawDeg)
if not bpName or bpName==""then return false,"没选蓝图" end
local rs=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ps=rs:FindFirstChild("PlaceStructure")
if not ps then return false,"找不到 PlaceStructure" end
local remote=ps:FindFirstChild("ClientPlacedBlueprint")
if not remote then return false,"找不到放置远程" end
local finalPos=pos
if mirrorOn then
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local char=player.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp then
local origin=hrp.Position
local look=hrp.CFrame.LookVector
local flat=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)
if flat.Magnitude<0.01 then flat=Vector3.new(0,0,1)end
flat=flat.Unit
local right=Vector3.new(-flat.Z,0,flat.X)
local d=finalPos-origin
local along=d.X*flat.X+d.Z*flat.Z
local side=d.X*right.X+d.Z*right.Z
finalPos=origin+flat*along+right*(-side)+Vector3.new(0,d.Y,0)
end
end
if snapOn then
local g=(gridSize and gridSize>0)and gridSize or 1
finalPos=Vector3.new(math.floor(finalPos.X/g+0.5)*g,math.floor(finalPos.Y/g+0.5)*g,math.floor(finalPos.Z/g+0.5)*g)
end
local yaw=yawDeg or 0
if mirrorOn then yaw=-yaw end
if alignOn then yaw=math.floor(yaw/90+0.5)*90 end
local r=math.rad(yaw)
local c,s=math.cos(r),math.sin(r)
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local ok,err=pcall(function()
remote:FireServer(
bpName,finalPos.X,finalPos.Y,finalPos.Z,
c,0,-s,
0,1,0,
s,0,c,
player
)
end)
if not ok then return false,tostring(err)end
return true,finalPos
end
local function playerYaw()
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local char=player.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return 0 end
local look=hrp.CFrame.LookVector
if math.abs(look.X)<0.001 and math.abs(look.Z)<0.001 then return 0 end
return math.deg(math.atan2(look.X,look.Z))
end
snapSec:Toggle("网格吸附","yqxGridSnap",true,function(on)snapOn=on end)
snapSec:Slider("网格大小（stud）","yqxGridSize",2,1,24,false,function(v)
gridSize=tonumber(v)or 2
end)
snapSec:Toggle("90° 对齐（朝向贴格子轴）","yqxAlign90",true,function(on)alignOn=on end)
snapSec:Slider("额外旋转（0-270°）","yqxYawAdd",0,0,270,false,function(v)
manualYaw=tonumber(v)or 0
end)
snapSec:Toggle("镜像放置（以你为中轴左右对称）","yqxMirror",false,function(on)
mirrorOn=on
notify(on and"镜像放置已开：落点会翻到左右对称那一侧"or"镜像放置已关","info",3)
end)
snapSec:Button("按规则放到鼠标位置",function()
if not bp_selected then notify("先在上面选蓝图","warn",3)return end
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse=player:GetMouse()
local hit=mouse and mouse.Hit
if not hit then notify("鼠标没指到东西","warn",3)return end
local ok,res=placeBlueprintAt(bp_selected,hit.Position,playerYaw()+manualYaw)
if ok then
notify(string.format("已放置 %s @ %.1f, %.1f, %.1f",bp_selected,res.X,res.Y,res.Z),"success",3)
else
notify("放置失败: "..tostring(res),"error",4)
end
end)
snapSec:Button("按规则放到自己位置",function()
if not bp_selected then notify("先在上面选蓝图","warn",3)return end
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local char=player.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then notify("角色未加载","warn",3)return end
local pos=hrp.Position+hrp.CFrame.LookVector*4
local ok,res=placeBlueprintAt(bp_selected,pos,playerYaw()+manualYaw)
if ok then
notify("已放置 "..bp_selected,"success",3)
else
notify("放置失败: "..tostring(res),"error",4)
end
end)

-- ==================== 多蓝图连续放置队列 ====================
local queueSec=buildTab:section("连续放置队列",true)
local bpQueue={}
local bpQueueRunning=false
local bpQueueStop=false
local bpQueueSpacing=6
local bpQueueInterval=0.35
local bpQueueMode="鼠标点向右手排"
local bpQueueStatus=queueSec:Label("队列为空")
local function bpQueueRefresh()
if #bpQueue==0 then
pcall(function()bpQueueStatus.Text="队列为空" end)
return
end
local s=table.concat(bpQueue," → ")
if #s>62 then s=string.sub(s,1,62).."…" end
pcall(function()bpQueueStatus.Text=string.format("队列 %d 个：%s",#bpQueue,s)end)
end
queueSec:Button("把当前选中蓝图加入队列",function()
if not bp_selected then notify("先在上面选蓝图","warn",3)return end
table.insert(bpQueue,bp_selected)
bpQueueRefresh()
notify("已加入队列: "..bp_selected,"success",2)
end)
queueSec:Button("连加 5 个当前蓝图",function()
if not bp_selected then notify("先在上面选蓝图","warn",3)return end
for _=1,5 do table.insert(bpQueue,bp_selected)end
bpQueueRefresh()
end)
queueSec:Dropdown("排列方式","yqxBpQueueMode",
{"鼠标点向右手排","鼠标点向正前排","玩家脚下向正前排","全部叠在鼠标点"},function(val)
bpQueueMode=val
end)
queueSec:Slider("间距（stud）","yqxBpSpacing",6,0,40,false,function(v)
bpQueueSpacing=tonumber(v)or 6
end)
queueSec:Slider("间隔（×0.1 秒）","yqxBpInterval",4,1,30,false,function(v)
bpQueueInterval=(tonumber(v)or 4)*0.1
end)
queueSec:Button("开始连续放置",function()
if bpQueueRunning then notify("队列正在跑","info",3)return end
if #bpQueue==0 then notify("队列是空的","warn",3)return end
bpQueueRunning=true
bpQueueStop=false
task.spawn(function()
local player=game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse=player:GetMouse()
local char=player.Character
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local anchor=nil
if bpQueueMode=="玩家脚下向正前排"then
anchor=hrp and hrp.Position or nil
else
local hit=mouse and mouse.Hit
if hit then anchor=hit.Position end
end
if not anchor then
notify("拿不到起点（鼠标没指到东西？）","warn",4)
bpQueueRunning=false
return
end
local dir=Vector3.new(1,0,0)
if hrp then
local look=hrp.CFrame.LookVector
local flat=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)
if flat.Magnitude<0.01 then flat=Vector3.new(0,0,1)end
flat=flat.Unit
if bpQueueMode=="鼠标点向右手排"then
dir=Vector3.new(-flat.Z,0,flat.X)
else
dir=flat
end
end
local done=0
for i,bpName in ipairs(bpQueue)do
if bpQueueStop then break end
local pos=anchor
if bpQueueMode~="全部叠在鼠标点"then
pos=anchor+dir*((i-1)*bpQueueSpacing)
end
pcall(function()bpQueueStatus.Text=string.format("放置中 %d/%d：%s",i,#bpQueue,bpName)end)
local ok,res=placeBlueprintAt(bpName,pos,playerYaw()+manualYaw)
if ok then
done=done+1
else
notify("放置失败 "..bpName..": "..tostring(res),"warn",4)
end
task.wait(bpQueueInterval)
end
bpQueueRunning=false
pcall(function()bpQueueStatus.Text=string.format("上次完成 %d/%d",done,#bpQueue)end)
notify(string.format("连续放置完成：%d/%d",done,#bpQueue),"success",4)
end)
end)
queueSec:Button("停止连续放置",function()
if not bpQueueRunning then notify("现在没在跑","info",2)return end
bpQueueStop=true
notify("已请求停止放置","warn",3)
end)
queueSec:Button("清空放置队列",function()
bpQueue={}
bpQueueRefresh()
end)
bpQueueRefresh()

end)
end)
if not bp_ok then
warn("[YQX] 建造Tab init error:",bp_err)
pcall(function()
notify("建造Tab初始化失败: "..tostring(bp_err),"error")
end)
end
end
end

local themeDropdown_funcs=nil

end)
local soundOptions={"曼波(默认)","哇哦(曼波)","Hachimi(哈基米)","Duangg(曼波)","欧耶(曼波)"}

do
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(0.1)
local gui=library.screenGui
if gui then
for _,v in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants())do
if v.Name=="TextboxBack"and v:IsA("TextButton")and v.Text:find("Background AssetID")then
assetTextboxModule=v.Parent
if assetTextboxModule then
assetTextboxModule.Visible=(library.currentTheme=="Custom Image")
end
break
end
end
end
end)
end
if themeDropdown_funcs and config.theme then
themeDropdown_funcs:SetValue(YQXThemeDisplay(config.theme))
end

local dbgSec=settingsTab:section(t("sec_debug"),true)
do
local ok,err=pcall(function()
local dbgLog=""
local remoteOn=false
local clickOn=false
local guiOn=false
local remoteHooked=false
local maxLog=60000
local function dbgAppend(line)
dbgLog=dbgLog..line.."\n"
if#dbgLog>maxLog then dbgLog=dbgLog:sub(-math.floor(maxLog*0.7))end
end
local function tryCopy(text)
local okc=false
pcall(function()if setclipboard then setclipboard(text)okc=true end end)
pcall(function()if toclipboard then toclipboard(text)okc=true end end)
if okc then
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_copied_prefix")..#text..t("lbl_chars_suffix")
pcall(function()notify(t("n_debug_copied"),"success")end)
else
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_no_clipboard_console")
end
end
local function pathOf(inst)
local t={}
local c=inst
local n=0
while c and c~=game and n<30 do
table.insert(t,1,c.Name)
c=c.Parent
n=n+1
end
return table.concat(t,".")
end
local function shortArgs(...)
local a={...}
local parts={}
for i=1,math.min(#a,8)do
local v=a[i]
local s
if typeof(v)=="Instance"then
s="I:"..v:GetFullName()
elseif type(v)=="string"then
s=string.format("%q",v:sub(1,80))
elseif type(v)=="table"then
s="{table}"
else
s=tostring(v)
end
table.insert(parts,s)
end
if#a>8 then table.insert(parts,"...")end
return table.concat(parts,", ")
end
local function hookRemotes()
if remoteHooked then return true end
local okHook=false
pcall(function()
local mt=getrawmetatable(game)
if not mt then return end
local old=mt.__namecall
if not old then return end
if setreadonly then setreadonly(mt,false)end
local wrapper=function(self,...)
local method=getnamecallmethod()
if remoteOn and(method=="FireServer"or method=="InvokeServer")then
local name=""
pcall(function()name=self:GetFullName()end)
local line=string.format("[%s] %s\n  args: %s",method,name,shortArgs(...))
dbgAppend(line)
end
return old(self,...)
end
if newcclosure then
mt.__namecall=newcclosure(wrapper)
else
mt.__namecall=wrapper
end
if setreadonly then setreadonly(mt,true)end
okHook=true
remoteHooked=true
end)
pcall(function()
local npc=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("NPCDialog")
if npc then
dbgAppend("[info] Found NPCDialog")
for _,c in ipairs(npc:GetChildren())do
dbgAppend("  "..c.ClassName.." "..c.Name)
end
end
local inter=ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Interaction")
if inter then
dbgAppend("[info] Found Interaction children: "..#inter:GetChildren())
end
end)
return okHook
end
local statusLbl=dbgSec:Label(t("lbl_ready"))
dbgSec:Toggle(t("tog_remote_mon"),"remoteMonitor",false,function(on)
remoteOn=on
if on then
local okh=hookRemotes()
statusLbl.Text=okh and t("lbl_remote_on")or t("lbl_hook_failed")
dbgAppend("===== Remote ON =====")
pcall(function()notify(t("n_remote_on"),"success")end)
else
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_remote_off")
dbgAppend("===== Remote OFF =====")
end
end)
dbgSec:Button(t("btn_cd_scan"),function()
dbgAppend("===== ClickDetector Scan =====")
local n=0
local hrp=speaker.Character and speaker.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local origin=hrp and hrp.Position
for _,d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("ClickDetector")then
local parent=d.Parent
local pos=parent and parent:IsA("BasePart")and parent.Position
local dist=(origin and pos)and(pos-origin).Magnitude or-1
if dist<0 or dist<120 then
dbgAppend(string.format("CD %.0f %s",dist,pathOf(d)))
n=n+1
if n>80 then break end
end
end
end
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_clickdet_prefix")..n..t("lbl_entries_suffix")
dbgAppend("Total "..n)
pcall(function()notify(t("n_cd_scan_done"),"info")end)
end)
dbgSec:Button(t("btn_scan_chatgui"),function()
dbgAppend("===== ChatGUI =====")
local cg=PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChatGUI")
if not cg then
dbgAppend("No ChatGUI")
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_no_chatgui")
return
end
dbgAppend("ChatGUI Enabled="..tostring(cg.Enabled))
for _,d in ipairs(cg:GetDescendants())do
if d:IsA("TextButton")or d:IsA("TextLabel")then
local t=tostring(d.Text or"")
if t~=""or d.Name:find("Prompt")or d.Name:find("Choice")or d.Name:find("Chat")then
dbgAppend(string.format("%s %s Text=%q",d.ClassName,pathOf(d),t:sub(1,60)))
end
end
end
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_chatgui_logged")
pcall(function()notify(t("n_chatgui_scanned"),"info")end)
end)
dbgSec:Button(t("btn_scan_stores"),function()
dbgAppend("===== Stores =====")
local stores=Workspace:FindFirstChild("Stores")
if not stores then
dbgAppend("No Workspace.Stores")
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_no_stores")
return
end
for _,store in ipairs(stores:GetChildren())do
local items=store:FindFirstChild("ShopItems")
local count=items and#items:GetChildren()or 0
dbgAppend(string.format("Shop %s ShopItems=%d",store.Name,count))
if items then
local i=0
for _,it in ipairs(items:GetChildren())do
i=i+1
if i<=15 then
dbgAppend("  - "..it.Name.." ("..it.ClassName..")")
end
end
if count>15 then dbgAppend("  ...")end
end
end
statusLbl.Text=t("n_stores_scanned")
pcall(function()notify(t("n_stores_scanned"),"info")end)
end)
dbgSec:Button(t("btn_copy_log"),function()
tryCopy(dbgLog)
end)
dbgSec:Button(t("btn_clear_log"),function()
dbgLog=""
statusLbl.Text=t("lbl_log_cleared")
end)
end)
if not ok then warn("[YQX] debug page failed",err)end
end
-- ==================== 配置导入导出 ====================
local cfgSec=settingsTab:section("配置导入导出",true)
local CFG_TAG="YQXCFG1:"
local B64CHARS="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local cfgStatus=cfgSec:Label("配置含主题/文字颜色/背景/点击音效等")
local cfgImportText=""
local cfgImportBox=nil
local function b64encode(str)
local out={}
local n=#str
local i=1
while i<=n do
local a=string.byte(str,i)or 0
local b=string.byte(str,i+1)
local c=string.byte(str,i+2)
local b1=math.floor(a/4)%64
local b2=(a%4)*16+math.floor((b or 0)/16)
local b3=b and((b%16)*4+math.floor((c or 0)/64))or nil
local b4=c and(c%64)or nil
table.insert(out,string.sub(B64CHARS,b1+1,b1+1))
table.insert(out,string.sub(B64CHARS,b2+1,b2+1))
table.insert(out,b3 and string.sub(B64CHARS,b3+1,b3+1)or"=")
table.insert(out,b4 and string.sub(B64CHARS,b4+1,b4+1)or"=")
i=i+3
end
return table.concat(out)
end
local function b64decode(str)
local map={}
for i=1,#B64CHARS do map[string.sub(B64CHARS,i,i)]=i-1 end
local out={}
local buf,bits=0,0
for i=1,#str do
local ch=string.sub(str,i,i)
if ch=="="then break end
local v=map[ch]
if v then
buf=buf*64+v
bits=bits+6
if bits>=8 then
bits=bits-8
local byte=math.floor(buf/2^bits)%256
table.insert(out,string.char(byte))
buf=buf%(2^bits)
end
end
end
return table.concat(out)
end
local function configToString()
local ok,json=pcall(function()return HttpService:JSONEncode({v=1,cfg=config})end)
if not ok or type(json)~="string"then return nil end
return CFG_TAG..b64encode(json)
end
local function copyText(str)
local okc=false
pcall(function()if setclipboard then setclipboard(str)okc=true end end)
if not okc then pcall(function()if toclipboard then toclipboard(str)okc=true end end)end
return okc
end
local function applyConfigString(str)
if type(str)~="string"or str==""then return false,"内容为空" end
local clean=string.gsub(str,"%s","")
local pos=string.find(clean,CFG_TAG,1,true)
if not pos then return false,"格式不对（缺少前缀）" end
local body=string.sub(clean,pos+#CFG_TAG)
local okDec,json=pcall(b64decode,body)
if not okDec or type(json)~="string"or json==""then return false,"解码失败" end
local okJson,tbl=pcall(function()return HttpService:JSONDecode(json)end)
if not okJson or type(tbl)~="table"then return false,"解析失败" end
local src=tbl.cfg or tbl
if type(src)~="table"then return false,"里面没有配置数据" end
local n=0
for k,v in pairs(src)do
if k~="v"then
config[k]=v
n=n+1
end
end
pcall(saveConfig)
pcall(function()
if config.theme and THEMES[config.theme]then library.applyTheme(config.theme)end
end)
pcall(function()
if config.textColor then
local r,g,b=string.match(config.textColor,"^(%d+)%s*,%s*(%d+)%s*,%s*(%d+)$")
if r then
local color=Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(r),tonumber(g),tonumber(b))
local gui=library.screenGui
if gui then
for _,el in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants())do
if(el:IsA("TextLabel")or el:IsA("TextButton")or el:IsA("TextBox"))and el.Name~="ScriptTitle"and el.Name~="Open"then
el.TextColor3=color
end
end
end
if library.currentColors then library.currentColors.text=color end
end
end
end)
pcall(function()
if config.bgTransparency and library.mainFrame and library.currentTheme~="Custom Image"then
library.mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency=config.bgTransparency
end
end)
pcall(function()
clickSoundEnabled=config.clickSound~=false
if config.clickSoundId and config.clickSoundId~=""then currentClickSoundId=tostring(config.clickSoundId)end
end)
return true,n
end
cfgSec:Button("导出配置到剪贴板",function()
local str=configToString()
if not str then
notify("导出失败","error",3)
return
end
local okc=copyText(str)
cfgStatus.Text=string.format("已生成 %d 字符",#str)
if okc then
notify("配置已复制（"..#str.." 字符），存到记事本就能换号用","success",5)
else
notify("剪贴板不可用，已填入下面输入框自己复制","warn",5)
if cfgImportBox then cfgImportBox:SetValue(str)end
cfgImportText=str
end
end)
cfgSec:Button("重置为默认配置",function()
config.theme="Peach Pink"
config.textColor="95,40,45"
config.bgTransparency=0
config.clickSound=true
pcall(saveConfig)
notify("配置已重置，重载脚本后全部生效","success",4)
end)
cfgImportBox=cfgSec:Textbox("粘贴配置字符串后再点导入","yqxCfgImport","",function(text)
cfgImportText=text
end)
cfgSec:Button("从输入框导入配置",function()
local str=(cfgImportText or"")
if str==""then
notify("先在输入框里粘贴配置字符串","warn",3)
return
end
local ok,res=applyConfigString(str)
if ok then
cfgStatus.Text=string.format("已导入 %d 项配置",tonumber(res)or 0)
notify("配置已导入 "..tostring(res).." 项，界面已即时生效","success",5)
else
notify("导入失败: "..tostring(res),"error",5)
end
end)
cfgSec:Label("换号时导出→存文本→新号粘贴导入。剪贴板读不到，所以导入走输入框。")

-- ==================== 日志面板 ====================
local logSec=settingsTab:section("日志面板",true)
local logLines={}
local LOG_MAX=400
local logPanel=nil
local logScroll=nil
local logText=nil
local logRender
local logKindColor={
success=Color3.fromRGB(120,230,160),
error=Color3.fromRGB(255,120,130),
fail=Color3.fromRGB(255,120,130),
warn=Color3.fromRGB(255,190,100),
info=Color3.fromRGB(160,175,255),
server=Color3.fromRGB(130,170,255),
}
local function logAdd(kind,text)
if #logLines>=LOG_MAX then table.remove(logLines,1)end
table.insert(logLines,{k=kind or"info",t=os.date("%H:%M:%S"),m=tostring(text)})
if logText and logPanel and logPanel.Visible then pcall(logRender)end
end
logRender=function()
if not logText then return end
local lines={}
local first=math.max(1,#logLines-220)
for i=first,#logLines do
local l=logLines[i]
local col=logKindColor[l.k]or logKindColor.info
local c=string.format("%d, %d, %d",math.floor(col.R*255+0.5),math.floor(col.G*255+0.5),math.floor(col.B*255+0.5))
table.insert(lines,string.format('<font color="rgb(%s)">[%s] %s</font>',c,l.t,string.gsub(l.m,"[<>&]"," ")))
end
logText.Text=table.concat(lines,"\n")
if logScroll then
pcall(function()
logScroll.CanvasPosition=Vector2.new(0,math.max(0,logText.AbsoluteSize.Y-logScroll.AbsoluteSize.Y+16))
end)
end
end
local function ensureLogPanel()
if logPanel and logPanel.Parent then return end
local gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name="YQXLogPanel"
gui.ResetOnSpawn=false
gui.DisplayOrder=500
gui.Parent=PlayerGui
logPanel=Instance.new("Frame")
logPanel.Name="Frame"
logPanel.Size=UDim2.new(0,430,0,300)
logPanel.Position=UDim2.new(0,60,0,140)
logPanel.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,20,28)
logPanel.BackgroundTransparency=0.05
logPanel.BorderSizePixel=0
logPanel.Visible=false
logPanel.Parent=gui
Instance.new("UICorner",logPanel).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke")
stroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(70,80,120)
stroke.Thickness=1
stroke.Parent=logPanel
local header=Instance.new("TextButton")
header.Name="Header"
header.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,32)
header.BackgroundTransparency=1
header.AutoButtonColor=false
header.Text=""
header.Active=true
header.Parent=logPanel
local title=Instance.new("TextLabel")
title.Parent=header
title.BackgroundTransparency=1
title.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,0)
title.Size=UDim2.new(1,-70,1,0)
title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize=14
title.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
title.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(230,235,255)
title.Text="运行日志"
local closeBtn=Instance.new("TextButton")
closeBtn.Parent=header
closeBtn.Size=UDim2.new(0,28,0,24)
closeBtn.Position=UDim2.new(1,-34,0,4)
closeBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(60,45,55)
closeBtn.BorderSizePixel=0
closeBtn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
closeBtn.TextSize=14
closeBtn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,180,190)
closeBtn.Text="×"
Instance.new("UICorner",closeBtn).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if logPanel then logPanel.Visible=false end
end)
logScroll=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
logScroll.Name="LogScroll"
logScroll.Parent=logPanel
logScroll.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,34)
logScroll.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,1,-72)
logScroll.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,14,20)
logScroll.BackgroundTransparency=0.1
logScroll.BorderSizePixel=0
logScroll.ScrollBarThickness=4
logScroll.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
pcall(function()logScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y end)
Instance.new("UICorner",logScroll).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
logText=Instance.new("TextLabel")
logText.Parent=logScroll
logText.BackgroundTransparency=1
logText.Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0,4)
logText.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,0)
pcall(function()logText.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y end)
logText.Font=Enum.Font.Code
logText.TextSize=12
logText.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
logText.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
logText.RichText=true
logText.TextWrapped=true
logText.Text=""
local footer=Instance.new("Frame")
footer.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,1,-34)
footer.Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,0,26)
footer.BackgroundTransparency=1
footer.Parent=logPanel
local function mkBtn(text,x,w,color,cb)
local b=Instance.new("TextButton")
b.Parent=footer
b.Position=UDim2.new(0,x,0,0)
b.Size=UDim2.new(0,w,0,26)
b.BackgroundColor3=color
b.BorderSizePixel=0
b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
b.TextSize=12
b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(235,240,255)
b.Text=text
Instance.new("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()pcall(cb)end)
return b
end
mkBtn("复制",8,56,Color3.fromRGB(45,55,85),function()
local parts={}
for _,l in ipairs(logLines)do table.insert(parts,string.format("[%s][%s] %s",l.t,l.k,l.m))end
local str=table.concat(parts,"\n")
if copyText(str)then
notify("已复制 "..#str.." 字符日志","success",3)
else
notify("剪贴板不可用","warn",3)
end
end)
mkBtn("清空",70,56,Color3.fromRGB(60,45,55),function()
logLines={}
if logText then logText.Text="" end
end)
mkBtn("关闭",132,56,Color3.fromRGB(40,60,50),function()
if logPanel then logPanel.Visible=false end
end)
local dragging,dragStart,dragOrigin=false,nil,nil
header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging=true
dragStart=input.Position
dragOrigin=logPanel.Position
end
end)
game:GetService("UserInputService").InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if not dragging or not logPanel then return end
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local delta=input.Position-dragStart
logPanel.Position=UDim2.new(dragOrigin.X.Scale,dragOrigin.X.Offset+delta.X,dragOrigin.Y.Scale,dragOrigin.Y.Offset+delta.Y)
end
end)
game:GetService("UserInputService").InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging=false
end
end)
pcall(logRender)
end
logSec:Toggle("显示日志面板","yqxLogPanel",false,function(on)
task.spawn(function()
pcall(ensureLogPanel)
if logPanel then
logPanel.Visible=on
if on then pcall(logRender)end
end
end)
end)
logSec:Button("打开日志面板",function()
task.spawn(function()
pcall(ensureLogPanel)
if logPanel then
logPanel.Visible=true
pcall(logRender)
end
end)
end)
logSec:Button("清空日志",function()
logLines={}
if logText then logText.Text="" end
notify("日志已清空","info",2)
end)
logSec:Label("自动记录脚本弹出的全部提示，面板可拖动，点 × 收起。")
do
local baseNotify=notify
notify=function(text,kind,duration)
pcall(logAdd,kind,text)
return baseNotify(text,kind,duration)
end
end

task.spawn(function()
task.wait(1.5)
YQXLoadUiFlags()
YQXStartUiAutosave()
end)
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(0.3)
if config.theme and THEMES[config.theme]then
library.applyTheme(config.theme)
else
library.applyTheme("Default (Dark Blue)")
end
if themeDropdown_funcs then
themeDropdown_funcs:SetValue(YQXThemeDisplay(library.currentTheme))
end
if config.textColor then
local r,g,b=config.textColor:match("^(%d+)%s*,%s*(%d+)%s*,%s*(%d+)$")
if r and g and b then
r,g,b=tonumber(r),tonumber(g),tonumber(b)
local color=Color3.fromRGB(r,g,b)
local gui=library.screenGui
if gui then
for _,v in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants())do
if(v:IsA("TextLabel")or v:IsA("TextButton")or v:IsA("TextBox"))and v.Name~="ScriptTitle"and v.Name~="Open"then
v.TextColor3=color
end
end
end
library.currentColors.text=color
end
end
if config.bgTransparency and library.mainFrame then
if library.currentTheme~="Custom Image"then
library.mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency=config.bgTransparency
end
end
if bgTransSlider_funcs and config.bgTransparency then
bgTransSlider_funcs:SetValue(config.bgTransparency)
end
clickSoundEnabled=config.clickSound~=false
if library.mainFrame then library.mainFrame.Visible=false end
end)
end
